## anevia

Authored return/rescue predicates: `anevia.trickster.returned`.

- `c9360bc8e195ac14d8157c08994bea5d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dream_End/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "I don't know. I don't!" {n}Arueshalae knocks on the wooden surface.{/n} "It's... just a table. Anevia's fresh bread used to be here, but now it's gone — and yet the table remains."
- `13430e05ffd973e43876cf1ae4f8b30e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Sosiel/Main_C5/KTC_TreverDead/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "Hail, Knight Commander." {n}Though it is rather unusual for her, Anevia stands before you at rigid attention, like a soldier. Her tone is exactingly formal, and only her piercing gaze hints at the roiling emotions she is suppressing.{/n} "Permission to report. Trever Vaenic is dead."
- `eca8e632b9ab7d94489239c4f96f18f0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Sosiel/Main_C5/KTC_TreverDead/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > "He attempted to force his way into your chambers with a weapon, shouting that he would kill you. It seems he wanted to avenge his brother's death." {n}Anevia looks you in the eye.{/n} "We couldn't stop him without bloodshed. Two guards were killed. We had no choice but to cut him down."
- `532cd23a0cb24de991d42ee67290385e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "What news would you rather bring back to Anevia? Will you tell her that her wife is dead, dooming her to heartbreak? Or will you tell her that everything ended well? I do not know what force created me or for what purpose, but perhaps I deserve the right to live? Doesn't a living Irabeth have more to offer this world than a dead one?"
- `88bff7e0dbc2498ea1dbcefe6e56290c` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Answer_0024.jbp`
  > "Are you going to avenge your death?"
- `4b398e2e795d4d86abd5a2845c3471ea` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Answer_14.jbp`
  > "She died a hero. May Iomedae welcome her."
- `daea393026d64c83952ee80542b3a6c6` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Answer_6.jbp`
  > "But I just killed you!"
- `0612767cb68c42398c43c83f9c4d8c56` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah gives Anevia a reassuring nod.{/n} "We don't abandon our own. We'll get them out, won't we, Commander?"
- `e09be6a7ca994edaa026fdd24ccea5e7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > {n}With a dark look, Anevia bites out through clenched teeth,{/n} "She died a hero. Of course she did. You know, you're the one who got her killed. If it wasn't for you, I'd have convinced her to desert ages ago. But all she'd say was, 'I can't leave the Commander.' That's on you."
- `379d5d2dd16144ab83acf92f5a5d0769` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia grimaces.{/n} "I decided that abandoning the crusade now, in the final battle, was a lousy thing to do. When we win, I'll be on my way."
- `04e3ab062ed644868ca7166a2e88d5d0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}The frantic shrieking of the whetstone on the dagger dies away.{/n} "I know the big push is coming, and now's not the right time, but..." {n}Anevia's eyes lock with yours and you can see the panic in them.{/n} "...we gotta save Beth. Before it's too late."
- `50276643939e42edadcc8f28848e5fdf` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > {n}Deities bringing people back from the dead has been known to happen. But Iomedae's decision to resurrect Anevia, who has never worshiped her, seems very odd.{/n}
- `2f412e5860a14c268dfdf5d4572ae654` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia confines herself to a curt nod. The ease with which she has accepted her own death and resurrection seems highly unusual, even for the cool-headed scout.{/n}
- `199d36d3db744650bcbda4b8111fce1f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_102.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia stares at you in shock before managing to say,{/n} "She's... dead? But why would you... No, shut it, you monster! I can't lay a hand on you, not while we're at war, but I won't stand here and listen to this! Damn it all, I need to be alone."
- `6795713cefe847bf8a8b186f57dd284e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_106.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia stares at you in shock before managing to say,{/n} "She's... dead? But why would you... No, shut it, you monster! I can't lay a hand on you, not while we're at war, but I won't stand here and listen to this! Damn it all, I need to be alone."
- `d7e69b576b1c4669b742d8c71cc1ab5a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_107.jbp`
  > "Leave me now. I need to be alone."
- `45c7b3f440cd49e1a9a879850825748f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_15.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia's gaze drops and her face hardens.{/n} "I knew it. But I didn't want to believe it." {n}Brushing a tear from her cheek, Anevia coughs and in a more even tone, says to you,{/n} "We'll talk later. I need to mourn her."
- `7f8bc800e717432ba8b14586ae3fe055` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_22.jbp`
  > {n}Her mouth set in a bitter frown, Anevia's voice is laced with pride and pain:{/n} "She died a hero. Of course she did. She couldn't go out any other way."
- `96bef7c725c0490ca47ed0fc75d0b6e7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_23.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia stares at you in shock before managing to say,{/n} "A creature? I guess there was no other way... What did you do to her? Though it hardly matters now." {n}The horrifying news has clearly left Anevia stunned.{/n}
- `dcf3c426a62d4534b734bf3c3676570d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_30.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia's gaze drops and her face hardens.{/n} "Damn it all. I need to be alone."
- `fdd7645daa464a6db80d712c7ab4cc0f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_39.jbp`
  > {n}With a quiet tenderness in her voice, Irabeth replies,{/n} "Every minute. When they hemmed us in near Iz, she nearly died trying to get to me. Now I'll lay down my life to ensure that not a single hair on her head is harmed."
- `dbeb8868ab9a4dfdadd675d1298bed39` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_44.jbp`
  > …s out plaintively as Anevia tosses it against the stones.{/n} "So what am I s'posed to do? Sit here and wait until all of 'em are killed?"…
- `a543e2c08d59438fbb9c38fdb2cd9c62` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_68.jbp`
  > {n}With a dark look, Anevia bites out through clenched teeth,{/n} "She died a hero, yeah? Of course she did." {n}She turns to the other Anevia and hisses furiously,{/n} "This is all your fault, you stupid bitch. If it wasn't for you, I'd have convinced her to desert ages ago!"
- `32d5fffa11af42449c41a724aa35306f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_69.jbp`
  > "Shut up." {n}Her mouth set in a bitter frown, Anevia's voice is laced with pride and pain:{/n} "She died a hero. Of course she did. She couldn't go out any other way."
- `745a1f40d98343439d50b311414e13a2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_71.jbp`
  > "I — We... need to be alone."
- `003ea0ebd4db49d29e633a18dbe2eeda` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_72.jbp`
  > {n}The other Anevia rasps,{/n} "Irabeth is dead, and you say thank you? You unfeeling bitch."
- `7b9a2b8ecb464c7f8d436aeafc8b42e5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_8.jbp`
  > "How goes it, Commander?" {n}Anevia looks entirely alive and completely unaffected by her recent death at your hands.{/n}
- `3de56057ba9d49e7aabb94e3c05d23aa` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_91.jbp`
  > "Brother Woljif, do you want to repeat what you whispered just now so everyone can hear? If you said I was lying, then just admit it — don't worry, I'll make your death painless."
- `a41c8e7dee76474c898f74d68612001b` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_94.jbp`
  > "Brother Woljif, do you want to repeat what you whispered just now so everyone can hear? If you said I was lying, then just admit it — don't worry, I'll make your death painless."
- `02c3704dd1ec41de83556ac482838f3a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC2_LowFantasy/Map1.5_Catacombs/Mercenaries/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "You don't know, do you?" {n}The sergeant looks away.{/n} "Anevia Tirabade is dead. She went missing along with everybody else when the demons came."
- `74b726d6f2484fc28fada2e23436355c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC2_LowFantasy/Map5_Tavern/Tavern_Anevia/Cue_9.jbp`
  > "Well, if that's true, then this journey alone should be worth at least one dissertation." {n}Rekarth smirks at Sendri as he usually does, but there is no malice in it this time — he is still basking in the afterglow of Anevia's praise.{/n}
- `d4b6dee2e672480aad6adb89af949ded` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC5_StoryEnds/AbyssHub/Sacrifice/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "Just do one thing for me: tell Anevia about this. Tell her I died as a crusader."
- `f8a5d88cf76a2134e8e3f3b8a617e2c1` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > The runaway from Nidal by the name of Anevia found no happiness in the River Kingdoms. She perished in obscurity, killed by one of the many beasts and cutthroats inhabiting that restless region.
- `ac60b84ed62f25f42a2eb0b86e19d345` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Answer_0121.jbp`
  > "Did Captain Seilkind and his people depart with the Queen?"
- `48e8e8f9452870a4a8ecbd00cc22354c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > …. And I wouldn't wish my homeland on my worst enemy. You've heard of the place, I'm sure. Ruled by monsters that aren't alive nor dead, and the official religion is the cult of {g|Zon-Kuthon}Zon-Kuthon{/g}. I grew up in a slum, like a weed between the cobblestones. I didn't have a dad, but I had lots of aunts and uncles... my mom's cronies. No prize for guessing the kind of business she was involved in. They gave me …
- `f9d8557898390b647a2754a43734fe78` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "When I was twelve, the monks of the Silent Shroud came for us. Creepy guys with their mouths sewn shut, they're the guards in Nisroch. Mom gave me to her friends, and we hid in a secret temple of {g|Desna}Desna{/g}. I never saw my mom again."
- `77ce51de8c99c064e98f7799040ac889` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "I lay low in the temple for the next few years, kept my head down. I washed floors, fetched water, listened to sermons. Funny thing, after a while I started liking {g|Desna}Desna's{/g} teachings. But as soon as I was old enough, I was outta there — I left {g|Nidal}Nidal{/g} and got as far away as I could."
- `fbbd07e1d3db90d4484cd7f651191b9a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > …seemed like heaven, my chance to sit back and enjoy life. But I wasn't happy. There just wasn't any joy in my new life. I was all alone. No one cared about me, and I didn't care about anybody either. I struggled to find a reason to drag myself out of bed every morning... tying a stone around my neck and jumping into the nearest river started to look pretty appealing."…
- `49f872a411248ad4f926f39a471270a0` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "After almost becoming a human sacrifice, I knew I never wanted to leave Irabeth's side. Desna knows, I fell for her instantly, and I fell hard. My misery was gone. And when Irabeth showed me what was in those papers — proof that the cultists had a nest in her home city — well, I offered to help without a second thought."
- `aa788ca3dc3b9924a96a13295bf2df9c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "It's like... living. Without her, I wouldn't be. Seriously, if I were alone, I'd definitely be gone by now."
- `9cdd999c06fd09743913cec652db5770` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > …s the crusaders say about you. Some say that {g|Iomedae}Iomedae{/g} came to you and appointed you her herald. Others say that you died, and an honest-to-gods angel is now leading the army disguised as you. Still others say it was just an explosion at an alchemist's lab, and I'm the one spreading rumors about your powers. I wish!"…
- `3bb9c39eecaeeef478c35571554cc700` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > "More dangerous. I have a gut instinct about these things. If I didn't, I'd already be dead. You're dangerous, Commander, and by Desna I hope you'll be dangerous to our enemies, and not to us... Or to yourself!"
- `585957b397ea5b14f9d62d7901757974` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0048.jbp`
  > … gut, there's some evil scum out there. We just have to look harder. Of course, I could send out more scouts, but they might just disappear like the first ones. Why don't you go there yourself and find out what's what?"…
- `c574c4b821079404cad660c7734cca7d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia looks at you sadly.{/n} "There's nothing good about this. The things you've had to do, and the fact we're fighting alongside the dead because we'll never pull this off without them. But at least we're moving forward, not falling back like before. I guess it was the only way. Bad times call for bad measures."
- `1a17e7f87f5783345a5142216b37cd68` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia shakes her head sharply.{/n} "Nope. Zero. Zilch. A whole lotta nothin'. The scum melted into thin air. Soldiers saw her jump out the window, run through the square in front of the citadel, and suddenly disappear. We haven't heard anything from her since. And I don't like it. I'm telling you, this is bad."
- `3bb36439829f9ed44b45c96e09c63f6f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0060.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia shakes her head in annoyance.{/n} "Not a clue. I've been trying to work it out for days. The vermin have gotta be coming from some kinda basement, you'd think. Or an abandoned building. Or, Desna forbid, some secret passage that we haven't found yet. We need to nip this in the bud — when those critters start breeding, it won't be pretty!"
- `e90e4094b175e1e42b19178b737441af` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia frowns and looks you dead in the eye.{/n} "I'm not gonna beat about the bush, I'll tell it to you straight. I don't have a clue what to make of all this. My paranoia's tellin' me to keep you far away from any position of power, in case you do something crazy... But my experience tells me that life is a lot more complicated than fanatics like Hulrun want to believe. So far, nothing you've done has made me do…
- `f600775f620d1d644833f3f9945fa29e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0067.jbp`
  > "Sure, I've done a lot things in my life, things I'm not too proud of. But if you start carrying on all hoity-toity with me because you've gone and got yourself some awesome new powers and now you know everything about everyone... I'm gonna lose all respect for you. Just a heads-up."
- `22f1b7ac20a43b747a0be6c7bb30a0ee` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0076.jbp`
  > "What else could I desire, when I have my Beth? Hmm..." {n}Anevia thinks for a moment, smiling, then looks over your shoulder and gives a cheeky wink.{/n} "Arueshalae, quit hiding. You put the Commander up to this, didn't you? Feeling shy, are we? Fine, I'll act like I never saw you. So, Commander..."
- `99d44492fddba354c9ad26e1415f5875` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0085.jbp`
  > … all kinds of scummy jobs. I crawled through marshlands, I scrambled over rooftops, I waded through sewers, I spied, I tracked, I killed... And that memory was the thing that kept me going, when I was sittin' up to my eyeballs in gore, or draggin' myself through the slums with an arrow in my side. I remembered the smell of that bread, the crunch of the crust, how it burned my little fingers. I remembered how happy I …
- `ac870114cdae9424299e1d452358b569` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0097.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia sighs.{/n} "Y'know... this war's left its mark on everybody, but her especially. All I want now is to live to see our victory and then whisk her away somewhere, as far away as I can manage. Someplace where it's quiet and bright, and where she'll never have to pick up a sword ever again."
- `42a59c793e7b4614a9f76ea6a2da339c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0102.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia smirks.{/n} "And it'll keep waitin'. The Nidal executioners are at the back of the line of all the people who want my head, behind the demons and the cultists. I won't abandon the crusade, you know that. Not because I'm tryin' to atone for my sins, but because it's my mission now. Mine and Irabeth's."
- `e7d5895a5967111418741f382a7494bf` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0107.jbp`
  > "When I left the temple, I lived as a woman. Not exactly hard, seeing how I was traveling alone. Then I met Irabeth. You can't imagine how terrified I was of telling her the truth... But she understood and she accepted me. And she even pawned her family heirloom to help me. The medicine she got for me, the thing she pawned her sword for, was an elixir to change my physical gender. That's it. That's the story."
- `74e7adc14fbfe86478643e83f83765d5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0114.jbp`
  > "Don't even ask. That name has meant nothing to me for years — it's just sounds. There's probably nobody left alive who knew that person. I'm Anevia Tirabade, that's all there is to it."
- `2aee5e7d9a0423c4a800bf44b25d2e1f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0129.jbp`
  > "I can't tell you the 'where,' but I can definitely tell you the 'who' — he's gone off searching for his beloved Sarkorians. It's a damn shame we lost him, too, we could do with another decent officer around here! Bloody amateur archeologist!"
- `171b262392778744aa04fbfdb9c7f8d0` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0132.jbp`
  > …and the gold Galfrey would put in their pockets. He was about to be thrown out on his ass, but Seilkind beat them to it — he just disappeared. He left Drezen and went who knows where. A new captain was sent to replace him, but the Queen took him and his soldiers with her when she left."…
- `ec1219cf3a664baab8987200e0fe1aa7` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_3.jbp`
  > "It's sad that Irabeth won't come into the kitchen, and I won't break her off a steaming, fragrant piece of my bread. I guess my dream wasn't meant to come true after all."
- `85be303d0128cab468a516df65c79ca2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > …en among the ranks of the paladins! After that, delaying my homecoming would be tantamount to desertion. Luckily, I was no longer alone. Anevia was as eager to destroy the cultists as I was."…
- `1f5578a8aac321243bb395ed8407e85b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "After the Queen entrusted me with leading the order, Anevia and I practically built it from scratch. We got rid of dead weight, people who weren't committed. We organized ways to transmit messages and orders safely. We introduced reliable ciphers. We found tacit allies around the city — from crusader orders to street beggars."
- `9829e10436576654ba5f3c87c2724ef8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0055.jbp`
  > …y of handing over the reins, at first. I was afraid that everything would fall apart without Anevia and me. But I took a risk and left a very clever lad in charge — a tiefling, as it happens. He infiltrated the cult alone — they couldn't imagine a tiefling wouldn't be on their side! He exposed an entire cell. I personally knighted him, just before the city was attacked. Of course, there were many who weren't happy ab…
- `cb1b7fa4c79b9f7438004adacabebbea` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0151.jbp`
  > …rabeth looks down.{/n} "This scabbard held the Solemn Hour — my family sword. My father once fought with it, and I took it when I left home to become a paladin. Alas, it was not stolen. I parted with my heirloom willingly, to help someone dear to me. Anevia needed expensive healing, and, having no other way to procure the money, I pawned my father's sword. I think he would have understood — I'm sure he would have giv…
- `0c6aaf1020e847e29ced793aa8f58ba7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_4.jbp`
  > …f Anevia's scouts knocked a rod from the cultist's hand — the rod I gave to you. In revenge, the demon worshiper cast a spell and vanished, along with the same brave warriors who dared to fight against him."…
- `5f306afcd4d305b41a611a59cc35e274` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_main/Cue_0084.jbp`
  > {n}In a lightning-fast motion, Anevia knocks something from the woman's hands — an amulet that seemingly appeared out of nowhere. It falls to the ground and disappears in a flash of magic.{/n} "What's the rush, sweetheart? Stay awhile. The Commander and me, we've got a couple questions for you, too."
- `a9f9d4eebd2c03e438ba0f3792ba7078` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MeetCamelia/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia peers at the dead man's face.{/n} "Hang on, I think I know him. His name's Aravashnial, the egghead from the library. He was a good lad, even if he was kinda stuck-up... May his soul rest in peace!"
- `07eb5aa03382f9943ac3e8ab9735bc26` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/NeathholmVillage/LannWakeUp/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "The messenger returned. The tribes are gathering at the entrance to the Maze, our people have already gone there. I've warned Anevia and that old guy who won't even acknowledge me. Take your time, there's no rush — the most important people always show up late." {n}Lann looks around.{/n} "Everything's going to plan, but one thing's bothering me: Wenduag's {d|c0 true mongrels}gone{/d}."
- `4defab5ab3382294f90ba5c85fbfea0d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GargoyleAttack/Irabeth/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia shoots an arrow at the monster as it flies away — and they both vanish into the black of night. She looks at you, terror and resolution warring in her eyes.{/n} "Commander... convene an urgent council." {n}Before you can answer, she runs off to the camp headquarters.{/n}
- `371b8aa404933b34fa911e02f5ace461` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GargoyleAttack/Irabeth_End/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia shoots an arrow at the monster as it flies away — and they both vanish into the black of night. She looks at you, terror and resolution warring in her eyes.{/n} "Commander... convene an urgent council." {n}Before you can answer, she runs off to the camp headquarters.{/n}
- `e17e3f0692055b447b4ce37c256f9c4d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/AneviaDialog/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia approaches the swarm queen's body. Her face clearly expresses a desire to kick the dead beast, but she refrains.{/n} "Just a brainless bug, and so many of our people died because of her! Just yesterday I was sitting by the fire with some of the people the swarm devoured..."
- `0258c2a124d2dd446bd98bf35591ddd8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/SwarmProblem_BE/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > The Commander and {mf|his|her} party disappear among the rocks, preparing to slip down into the canyon, while the swarm is distracted by their bait. A tight-lipped Anevia shows the way. Behind them, the final preparations are being made.
- `6d4fa662ea5954045b91946acdc923bb` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/SwarmProblem_BE/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > …ind the first body — the mangled corpse of a refugee from {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g}. A few straggling vescavors wriggle inside the dead man's gaping chest. Tiny larvae in greenish shells, all shuffling legs and hinging jaws — they don't seem menacing at all. But then, the rasping noise stops for a moment, and a high vibrating note pierces the air with new force. A blur of a blackish-green cloud rises above the canyon.…
- `12c9083b96cd6c243b2ac50c7557b492` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Drezen_C3/Irabeth_C3_Intro/Cue_0070.jbp`
  > "We've temporarily placed the body of Staunton Vhane in the dungeon. You slew him — what are your orders regarding his body? Anevia thinks we should burn it quietly and forget about it. Some want to see his corpse displayed in the square. But I don't think we should behave like demons and barbarians. He may have been our enemy, but he deserves to be buried as a knight. However, the decision is yours, of course."
- `e565c70d9cd5d7e46914b341ac32872f` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/Coronation/Answer_0432.jbp`
  > [Turn to Anevia] "I'm sorry Irabeth is dead."
- `a1c86f7c0c3358c4ebfceb58739fe96e` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/Coronation/Answer_0434.jbp`
  > [Turn to Anevia] "I'm sorry Irabeth is dead."
- `555f2e30c2870fa42a2a3761613edbb9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/Coronation/Cue_0068.jbp`
  > {n}You grant Irabeth and Anevia your mercy — their lives end at the same time, and both women depart easily, neither forced to watch their beloved suffer.{/n}
- `f6ebe7bcf17cce24d9a6d02a02ec496f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/Coronation/Cue_0080.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth and Anevia are trying to fight back — but the forces are laughably unequal. You make sure their death is especially long and painful. Once Anevia realizes the end is near, she tries to turn to her wife and say something... and you dispatch the two lovers one moment before their eyes can meet for the final time.{/n}
- `ebd2589ca47ae384498409a4aacda913` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/Coronation/Cue_0426.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia flinches and immediately vanishes from view, as if she had been expecting that. The last thing you glimpse is her hateful glare.{/n}
- `498efe7685991e44fa9b065dfb9a5a44` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/Coronation/Cue_0431.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia flinches and immediately vanishes from view, as if she had been expecting that. The last thing you glimpse is her hateful glare.{/n}
- `f20c5d72f04acc04dbcac3de10b1c5c2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia's clothes are dirty, and her face and hands are smeared with dust. She uses the broom she's holding to offer you a cheery salute.{/n} "Nothin' to it! A couple days and the city's as good as new. The streets have been cleaned, all the ashes left from the demons have been swept away, and we've patched up the fortifications. We got guards on the walls, and you in charge... It's almost like the good ol' days."
- `e2dcefc6e2d1d0249937b80737d4c9b4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > …ell it like it is — things still ain't looking all that good for us. Supplies are running short and there are hardly any soldiers left to defend this place... but the city's standing, that's what counts. This war ain't over yet."…
- `8409b3ca6ae9eea49aea6cefc5efff70` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0034.jbp`
  > …orns out of her pockets, and shakes something that looks suspiciously like confetti out of her hair.{/n} "Just look at this — you left the city at the mercy of the 'free' crusaders, and see what they've done! I don't know if they're manning the defenses or rehearsing a circus act! But I'm not really mad, of course — you all gave the demons such a thrashing, they probably haven't even come to yet!"…
- `6429577e90ee7af4791e47eb1413d9b8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia's wavering, anxious voice seems grotesquely out of place. She speaks, shamelessly breaking the tranquility and remarkable hush that reigns in the city.{/n} "Well, that's it, Commander. We're all dead... or we soon will be. This ain't our crusade anymore, it's just yours."
- `d9079d27b538554418be4a05f14b6bb8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > … is staggering slightly — truth be told, you haven't seen a completely sober person since the moment the goddess and the demoness departed the merry city of Drezen, leaving behind its jubilant crowd headed by the King. Anevia approaches you and salutes you with her full tankard. You notice a pair of fluffy ears on her head.{/n} "Here's to you! And to Drezen! I knew you'd be back, and it'd all be like ol' times again,…
- `681fbe379bd262142a2514d2b624cab1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0071.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia sighs heavily and looks at you sadly.{/n} "You used to be different... What have we become? An army of the dead... It's a mockery of what was once called a crusade... And the scariest part is, now it's too late to change tack. We've got no choice but to keep going."
- `a3efa48f066d25e49a91f0dbea253408` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0091.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia shrugs.{/n} "Somebody's killing somebody. Before you came back, I would've called 'em vicious street fights, but now it's just... normal life in Drezen. The commanding officers are dead and the ties between the various units are broken. Now each one just does what it wants — and they all want bloodshed and plunder. This horde will only listen to you."
- `92647614716be694abbbc91375d73d47` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0096.jbp`
  > "It's good that Irabeth went with the Queen. She... She doesn't need to know what your dead did to the folks that survived the attack. If fate brings the two of us together again, I'll tell her that they were as good as dead anyway, even before you came." {n}Anevia's voice sounds hollow.{/n}
- `501529cbeb728ae42b8f028131f7aec4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0097.jbp`
  > …after you, and you will kill me. I swear this in memory of all those who survived the demons' assault only to be devoured by your dead. I will never be able to forget their screams. But until we win, I will fight and fulfill your orders." {n}Irabeth lowers her gaze, ignoring the expression of horror on Anevia's face.{/n}…
- `93c6b0b36718a524295469b485c88fc6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0127.jbp`
  > …ly free from sin? What's the plan now — drive everybody out of the city, or sentence them to a hundred lashes? There'll be nobody left. Sure, it'll be nice and quiet around here, but who's gonna fight for you?" {n}Anevia catches her breath.{/n} "Sorry for talking so bluntly and all. I'm used to speaking home truths, and not bottling up things that I think need to be said."…
- `53da0bb18b8d626439d166a35907cdc4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia folds her arms.{/n} "Things were quiet at first. Y'know, as quiet as they were when you were here, I mean. The Queen took hold of the reins, but she wasn't gonna go against your wishes, so she kinda left everything as it was. She just told everybody to hold on, that you'd be back soon." {n}Anevia sighs.{/n} "Things carried on like that for six months."
- `2266e46fb91b32846ac0cd3e613a4643` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > …he Abyss. But you know our Galfrey... she's laid plenty of friends to rest in her life. If she called off the crusade after every death, we'd have lost to the demons a long time ago, right?"…
- `04923e59ebb21f943b7a2a330a6a8820` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "You were declared dead, see. We did things properly, though, threw you a big funeral feast and everythin'. Everybody was bawling their eyes out — even Beth shed a tear for ya. The Queen sent all the civilians to Nerosyan, took the Sword of Valor, and ordered all the troops to follow her. Only... some folks kinda turned down that particular honor." {n}Anevia shoots you a smile.{/n} "You're a wonder. Our wonder. You'v…
- `e50de7637b98eb144bed74c612bdd7e6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "So, about a week goes by, and then the demons, the scum, started sniffing around, it was obvious the Queen was long gone... and that's when it all started. They came at us with a whole bloody army, those sons of bitches! And they're led by Orengofta, a fat hog of a demon. We had to surrender the fortress and retreat to safety. We've been here ever since — scouting out what they're planning next, listening in when we…
- `30b3f3ec4777101459085fc12f5379b2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0024.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia frowns.{/n} "We're not the only ones, y'know. There's a whole gang of us — a ragtag crew, for sure, but we're all united, we're all fighting for you. Except, less than a week after Galfrey left with the army, the demons have got us in a pincer movement — Orengofta on the one side, that fat hog, and on the other side, that bitch Aponavicius."
- `7eee0c2e98cbc9847858b22a0be872c9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > …le gang of us — a ragtag crew, for sure, but we're all united, we're all fighting for you. Except, less than a week after Galfrey left with the army, the demons had us in a pincer movement — Orengofta on the one side, that fat hog, and on the other side, that bitch Aponavicius."…
- `b543043ab19ed644596842e5c28a18bb` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia grits her teeth.{/n} "Right, Commander. We're bloody fools. We should've just given up and left like everybody else, but instead we decided to hole up here and try to do somethin'. I don't know what I was expecting from you, but it definitely wasn't that. Anyway, you don't know anything that's been going on, so I'll get you caught up."
- `e8412ce1c81f3ab40b3f38077d04a1e6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > …l punch. But you better hurry — I haven't heard from any of the units in the city for a long while. Maybe... maybe there's no one left to round up."…
- `27c812b08e84e324ea333ef480a99de6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > …he was headed, because the Commander would be back soon and would be asking about it. That's the last I heard from him. He's been gone half the day."…
- `94aa64341a893fb479dc7cef9cc81f49` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia grimly shakes her head.{/n} "Deserted, they did. I didn't see that coming, I'll admit. They were good people, strong. There were no signs anythin' was wrong. But one night, they all just got up as one and left. The guards at the gate tried to stop 'em — fat lot of good that did! The mongrels slit the guards' throats and vanished into the Wound. Nobody knows what happened to 'em, but there've been rumors."
- `9c46f21ca2eb32a478e9f6aa88dc5607` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/HilorDangerousPrison/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Hilor the Pathfinder salutes you.{/n} "Commander. You've risen from the dead after all, and not a minute too soon... I suppose that means I owe Anevia money now. I've never been so happy to lose a bet in my entire life. By the way, we've got a little brawl coming up here — we could really use your help."
- `32022c917b3067f4785c2ede2fb74c68` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/HilorDangerousPrison/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}Hilor the Pathfinder salutes you.{/n} "Commander! You've risen from the dead after all, and not a minute too soon... I suppose that means I owe Anevia money now. I've never been so happy to lose a bet in my entire life."
- `fa75ecb22a8c7cd408e23eeb380f5e16` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/StorytellerDangerousDrezen/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > …supposed to serve the Queen, but our hearts are here. Besides, you are still the commander of the crusade, and I have no right to abandon you — even if there's almost no hope left. Although I must admit that the Storyteller's tale about his vision of your imminent return seemed quite vague. I followed him here to make sure he didn't accidentally hurt himself." {n}Irabeth smiles happily.{/n} "I'm glad that he proved t…
- `654173d86f14b5f40b3be978ffd31e43` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/VendorDangerousTavern/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "You can say that again!" {n}the tiefling says in a loud, excited whisper.{/n} "We've already had your funeral! Where'd you come from? Oh, I see — Anevia must have sent you to help us. And she was right to do so, I tell you!"
- `1f6f05c729cf7274390fa5c640436dcc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethDies/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "Anevia. Tell her I love her. Take care... of my Nevi." {n}Her eyes roll back. Irabeth Tirabade is dead.{/n}
- `eab42dfba54ded24eb49467579d74de1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/KTC_WintersunHelp/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia looks sad and troubled.{/n} "Terrible news, Commander. Wintersun is... gone. Our scouts report that nothing remains but corpses. The clan fought the demons... and it was the death of 'em."
- `863db27d7f8780142b1413bdb4bd98ae` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/KTC_WintersunHelp/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia looks sad and troubled.{/n} "Terrible news, Commander. Wintersun is... gone. Our scouts found the last of 'em leaving. The clan fought the demons... and most of 'em didn't make it."
- `50e268f36f5c7e64183211b88660e5ca` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/KTC_WintersunHelp/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > "Same thing, just living {d|c3 wintersun illusions stay}in ignorance{/d}. They thought the ones comin' to their village were travelers. So when the demons attacked, they sent word to us, askin' us to drive off the bandits." {n}Anevia chuckles mirthlessly.{/n} "We'd have helped, but that was when the Queen left, so we had our hands full. Had our own 'bandits' to deal with."
- `0b5bd36cd98c4c642832bfd4a3501cb6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/IrabethAnevia_Threshold/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "It was unforgettable. That attack on the Gray Garrison was the last desperate push, the last hope of holding the city when it seemed only a miracle could save us. We would've died there — but you and Anevia leaped out of the ground and then miracles began to happen!"

## aranka

Authored return/rescue predicates: `aranka.trickster.returned`.

- `0f80699cd8f64991a0d02faed51e2693` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Aranka/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "But... why?" {n}Aranka gasps.{/n} "Oh, I had so many wonderful wishes left to make. And no doubt the Portal will be terribly bored now."
- `35a7285b07f44638b62a119cc45fd3a4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Aranka/Cue_4.jbp`
  > "This is the last requiem of Maestro Ecclé. Completed. Do you understand? He died before he could finish his last work — but here it is! I have seen the Maestro's handwriting a thousand times, this is the real thing!" {n}Aranka gazes at the sheet music like it's a precious relic.{/n} "And I have many more treasures that are just as wondrous! Many, many more!"
- `2e756329fd5fa1c4daa7c654bf53df93` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/OraclePartyHouse/DesnaAdept2/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "I'm Aranka, traveling bard and follower of {g|Desna}Desna{/g}. Many of my fellow bards are rootless misfits who wander the world, unwilling or unable to live a 'normal' life. For me, that's only partly true. I have a wonderful family: my mother, father, grandmother, and brother. Right now they are far away and I'm sure they miss me... but they have always supported me in my vocation and they let me be free to roam. …
- `844173bc3da0744439eb2f977ae8dd0f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Azata/IslandTalk/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}The island is {d|azata chaotic}abuzz{/d} — the free crusaders are busy with military exercises. Judging by the din, friendly skirmishes flare up between different groups and occasionally grow into something rather less friendly. Standing alone in the midst of the chaos, Aranka tries to sing. Noticing you, she gives up her futile efforts and waves at you cheerfully.{/n} "Commander! Aivu! Finally!"
- `82118d47a2fb23f4d88d276c75c36e8e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Azata/IslandTalk/Cue_0050.jbp`
  > "I don't know if you've heard, but after you left, we were kicked out of Drezen. Though I wasn't very upset by this — our friends were disorderly. I'm not talking about anyone in particular..." {n}Aranka pointedly avoids looking at the halfling warriors shifting from foot to foot.{/n} "Anyone at all. But setting the courthouse on fire, even just 'a little bit,' was definitely uncalled for."
- `17f9a30dfc9351b4dbbd95365b3b3ccb` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Azata/IslandTalk/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > "I don't know if you've heard, but after you left, we were kicked out of Drezen. They said it was for our own good as well as the common good. It was hard for the free crusaders to live among the un-free ones! Well, I don't know about our strict, well-trained paladin friends, but it definitely worked out best for us — we didn't have to look at their long faces anymore!" {n}Aranka snorts.{/n}
- `4484561be5915fd45839b06a3b10d09a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Azata/IslandTalk/Cue_0052_MournTall.jbp`
  > "Any yet..." {n}Aranka lowers her head.{/n} "We had a hard time without Wallflower around. He was the calmest of us all... He never made a fuss, or picked fights, or bawled songs... But when he died in Midnight Fane, the world seemed to grow quieter, as if our song had lost a note. Everything was not as it was before..."
- `b638901ae607bfa48885ef2f5caed1f0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Azata/IslandTalk/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "Any yet..." {n}Aranka lowers her head.{/n} "We had a hard time without Wallflower around. He was the calmest of us all... He never made a fuss, or picked fights, or bawled songs... But when he died in Midnight Fane, the world seemed to grow quieter, as if our song had lost a note. Everything was not as it was before..."

## arueshalae

Authored return/rescue predicates: `arueshalae.trickster.returned`, `arueshalae.trickster.returned.counting`, `arueshalae.trickster.returned.hundred`, `arueshalae.trickster.returned.sosiel`.

- `74e14e8f7aed09544bc2d1a338dbeb65` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Answer_0248.jbp`
  > "Don't you feel lonely in here?"
- `5614b9d4c520e9a408d7d1a8bc928fd4` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Answer_0278.jbp`
  > "Desna has abandoned you?"
- `559dabba6b9738d468944e6f42181589` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Answer_0287.jbp`
  > "I saw you disappearing in a kaleidoscope of butterflies. What was that?"
- `03bc31758afc5ca4aaa0f49c7bfd4e03` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "Now I see I knew nothing. A demon warrior probably also considers himself an expert on mortals because he's killed so many of them. He certainly knows where to thrust the spear for a quick death, and where to thrust it for a long and painful one. And he thinks that's enough. My knowledge... it was of the same kind."
- `90550487f6fe72b49ac32941029a05db` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "Normally, when a human dies, the {g|Pharasma}Lady of Graves{/g} decides which plane they go to. Most often, those who arrive in the Abyss turn into larvae — you know, the little worms no one notices until you step on them. They crawl in the mud for centuries, devouring each other... and the strongest ones turn into demons."
- `3f6f1c49031ec6c49a386b9a4454c8aa` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > …ntary images. Terrors, executions, orgies... I remember some poor lad, half-gnawed by the larvae. He was crying and begging to be killed. And I pointed at him, and laughed, and said I wouldn't. Desna forgive me, I'm so ashamed..."…
- `9bed9bb73750a8e4aaf409d09bef1233` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0071.jbp`
  > "Maybe I could have found a unit that would accept me... Or maybe the first mortals I revealed myself to would have killed me. Or worse, I might have ravaged those foolish enough to trust me. I preferred to act in the shadows until I knew what to expect from myself — and others."
- `366e8fa8a702c79429aebb735b034dce` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0149.jbp`
  > {n}You have never seen Arueshalae looking so happy and inspired. She gives you a wide smile.{/n} "There you are. I can't wait to share my joy with you!"
- `3dc872b5fa1b53144b0bd0a7a226bf50` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0169.jbp`
  > "An old friend from days long, long gone."
- `d8b75fd01bbc13b4591cb0d14d5d32b8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0173.jbp`
  > "There are so many things I still have to learn... Mortals usually find ways to deal with their nightmares, and so will I. Thank you for your help and... I'm sorry that you had to watch that awful spectacle unfold." {n}The succubus gets to her feet.{/n} "Now please excuse me. I need some time alone to think about everything I've just seen."
- `fb99761a51aae8c44b6ed7273cad9984` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0181.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae purses her lips. Her ruby eyes are full of pain.{/n} "It was from an old friend... From days gone by. He found out that I was in the city and decided to drop me a note. A reminder of things I don't want to remember at all. I don't want to, and I won't."
- `05798d562a9e7b24c91d715c7b8fba89` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0184.jbp`
  > …duced him, sent him away, beckoned him back with false hope of a reunion... I made him do things I'm too ashamed to recall now. I left him somewhere in Alushinyrra, thinking that he was going to die in the nearest ditch, yet he somehow survived. And now... This wretched creature has spent so many years in the Abyss that I'm not sure if there's anything still left of the person I knew."…
- `7b71d031eb3ef064cbb7a953e2485a23` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0215.jbp`
  > …rney? Of course I needed them. This revelation would not have helped me at all back then. It took me several decades of haphazard wandering in order to understand how dreams work and what I should do with them. The answer was simple, yes, but this journey was the actual lesson the goddess wanted to teach me."…
- `09388e891eb1d854b9e027aaf970feb7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0217.jbp`
  > …n in both cases. Warden Staunton of Drezen did not believe that I had come to the fortress gates to turn myself in. He would have killed me on the spot if not for the interference of Desna's priests. That's how I ended up in the dungeons."…
- `2cad5dfc7bc6c8142bd92c1ed1881f24` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0258.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae shakes her head.{/n} "I am not going to discuss that. Not with you. The person who was so precious to me is gone. The last thing {mf|he|she} did in {mf|his|her} life was rescuing me... from {mf|himself|herself}. I will honor {mf|his|her} memory by staying with you and helping you finish {mf|his|her} war, but don't even try to make me talk to you about {mf|him|her}".
- `8d07b26f5d9d9f2419b98f5940b49a24` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0265.jbp`
  > "You did so much for me that I simply couldn't leave you alone in this. This is how we fight the Abyss: it is constantly trying to break us one by one, but we hold on to each other and stand strong together."
- `1b26f732ffa48c04fa2efcfca7933ed9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0290.jbp`
  > "No, her gift did not come back. She is not going to rescue me from danger anymore, but I am not afraid. I am no longer alone. I have allies who won't let me die. I... I have friends now."
- `8c8f0af6a70f37f429aaf90686a595b7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Arueshalae/Cue_0291.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae bows her head.{/n} "You do not wish to have a demon in your ranks. I understand. But please let me stay just for a little while longer. I have one very important thing left to do, which is also going to help the crusade. I will tell you everything in detail very soon, I just need to gather my thoughts."
- `9ec68b1154d41124298a92c1d029bc78` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Evil/EvilArueshalaeCompanion/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "Oh, I've always liked mortals. Of course, I abandoned my foolish attempts to learn something from you all. Don't be offended, but being a demon is much better, you've got to admit. And yet, these fragile little lives... It seems watching them has become a habit of mine."
- `7f51f9ccd3e33f54aa43663ada7692d6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Evil/EvilArueshalaeCompanion/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae waves her hand carelessly.{/n} "They're gone — who cares? What were they good for, anyway? Hoarding rubbish? Housing the shade of that fool Dimalchio? No, I don't care for that nonsense anymore. I have enough pleasures in the waking world."
- `ef9cab697eec5ed4794c9861816b4cd8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q1/DreamVisit_1-1/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}Suddenly a pile of dead demons appears before you as if by magic.{/n}
- `d3c260859f9f34d4a82c972828acbb2b` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q1/DreamVisit_1-1/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "Right... The Commander's title alone gives you a lot of power over your people, but what if you want more? Perhaps a royal title would suit you better? I can picture you in a crown quite well."
- `159ca9f0c07d049438437bb95a1fafa1` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q1/DreamVisit_1-1/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > {n}A subtle blush touches the demon's pale skin.{/n} "Mortals have so many different feelings. Friendship, loyalty, sympathy... Even love. They are all so mysterious and beautiful... But one can't truly learn anything about them alone, and who would dare share their heart's desires with a predator from the Abyss who is unable to reciprocate?"
- `004e02b68eb0d5f488571fc0cc7fc40b` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/ArushaAfterBattle/Answer_0006.jbp`
  > "You ruined his life, and now you've killed him."
- `867ae361d91c4c340b7fa35135614d38` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/ArushaAfterBattle/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae kneels before the dead azata's body. She closes his eyes and plants a kiss on his bloodied forehead.{/n} "Forgive me, Dimalchio. Forgive me, and farewell."
- `e18108434e9f5b3438077f5bce2e35bc` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/ArushaAfterBattle/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > "Let's go, {Name}." {n}The succubus wipes away her tears.{/n} "There is nothing left for us here."
- `d6e0f0a63d170aa4d9a94f90645e6f46` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/ArushaAfterBattle/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "Choice and free will are just words that liars and manipulators use to justify their actions. I know this too well. It's easy to break a person's will, and the freedom to choose means little when you don't know your options. No, {Name}, don't console me. It is my fault, and my last chance for atonement now lies dead."
- `77b48ec089505bb47a1b39378a954fec` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/ArushaAfterBattle/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > "Yes. It is my fault, and now I have no way to atone for it. What is left for me now? All I can do is be kinder to those around me, whether mortal or not."
- `50b801460f40d194f98c0b748ee835b2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dimalchio-after/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > …I didn't mean to say any of that — I just missed you so much... I needed you so much... You're my only desire, my only dream. You alone!"…
- `54b802f68a88776478daf8483bfcc188` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dimalchio/Answer_0046.jbp`
  > [Reach for your weapon] "You've bored me to death!"
- `429a0817b1463c842b50063068dde32b` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dimalchio/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > … with whomever I want. They respect me in the city — I open my purse, and they prostrate themselves before me. All my enemies are gone now. What else is there to want? I'm happy!"…
- `41d7f57516b5cec49bfdf61f6f80ef12` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dimalchio/Cue_0085.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae nods. She takes a deep breath and proclaims in a timid, trembling voice,{/n} "Dimalchio, I... I have embarked on the path of ascension. I'm going there, to {g|Elysium}Elysium{/g}. I'd like you to... I'd like you to abandon the Abyss and... together we could... I'd like you to try..."
- `f927a7d07c91e4d4bb8acdf6550a7f26` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dream_End/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > {n}The flames rise higher and higher. Arueshalae's dream disappears in the all-consuming fire.{/n}
- `c9360bc8e195ac14d8157c08994bea5d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dream_End/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "I don't know. I don't!" {n}Arueshalae knocks on the wooden surface.{/n} "It's... just a table. Anevia's fresh bread used to be here, but now it's gone — and yet the table remains."
- `3db51d2b8b0df8e4abb47547db298b93` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dream_End/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "I don't know." {n}Arueshalae sighs pensively.{/n} "Perhaps. Perhaps I won't do anything yet. Everything has burned down, apart from my nightmare and this table. I'll come back here to..." {n}She shakes her head.{/n} "I need some time. I need some time alone to think about it. Later. Let's go back to the real world for now."
- `65f230708d3a7e3479939b6a9e3fff90` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dream_End/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "I don't know. I probably won't do anything yet. Everything has burned down, apart from my nightmare and this table. You know, I might just..." {n}She cuts herself short and turns away, blushing a little.{/n} "No, that won't do... Or... I'm sorry, I need some time. I need some time alone to think about it and sort it out in my head. Can we discuss this in a few days? For now, let's just go back to the real world."
- `b480265bac79c0443917ae1aa998ee4a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/Dream_Start/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > {n}The flames rise higher and higher. Arueshalae's dream disappears in the all-consuming fire.{/n}
- `9e430a2cb24a2b54ca52d4e5debccfaf` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/KTC_Letter/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > "He was an {g|azata}azata{/g} once. A true hero — a knight and a poet, a defender of the weak. The pride of Elysium. And I... I corrupted him. I made him fall in love with me. I broke his heart. I made him commit foul crimes I cannot even describe, and when he became indistinguishable from a demon, I left him in the Abyss."
- `86662e428adc96744b5557a86d828ef2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/KTC_Letter/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "That is what my nightmare calls me. My most horrific disgrace. There was a time when that name suited me perfectly. I've poisoned so many souls... I've ruined so many beautiful creatures, both mortal and not..." {n}The succubus shakes her head forlornly.{/n} "Almost all of them are dead now. But this nightmare of mine is still alive, and that means there may still be hope of saving him."
- `a2b3b83075ccfd74ca5bb6815a783971` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q2/KTC_Letter/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > "I don't want to. You have no idea how afraid I am. What will I say? How will I look him in the eye after everything I've done to him? But I feel I must do this. I must see him. I must tell him that a fall like his is not a death sentence, that there is a way back. And if he won't listen to me..." {n}Arueshalae looks down once again.{/n} "I'll simply ask for his forgiveness."
- `72eb687afc938d4479a1bd3b46f452a5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/BackToReality/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}The monster is gone, and the kitchen is peaceful again. The smell of freshly baked cookies wafts through the air, and steam rises from cups of hot, fragrant tea that have been set out on the table. Arueshalae gazes around her longingly. You have never seen her so happy.{/n} "I... I just want to live here, in the world of mortals. With the ones I hold dear. I have solved your riddle, goddess. This is my true dream.…
- `5a7d0107b72b35249be56ff084ca7e48` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/BackToReality/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > "I've never felt better! Everything that I hated about myself is... gone. I look at you, and I don't feel hunger. I don't want to kill. All the unholy cravings I experienced as a demon — they're all gone. Now I just want to be happy. To live... and to love."
- `c94e1d5b0d8b2034986d2df9351734c2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/BackToReality/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > {n}The monster is gone, and all is peaceful once more. The smell of freshly baked cookies wafts through the air, and steam rises from cups of hot, fragrant tea that have been set out on the table. Arueshalae gazes around her longingly. You have never seen her so happy.{/n} "I... I just want to live here, in the world of mortals. With you. I have solved the goddess's riddle. This is my true dream!"
- `b96e4f57163667344b50d8008830c490` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/BackToReality/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > "I've never felt better! Everything that I hated about myself is... gone. I look at you, and I don't feel hunger. I don't want to kill. All the unholy cravings I experienced as a demon — they're all gone. Now I just want to be happy. To live... and to love."
- `390987aab66ad7d4fbcfd4a8cce9aad8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/FinalDream/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "Planning to drink some tea?" {n}A hissing voice full of bitter hatred can be heard in the darkness.{/n} "You used to drink souls before. How many living souls did you kill, you fiend? How many did you torture to death? You don't deserve a life like this!"
- `2a907a7cf2a4c8742a8c388b26fbb990` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/FinalDream/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae stops to catch her breath.{/n} "These nightmares will never leave me alone. I know that I deserve them, but... it is so hard to fight them off over and over again."
- `bd3acc07bba09ae46aa4b04e712e8851` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/FinalDream/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > "I have wronged you badly, and I feel guilty. I'm not trying to make excuses. There's nothing I could say that would excuse what I've done. But you are dead, Dimalchio. I will never forget you — but I will not let you take my life."
- `08e5ee3cafa2e954f90f53098e5e0e30` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/FinalDream/Cue_0037.jbp`
  > "But I am not alone. Who's that with me? It's you! We're laughing! We're carefree and happy. We're drinking tea and reminiscing about days gone by."
- `4cb3c734954765e4696c7bf066e200e8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/FinalDream/Cue_0039.jbp`
  > "It's a shame that we're the only two here. I would have liked to invite all my friends, but... I guess you're my only real friend." {n}The succubus looks sad for a moment, but then she brightens.{/n} "Still... I am not alone."
- `daea9e6e98d266f4dab852aae22fcd40` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/KTC_AskAnevia/Answer_0008.jbp`
  > "Leave me alone. I have more important things to do than rummage through your dreams!"
- `210ca780c93428b49b4e682d97ed84c2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Arueshalae/Q3/KTC_AskAnevia/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "Not openly. It still lurks in the shadows, gnawing at my soul from within... It never leaves me alone. But it hasn't tried to fight me again."
- `78b4479b4afb4b948870a25d33a9d719` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C3/After_Lab/Cue_0024.jbp`
  > …ing that I haven't spared a thought for people who aren't just worried about thoughts and feelings, but about matters of life and death. If a troop of crusaders is saved because of information I procured, that matters more than all the things I'd like to do to them, and..." {n}Arueshalae stops talking and looks down. In a quiet voice, she continues,{/n} "Thank you for reminding me about the world around me. My though…
- `7535a236753b7c043bad656cbcfe6410` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C3/After_Lab/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > "I've spent so much of my life alone, with nothing but my own thoughts for company. Sometimes it feels like they've become more real to me than the world around me."
- `0d70e0017b7c49e42ab98f1a849f0520` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C3/After_Lab/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > {n}She smiles pensively.{/n} "Inside my head, alone with all these desires, doubts, and thoughts, everything seems so frightening and baffling. But talking with you makes me feel so much better. This doesn't happen with anyone else, just with you. When you're near, I have no doubt that I will break free of the Abyss. And I know that, if I happen to lose my way, you'll show me the right path."
- `c628f49a3c7049c409558ac074d093b7` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C3/After_Wintersun/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "A whole village living in a world of illusion for a century... You know, ever since we left, I keep thinking, 'What if the same thing is happening to me now? What if I just think I've joined the crusade and I'm fighting against the demons?' It's just that, what I'm doing here, it all seems so unreal."
- `53d1119730c6bf749ab51a223d26a219` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C3/After_Wintersun/Cue_0034.jbp`
  > "Do I?" {n}Arueshalae wrinkles her forehead.{/n} "I think I do... When you talk about it, everything seems so simple. But when I'm alone again, all the doubts come back. They pile up, more and more of them, and by morning I'm back to not knowing what's real and what's not."
- `6d1e6787dadc62346942aaf969f28869` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C3/After_Wintersun/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > "Is that so? That means that neither mortals nor demons can tell for sure if they're living their real lives or if everything they see is just an illusion." {n}Arueshalae sighs.{/n} "Then the only thing I have left is hope. Even if my life is just an illusion, it's still better than before, when I lived in the Abyss... when I lived without you by my side."
- `89a8f8954ec9b4c40b378cefcd345d7a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C4/Beggar/Cue_0027.jbp`
  > "You don't have to do anything. This is simply for you, no reason... Pass it on!" {n}The succubus says the last words to the abrikandilu back as he scurries off.{/n} "That's it. He's gone. I'm not even sure if there was any point in all that. I bet he didn't even understand a thing..."
- `43fe5f1e0882dc245b5fdd7cf5b000f0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C4/Brothel_Inside/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > "Trust me, Rokhorn, you don't know me at all now. The Arueshalae that you used to know is long gone."
- `6b27eaf9f10e1654eb99924d3f1ba8de` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Additional_C4/Brothel_Inside/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > {n}The smug grin vanishes from the incubus's face. Glancing angrily at you and frowning, he turns away.{/n}
- `173815663ca7eb947a36d21271b2a20b` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C3/Arueshalae_Jailed/Answer_0041.jbp`
  > "I've killed."
- `fd5df8d65288741418dbd834aae89605` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C3/Arueshalae_Jailed/Answer_0054.jbp`
  > "I'll leave you alone. Actually, it would be better for you to stay locked in this cell until you learn to curb these inappropriate desires of yours."
- `6ae862e910dff59499916f2fd5975ba1` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C3/Arueshalae_Jailed/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > "This is where I belong. You shouldn't be alone with me. Please leave, I'm begging you! It's dangerous. I... I'm dangerous."
- `fe30c4aa7547e9b4aa3129f27a439265` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/KTC_Arusha/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > "No. It remains a mystery to me. But I had another idea. Something... Something I can't do alone."
- `1b7662aa199e0734297f87a2d44972a6` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/Nightmares/Answer_0057.jbp`
  > "Are you the priestess who Arueshalae buried near the Greengates?"
- `49b658d7d3cc4d346993a6748949fd75` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/Nightmares/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "We don't care about you — we are dead, we're not even here... Oh, I tell a lie — we are not part of reality anymore, but now we're right here, with you." {n}The shadow points his finger at the sleeping girl's temple, as if taking aim with a crossbow.{/n} "And this is the place we'll never disappear from."
- `faa27d25716440042af9260d1d6a287b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/Nightmares/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > {n}A shadow emerges from the crowd. It looks somehow different from the others. It is a young woman in a loose robe with the sacred symbol of a butterfly on her chest. She looks dark, not like the darkness of despair, but like the night sky. You think back to when Arueshalae told you her story, you were both standing next to a grave. This must be the priestess whose death prompted Arueshalae's redemption.{/n}
- `646be27961e928b4c9c2148fdc86ff9c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/Nightmares/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > {n}The shadows step aside and vanish into the darkness. The dream dissipates. The last thing you hear is the priestess's voice saying,{/n} "Take care of her."
- `7c4ab28580f7b80428b8d72f09c5dcdf` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/Nightmares/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > {n}The congregation of shadows begins to move. Maimed bodies and despair-stricken faces surround you. From every side, you hear a choir of voices listing Arueshalae's crimes.{/n} "She broke my legs and left me to die of thirst... Made me watch her kill my friends... Desecrated the temple of my deity with my intestines..."
- `1906ffea415c92a4d8fcf7a30ed9d366` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Arueshalae/Main_C4/Nightmares/Cue_0056.jbp`
  > "After our encounter, after Arueshalae killed me and then realized how much evil she had wrought throughout her life, she fled to a desolated place. She hoped that being far from humans would help her avoid temptation. But all isolation can do is help her unlearn her evil ways. Who, if not humans, can help her learn kindness and love?"
- `4a2b8019af264efba855cde3f3f1a08e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC6_ADanceOfMasks/KenabresFestival/Arueshalae_Event/Arueshalae_Event_Cosplayers/Cue_2.jbp`
  > "And she's not alone! Greetings, Commander!" {n}Another young woman dressed as Arueshalae gives you a curtsy.{/n}
- `b5f6f5fa8356426a996a1431aea34ae7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC6_ADanceOfMasks/KenabresFestival/Arueshalae_Event/Arueshalae_Event_Cosplayers/Cue_7.jbp`
  > "Interrupt?" {n}An old man laughs good-naturedly at Arueshalae's question.{/n} "You certainly did! Your costumes have left us speechless! I don't even remember what we were talking about before you arrived."
- `b70e6293f34a1544c94cc4e02be5d36f` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0452.jbp`
  > …ae would often descend to the Material Plane, where, as an unseen angel, she would keep watch over mortals, helping those who had gone astray to find redemption.…
- `6600a27a73da42da908247f71fc1b0b9` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_37_EEArushaAndAivu.jbp`
  > One day, Arueshalae had an unexpected guest: Aivu, the havoc dragon. By the end of the visit, all the pretzels had disappeared from the house, and all the apples were gone from the nearby trees.
- `22f1b7ac20a43b747a0be6c7bb30a0ee` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0076.jbp`
  > "What else could I desire, when I have my Beth? Hmm..." {n}Anevia thinks for a moment, smiling, then looks over your shoulder and gives a cheeky wink.{/n} "Arueshalae, quit hiding. You put the Commander up to this, didn't you? Feeling shy, are we? Fine, I'll act like I never saw you. So, Commander..."
- `4bd47d6746cef99468e225b1c14223e8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/Arusha_DrezenPrison/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae joins in, and your voices sing in unison. You see something you've never seen on a demon's face before — pure, radiant joy.{/n}
- `cc0d84a2bb8afb64e8cdd88c51e19dab` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/BookEvent_ArushalaeCalls/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > The sacred words disappear into the void like stones down a well. It's unlikely they reached the ears of any deity.
- `3b7fd279bc1bd8e4fbab65b6d000c988` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_End/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "I didn't even know who she was... I seduced her, deceived her, and killed her — and she saved my soul." {n}Arueshalae wipes her eyes.{/n} "If only I could die and give her back her life... But I can't, {name}. All I can do is live on and help those who are still alive."
- `a5b751acac4d62a4783dbd725750ab70` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_End/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > {n}With a parting nod, Arueshalae disappears into the shadows.{/n}
- `b3f4aa6a097211a4f8fa072a20b68349` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae brings a hand to her mouth, blushing.{/n} "Don't joke like that! I'm a succubus. My beauty spells death for mortals."
- `5d1f21dacecfabf4791699a15aaea28c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae bows her head.{/n} "As you wish." {n}In the blink of an eye, she vanishes into the shadows.{/n}
- `9ae1173739f22b34393158b07ab184e8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0050.jbp`
  > "May Desna aid us." {n}Arueshalae vanishes into the shadows.{/n}
- `b8eab527524cbd3418bc72ea877d3487` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > "There you are." {n}Arueshalae waves at you from the crumbling fortress wall.{/n} "I'm sorry I left the city so suddenly, but I couldn't miss the chance to catch the old flesh-eater!"
- `91963f2fcfa647a47bdfc80cf084301b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0062.jbp`
  > "Do you really think so?" {n}Arueshalae looks you dead in the eye. She cannot withstand your scrutiny and is the first to look away.{/n}
- `14020bb3c22950f42890a7bfedaf0ad4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae gives you a long, mournful look.{/n} "I'll try not to let that happen." {n}In the blink of an eye, she vanishes into the shadows.{/n}
- `9594caec101655a43862e49dbb004c92` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Arusha_Start/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > …ed to the enemy, you found Arueshalae in those same dungeons, imprisoned on Staunton's orders. You wanted to execute her, but she vanished in a flurry of Desna's butterflies.{/n}…
- `769cb3f5284c5f743a82d7b92b708d13` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Hag/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > {n}With her hand now drenched in the hag's blood, Arueshalae reaches into her bag and pulls out a map, making a scarlet mark on it.{/n} "You'll find the source of the demons' power here, in the Ivory Sanctum. And now — goodbye." {n}She tosses you the map, takes a step sideways, and vanishes into the shadows like she was never there.{/n}
- `637ae3df32c42254193b7fa7b2134248` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Hag/Cue_0068.jbp`
  > {n}The hag disappears in a burst of light. Arueshalae stomps her foot in frustration.{/n}
- `9b428fb41d3d5e54f8f6dbe105b11a8a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/ArueshalaeRedoubt/Fortress_Hag/Cue_0099.jbp`
  > "I am a monster and I have earned death a thousand times over. But I cannot die now. First I have to make amends for everything I've done." {n}Arueshalae reaches into her bag and pulls out a map, making a scarlet mark on it. She tosses the map to you.{/n} "You'll find the source of the demons' power here, in the Ivory Sanctum. And now — goodbye."
- `ab733a151034be740aa5f3b8fa2145b1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/BattleBliss/HiringSuccubs_dialogue/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae looks around the house, and shakes her head.{/n} "A night with a succubus, and it ends in death... A typical love story in the Abyss."
- `9a498089bb3e15b468b1cd7cf1f555f2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/BattleBliss/Irmangaleth_dialogue/Cue_0181.jbp`
  > …Battlebliss with an appearance in the arena? I wish you could see how good she was... She would toy with her opponents before she killed them. She would stretch out their suffering, making them writhe in the agony of a slow, excruciating death. Even those who were normally impervious to pain would beg her to put them out of their misery. She was the true embodiment of cruelty..."…
- `73addadd420358f4882efa18a9040c78` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/BattleBliss/WinThirdFight_dialogue/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae's whisper is barely audible.{/n} "Glory and death... That is the battle cry of doomed madmen. How many times have I heard it, how many times have I shouted it while standing in the arena... I run from what I have done. I run as fast as I can, but even the smallest thing can remind me of my past. Then it all comes flooding back, and I remember all the terrible things I've done."
- `a0828c81c14f8ec49a6570c93322cd12` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FinalDungeon_Colyphyr/Hepzamirah_main/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae's eyes flash with a dark, burning rage.{/n} "Who are you, girl, to lecture me about the Abyss! You are nothing more than a slab of meat yourself, a half-mortal perversion of your father! You're not even a real demon, so don't you dare—" {n}The succubus stops short, and the fire in her eyes disappears. She gasps, horrified.{/n} "Oh no... What am I saying..."
- `6febe35f936b88340a84e13ad87ee3d5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nexus_FinalOfChapter/CampSurvived/Cue_0103.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae flinches as if about to leap away from the boy.{/n} "The qlippoths? Enemies of demons since time immemorial! When they come, death reigns!" {n}The succubus looks at Xorges as if he were a venomous creature, but the fear in her eyes quickly gives way to shame.{/n} "Forgive me. It is not your fault. I... Most mortals view me the same way, as nothing but a monster. It was wrong of me to say that."
- `8bbdf78712d9e274bbb88343fe6bba11` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nexus_FinalOfChapter/XorgesAlone/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae flinches as if about to leap away from the boy.{/n} "The qlippoths? Enemies of demons since time immemorial! When they come, death reigns!" {n}The succubus looks at Xorges as if he were a venomous creature, but the fear in her eyes quickly gives way to shame.{/n} "Forgive me. It is not your fault. I... Most mortals view me the same way, as nothing but a monster. It was wrong of me to say that."
- `beef14d2b9d9bff4fb87ca62e9f715a6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Azata/AgainstMentor/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae stretches and spreads her wings.{/n} "The devil is gone, and we are free from his pernicious influence! And I feel so... so... I feel like flying!"
