- `a4fce26b17ae5a346982e8a8b71b4cc5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/TheDarkestRitual/Cue_0069.jbp`
  > {n}Arueshalae's paralyzed body is still and unmoving as you strike the final blow. The succubus's ruby eyes are fixed on you with an expression of infinite sadness until death finally clouds her gaze... forever.{/n}
- `8c5622f8b41752f45b4010c283db6df4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/CompanionFarewells/ArueshalaeFarewell/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "I did not do it alone." {n}Arueshalae smiles.{/n} "You have led your army to victory, and me to my dream."

## camellia

Authored return/rescue predicates: `camellia.trickster.returned`.

- `f5d9b8bd0fa6b434eb78bf860fa48436` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Answer_0069.jbp`
  > "Your amulet is fascinating. I've never seen anything like it."
- `9e54ae07e3066e94db17415271aa710a` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Answer_0262.jbp`
  > "I want to spend some time alone with you."
- `15e78a780a1b7eb4d9e6d7b9fe8dfcda` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > "Greetings." {n}The neat smile on Camellia's lips disappears so quickly you almost wonder if you imagined it.{/n}
- `51e1f89df27b4c84aa3ae97fd861b4f7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > "I won't keep you." {n}The dawning smile on Camellia's face freezes and quickly dies away.{/n}
- `c14e6645d8993174d97139bd432c1fa0` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > "With pleasure! Friends share everything! What do you want to know? How many people I've killed? Which organ is the most delicious? I'll tell you everything!"
- `27f1b185659f6544fa4bf962dea3fd5f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0071.jbp`
  > "Yes, it's the only thing I have left of my dearly departed half-elf mother. Father told me that she liked working in the garden so much that she even gave her child the name of a flower." {n}She shrugs vaguely.{/n}
- `3c75f9fe22b50874eb910521a850ba36` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0077.jbp`
  > …hese lands for many years. The Worldwound keeps growing bigger and bigger... It's no surprise that the spirits of this place have gone mad and endlessly thirst for more and more blood. Only when bathed in blood can they regain their senses, at least temporarily."…
- `ed1bda17f292cd74e8af9401976bcc15` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0097.jbp`
  > "She was a vicious crone and a nasty piece of work. She would hit me with a whip just for looking at her. She would yell at me when I failed to communicate with the spirit I summoned. And when Father left for a few weeks on some errands, she locked me in the basement at night. Now I see that I terrified the old woman. Well, it was her fault. She should have taken the money and run while she had the chance."
- `7e6a90196fdf808498d5023ed19f4b89` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0105.jbp`
  > "I wonder if I should be grateful to her. I don't know. I'm glad I killed her. I would have even if the spirits hadn't asked me for her blood."
- `f8be62c9aba2add4bb0107c478fc7926` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_0255.jbp`
  > {n}Cold contempt, fury, shame, and interest all flicker in Camellia's eyes. She quickly looks down.{/n} "I'm in no mood to talk. If you would be so kind, I should like to be left alone."
- `56e358fbad4647a7832e772857e95219` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Camelia/Cue_4.jbp`
  > "How kind of you to share that you've gone through the Gwerms' dirty laundry and drawn some bizarre conclusions. If you value my company, please refrain from making such insinuations in the future."
- `5f6741fa4b8eb764882881c23baf4883` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Answer_0025.jbp`
  > "This isn't your first or even your second victim, is it? How many have you killed?"
- `0d1635ff8dc9ebd4992b3a4a19e28d5d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > {n}Hearing steps behind her, Camellia whips around — and freezes. She glances at the bloodied dagger in her hand, then at the dead body at her feet.{/n} "{name}. How lovely to see you here," {n}she says evenly. Her lips quiver for a fraction of a second before settling into an unimpeachably polite smile.{/n}
- `955d91f4fd1053842aac0844fc63256a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia gives an indifferent shrug, but contempt flashes in her eyes.{/n} "That is your right. Just as a mole has the right not to believe in the existence of the sun. A butterfly has the right not to believe in summer. An infant has the right not to believe in death. I have revealed the truth to you, you are free to reject it if you wish."
- `f9127fc6f8f99d940b3dbeaec466b6e9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia tilts her head to one side, as though listening for something.{/n} "I do not think. I know. I sense things many people cannot, I can infer what the spirits want. For example, there is one spirit hanging over your left shoulder as we speak. It came to enjoy Mireya's victim. Can you sense its hunger?" {n}Camellia laughs airily.{/n}
- `e41e5c9a15d6797418737579a4712063` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0042.jbp`
  > "We're fighting against demons. It is a just cause. But imagine we win and the demons vanish for good: do you think the {g|Worldwound}Worldwound{/g} will vanish along with them? That it's something we can defeat with weapons? That it won't burst open again at some point in the future, like an old, festering abscess?"
- `58c2eece7db8a0c46bc32fecc85c6c67` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0057.jbp`
  > "Have you ever watched a heart stop in an open chest — and known that you were the cause? Have you ever held someone on the very brink of death? When your victim's strength is fading with every second, before it fades utterly, and the only evidence of your duel will be the bruise on your wrist left by his failing grip...?" {n}Camellia studies her blood-stained hands. A shaky sigh emerges from her chest.{/n}
- `af252c0a8c2e658498c66e93e665273d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's face breaks into a radiant smile. You realize that you have never seen her this happy before.{/n} "Thank you, Commander. I am in your debt."
- `303e73a5c7082864abb43f97769ce9ec` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0076.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's voice drops to a murmur.{/n} "I need to take myself in hand. It's a pity this fellow died so swiftly. Damn it all, I need sex. I won't sleep tonight without a man..."
- `7bfe6d21c0e6b6341ba4399bfd8f50e9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/CamelliaRedHanded/Cue_0080.jbp`
  > {n}You know that look. It holds no fear, no shame, no doubt — only pure exultation in the sacrament of pain and death. You have seen this look in the eyes of other followers of Zon-Kuthon. Perhaps someone has seen it in your eyes, too. Perhaps it was the last thing they saw.{/n}
- `d160f1730bbc9ea4d9d7bc4da8f2f8d3` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/FindMurderer/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "But I never gave up on the investigation, even when everybody else stopped digging. I know we're at war and that people are dying left and right, but that's no reason to turn a blind eye to crime."
- `58689712febab974696b4044c30b54bc` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/FindMurderer/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > "So, that's it, Commander. Another crusader has just gone missing. He left the barracks to come here, he would have gone past the gates. My gut tells me that the killer won't keep him alive for long. So, Commander, you need to start searching this area right now and try to find out where he's been taken."
- `b46b7c41621d12a44a6fa631c8e91026` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/FindMurderer/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "Death often shows up when you least expect it. It's not like I picked the timing."
- `f09fb678f3ee6404cb72decf9457dbae` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q1_NobleIntent/KTC_CamelliaEscaped/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "I ran to find Horgus immediately, but he's gone too. His daughter clearly found time to warn him."
- `dd0aa2a998623ad4eb8d114af305178a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/DemonsLoveCommander/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > {n}The demon leers.{/n} "Are you counting down the seconds to your death?"
- `99b641a4bbe0a284b8dae9c4d71dcc86` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/HeartsDesire/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia recoils as if from a blow and sways on her feet. She grips her amulet in her left hand, her knuckles turning white.{/n} "This can't be! It can't be you! Why? How did this happen? Could Herrax have lied to me...? No, why would she? Is this some kind of joke? But what if it isn't? What if this is... what my heart desires?"
- `7ff10ad504c1b6946a5d4e05894a6e8b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/HeartsDesire/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia wets her suddenly dry lips. Her left hand grips the bone amulet tightly. A sharpened dagger gleams dully in her right hand.{/n} "Watch closely, my friend. I want you to see. I don't want you to miss even the smallest detail. I shall take my time so you may better observe."
- `b66c525e5ee6d35428e9dea354a185cd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/KTC_LetsGoBrothel/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "My friend. It has been so long since we last spent time together. Wouldn't you agree? We haven't even gone for a stroll. I have missed your company." {n}Camellia's eyes have an unnatural sheen to them and her shallow breaths emerge from her throat in a feverish staccato.{/n}
- `0f56bddb022a2254eb5d68819061aca3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/KTC_LetsGoBrothel/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > "You allowed me to feed Mireya in Drezen. So why stop now? We're not even in your lands any longer. What difference does the death of one unknown pleasure slave make, when the fate of all Golarion might depend on that death?"
- `818bc19df1c13b6478733668f6fe96e3` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/KTC_LetsGoBrothel/Cue_0024.jbp`
  > "You forbade me from killing your subjects in Drezen, but here in the Abyss it's an entirely different matter. We're not even in your lands any longer. What difference does the death of one unknown pleasure slave make, when the fate of all Golarion might depend on that death?"
- `4f7b9b4b48d8a3145a341f8f8e4fa439` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/AfterHorgus/Answer_0014.jbp`
  > [Attack] "You're insane! I should have killed you long ago!"
- `ee95baad0b1b4ec45a1152a0621e0613` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/AfterHorgus/Answer_0017.jbp`
  > "Camellia, stop. You've killed enough."
- `d4c7625cba8a934498e765ca78aafc59` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/FinalTruth/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > "Ah, that's a good question. You would have learned the truth sooner or later, you know. I've told you too many stories about Mireya, you never would have let the matter lie. Of course, I could have left — fled in the dead of night, leaving you a farewell note. But..."
- `2991d6281d5ab2f4fa5732839cc5f838` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/FinalTruth/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > "This?" {n}Camellia fiddles with the bone snake in her hands.{/n} "Once, a snake crawled into our garden. I killed it, gutted it, dried out its bones. And then I told Horgus about Mireya, and asked him to decorate the amulet with gold and make it into a necklace."
- `dcc40583a0059da4b8f45ce891f71f78` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Answer_0011.jbp`
  > "What did you do after I left for the Abyss?"
- `fd63349c65f3d5a4db963425ca03dcef` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > …r.{/n} "Hello, Camellia. So, you finally came, and you even dragged the Commander along — I suspected that you wouldn't come here alone. You know, I've been wondering when you would finally show your face here. Haven't I always taught you that it is extremely impolite to be late for an appointment?"…
- `5a871c0fefd96a74ca748701ec85e207` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "I left Drezen as soon as the Queen took hold of the reins of government. Of course, I highly respect our dear Galfrey, but it was you I had followed into the crusade, not her. For whatever reason, I do not believe our Queen will succeed in her attempts to defeat the Wound. So, I moved to Nerosyan while my mansion in Kenabres was under renovation..."
- `8658b3aa9b63eec46acc8cac80fba2bf` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > …want so much blood?" {n}Horgus takes a huge gulp from his goblet and immediately refills it again.{/n} "You do know that Camellia killed five people while she lived under this roof, don't you? First, her mentor, an old Varisian woman. Then four more. Well, they were thugs, bandits, and murderers — they had it coming, to tell the truth. They broke into this mansion seeking to rob us. That's Kenabres for you — a border…
- `04df39b0ab783644fa79d01cde4d4632` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > …de short shrift of such rabble. Hic—" {n}Horgus belches noisily.{/n} "But the things Camellia did to them down in the cellar... I never saw it myself. I only heard it. And then later, when I buried their corpses, I... I felt relieved that I was not in their place. Ha. Hahaha."…
- `50d69a0cf638f56459a46afeea9b97e3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > …d his bloodline live on. I have had to lie and cover up crimes along the way. Well, the time has come to pay the piper, and if my death helps to uncover the secret of closing the Worldwound — well, it's not the worst way to go." {n}Horgus gulps.{/n} "But before you do what you came here to do, Camellia, let me reveal my final secret to you."…
- `1685d568e2a3e2e41b0893bed28af2f5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > "Your mother, Iris, genuinely loved you, Camellia. But after she saw how you killed that pup in the garden, she started to worry about you. It was she who insisted on the doctors and the psychics and the exorcists. They all examined you, but nothing helped. You kept on killing animals — neighboring cats, stray dogs... You told us stories about the spirits who whispered in your ear. Iris didn't believe you really had …
- `a06146ac355d57542ad16bdd956a80be` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > … pillow in her hands. I pushed her away from you. It was dark, Iris stumbled and she hit her head on the corner of a cabinet... I buried her in the garden, and planted a flowerbed of camellias over her grave. I thanked the gods that you remembered nothing of that horrible night. Now you know everything, Camellia."…
- `582ffa6868af6394d8bc9917cd482dc1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0035.jbp`
  > "You... You killed my mother? And you decided to tell me that now?" {n}Camellia's whisper sounds like a hissing of a venomous snake.{/n} "Why? To unburden your soul? To show me how you protected me back then? Maybe you're hoping I'll hesitate before I stab you in your disgusting heart?"
- `24353fd8d3a2e544489e1b01ef383367` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}Horgus lowers his head.{/n} "I'm not looking for excuses. I have long been ready to accept my fate. The bloodline of Horgus Gwerm will not be broken with my death."
- `39de58134e690e54a99d1b6a0cef4d4d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > "Or are you trying to make me weep for my mother, the innocent victim? Well, I don't give a damn about her. The bitch wanted me dead and she failed — good riddance, I say."
- `50c5fb322d3ff9e4a9203866786eef5e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "But now... now I don't need you anymore. I have {name}, who will protect me and hide me from justice if it comes for me. As for you, you are just garbage left over from my former life. And it's time to dispose of you." {n}The stiletto trembles in Camellia's hand. She grips the handle so tight her knuckles turn white.{/n}
- `5e80349c90a451c4a835f392072fa75f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/KTC_ReturnToManor/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > "Because it is what I want, understand? My desire alone must be enough for you—" {n}Camellia stops. A shadow of fear crosses her face, but she immediately hides it behind a modest smile.{/n} "It seems I lost control over myself. I apologize. Over the past few days, I have expended great effort trying to summon Mireya and make her talk. The exertion... it has taken its toll on me."
- `d72f9923ab0f6cc4aa2aac3238c71e59` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/CamelliaRoom/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia turns to the windows and looks at them closely, as if seeing them for the first time. Then she shrugs.{/n} "They were always here. I moved to this room immediately after my mother died and this is where I stayed, leaving it only on rare occasions."
- `362bd2804e3ef104497d2fcace9e55a5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/DogGrave/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "This is the grave of my pup. I dug it myself and buried the poor animal." {n}Camellia chuckles and turns to you.{/n}
- `0c5f8ae938c5ddd4fa8b4303d01d4e53` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/FlowerBed/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "This is the grave of my mother, Iris. She died when I was a child, so I scarcely remember her. I don't understand — why would Horgus tend this grave all these years — and why would he plant camellias over it? My father has never shown any tendency toward sentimentality."
- `2456b7df7a7da344d92bf6b0e4b10272` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/MeAndFather/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > "Hmm. So that's what you think..." {n}Camellia's gaze wanders the room, never resting on anything in particular.{/n}
- `c6dd71eb46b384b4b9d3548108823eda` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Answer_0005.jbp`
  > "So how many skeletons are buried in this cellar?"
- `bf953f1e40d534a468387c03b7549381` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Answer_0006.jbp`
  > [Sense the dead bodies hidden underground] "Let's see if you are telling the truth."
- `7d724f046c1f9134c912753bd03b9b19` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > "This place holds special significance for me. Because this is where I killed those thugs who tried to rob Horgus's mansion. They were caught by the guards, and I sacrificed them to Mireya. This is where they are buried."
- `a8e41c3e3e6030d4eaca05483ba0b4cc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's gaze wanders without stopping on anything in particular. It seems she is remembering — reliving — all the sacrifices she made here.{/n}
- `79740addc60e7504596f88cd0aa5c1cc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia arches her eyebrow.{/n} "What hard evidence do you have to support your doubts? However, if you wish to make sure that I am telling the truth — feel free to take a shovel and dig. But do not expect me to sit here obediently waiting for you to finish. And I must warn you in advance — Horgus buried all the victims as deep as he could."
- `6e3a7df06a3f8d14aba0feaa1f7d707a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > {n}At first, all you sense is emptiness, but within moments you detect the presence of death here. The skulls and bones buried here have long lost their connection to their souls, which were summoned to Pharasma. The remains are buried very deep — Camellia was telling the truth. As you focus on your senses, you realize that there are five or six different skeletons hidden here.{/n}
- `29e82e8d5fd158349af4bea0e583a96a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "Nevertheless, I can assure you that all my victims buried here were hardened criminals and thugs. No one shed a tear when they never returned from the Gwerm mansion that night."
- `66db834db536b7f4187f410c72c60ca6` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/TortureCellar/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > "I suppose he could have killed me and buried me right here in the cellar..." {n}She shrugs hesitantly.{/n} "I have no idea why he didn't do it while I was a little girl and he could overpower me."
- `51cb28b916179db45aadae5d4a591b06` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/MiscObjects/VarisianTeacher/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia shrugs.{/n} "She moved into this house a few years after my mother's death. Horgus often had to go out of Kenabres on business, so he gave me into the care of that old shaman, hoping that she would teach me to keep the spirits on a short leash. That old hag must have known what she was doing was illegal. She was very good at hiding the evidence of her 'teaching' — she always made sure to heal my scars by …
- `911a2f428089c064ca334ac00fb186c9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/BalconyAwaits/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > {n}The wine is dry and tart. It leaves a strange bitter aftertaste on your tongue. While you're trying to figure out what this means, Camellia drains her glass and vanishes into thin air.{/n}
- `46d82c20f941bb847b325e368f7ad977` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/BalconySex/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}You embrace Camellia even more tightly, until there is no space left between you.{/n} "Yes. Like that. I knew you'd enjoy this." {n}Camellia's voice sounds breathy.{/n}
- `32e76385f9fbdc740a4765e7c20edbf3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/GiveMeAnIdea/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia looks uneasily back at you.{/n} "I think you have taken my question altogether too literally, my friend. I am not opposed to some danger, but I do not wish to be killed. Anyway, thank you for sharing your opinion."
- `f8158f5d3284dcd4882fbdd968548218` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/ImNotNormal/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}She smiles warmly and sincerely, and all the tension you felt in the air vanishes instantly.{/n} "That was a stupid date. A mistake that almost ruined our relationship. But it also gave me new sensations, things I'd never felt before. I was angry and anxious. At first, I wanted to kill you, then I wanted to embrace you and feel you with my entire body. It was... indescribable."
- `2033b37ae6a200d4eb2d3293041775f3` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/ImNotNormal/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > "You understand me better than anyone else. Sometimes even better than I understand myself. I first realized it back in Alushinyrra, when we left the city and were heading to Colyphyr."
- `71d9c81b6fe194345a2b80fb4f9dba49` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/NormalDate/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "You think so? Thank you, I'm flattered." {n}Camellia smiles warmly, but her smile vanishes in a moment.{/n}
- `8056e6b69f3ddce4fbe212483f050b8d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/NormalDate/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > {n}Her eyes are like shards of ice. Even her usual half-smile has vanished from her face.{/n} "Is that it? I didn't expect you to give up so quickly." {n}Camellia tosses a glance at the bed.{/n} "Or perhaps you would like to make up for your pathetic attempt at conversation with a masterful performance in bed?"
- `d7a70988979a9bb4080590812fdc19b3` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/TempleSexWhy/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "What nonsense! If I had wanted to kill you, you would be dead. Trust me, I know where to strike to take a life, as well as all the places that can be punctured almost safely. Your wound belongs to the latter category. I bet it doesn't even hurt anymore."
- `a0d62d4761af0264a8221981a31aa383` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/TempleSexWhy/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > "You see, this step... entering a relationship with you — this is very serious for me. It's been so long since I have wanted to be in a relationship with anyone, let alone make plans for a future together. I... I need to think about it."
- `1dd8cb5d78b2f4848a349afda7824fd9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Camellia/TempleSexWhy/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia puts her palm on your chest.{/n} "Please. This is very important for me. I need time to figure it all out, to rediscover myself. And I must do it alone."
- `3c192e86656144dbbf5c8e484f621627` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/SoanaMain/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia licks her parched lips.{/n} "Would you allow me a moment alone with her? For old times' sake."
- `84236579c0278d940b9f1a9a2b9e7a47` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Horgus/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > …arden. I always wanted to join the Gwerm name with another noble family. The Gwerm legacy must not be mixed with common blood and vanish into obscurity. Iris didn't mind, and we managed to hide our relationship. When Camellia was born, I did not claim her as my own. To all of Mendev, Camellia is the daughter of a lowly servant who died over a decade ago."…
- `735b2c66ed87ab0439d4fc955b09cc4d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Horgus/Cue_0060.jbp`
  > "I heard that you... That Camellia is gone." {n}Horgus stands with his head lowered. His shoulders tremble slightly, but his voice is firm.{/n} "I... I understand, and I don't blame you. Camellia's been walking on a knife's edge for a long time, she has no one to blame but herself! But tonight... tonight I shall get tremendously drunk. Please leave me, Knight Commander."
- `dead79964a65e1044bd78c2170f3f8b8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/HorgusManor/HorgusSecret/Cue_0058.jbp`
  > … Camellia was born, I did not claim her as my own. As far as Mendev knows, Camellia is the daughter of a Gwerm family servant who died over ten years ago."…
- `057486ff8eb19084cafb129685ff1514` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/HorgusManor/HorgusSecret/Cue_0099.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's whisper is barely audible.{/n} "Keeping secrets is even easier if you're dead."
- `d3b32c77e5804ad45accbd8bee935c85` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/LostChapel/Boss_End/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's cheeks grow red and her eyes are gleaming.{/n} "What do you feel, {name}? Power? New abilities? All of this is so... exciting!" {n}She lazily kicks the body of the dead nabasu.{/n}
- `37f6c26e8763a324ebaf628136b9b618` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/BattleBliss/WinThirdFight_dialogue/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "I killed. I maimed. I tormented bodies and souls. I plunged them into the darkness of despair. And then I did it again and again..." {n}The succubus speaks in a dull voice, and her eyes are filled with unshed tears. She doesn't notice the mysterious smile that flickers on Camellia's lips.{/n}
- `b46cb73eb6414674d98ca2b842a44258` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FleshMarket/HologramSlaver/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > {n}Sosiel nods encouragingly at Camellia.{/n} "Facing such evil can shock even a seasoned veteran, let alone a sheltered young woman such as yourself. But I promise that we will get out of this terrible place and return home. All of us."
- `c7b7650b256e0e34f88216f2cdd5eda7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nexus_Camp/NexusMines/DyingSuccubus/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's eyes are following the dying succubus intently. Noticing your attention on her, Camellia hastily turns away.{/n} "I just wanted to make sure that her death wasn't simply an elaborate ploy or yet another trap."
- `c5e964bfd52014e4aa8dae9587b3f6b7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nexus_FinalOfChapter/Companions_NoCamp/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia shrugs indifferently.{/n} "This miserable place? I immediately thought it was a wasted effort. These creatures lacked true spirit and knew nothing of survival. In the end, they merely delayed their deaths a few months. And still died."
- `4e1bef2c0da0b7c4799422ac130037a3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Portal_ToColyphyr/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "We... we're leaving Alushinyrra, aren't we?" {n}Camellia addresses you but it feels as if she were speaking to herself.{/n} "I have this odd feeling that we won't return here in the foreseeable future."
- `8c9a58ca27fd07d47829d08e69c575ae` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Herraxa_dialogue/Cue_0275.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia sighs and casts a sidelong look at the smiling Herrax.{/n} "Yes, I have, several times. I visited while our party was resting. I killed several pleasure slaves. But their deaths gave neither {g|Mireya}Mireya{/g} nor me any real relief. So I thought it would be good for me to come here with you — if I perform the ritual while you're watching, I hope that Mireya will accept the sacrifice."
- `a11301d12fc94bd4f81167ab4b8b1fbd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Herraxa_dialogue/Cue_0288.jbp`
  > "It's so heartwarming to know that true chivalry is not yet dead." {n}Camellia smiles approvingly.{/n} "You have my sincerest gratitude, Commander."
- `2532e51409409af44bb056bc3362bb2a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/LabyrinthOfBaphometh/Svendack_PrisonBoss/Cue_0035.jbp`
  > "I, too, always look in the eyes of those whose lives I cut short." {n}Camellia's breathing suddenly grows heavy, she turns her clouded gaze on you.{/n} "We must remember all whose lives we take. Without that, death loses its significance. That's what my father taught me."
- `bc4ab33b42c654a4bbaf64353603c00e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/TheDarkestRitual/Cue_0061.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia's paralyzed body is still and unmoving as you strike the final blow. The noblewoman watches you in horror until death clouds her gaze... forever.{/n}

## devarra

Authored return/rescue predicates: `devarra.tower.flown`, `devarra.trickster.returned`.

- `d368680393304b5ba324dd5a517140ed` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/StorytellersTower/Chaleb/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > "I am Devarra, bane of the Worldwound. And because of you, my clutch was pillaged, and my offspring died before their birth. Do not think I have forgotten." {n}She lets out a vengeful hiss.{/n}
- `8273aba6f8ab4c328661380272cee583` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/StorytellersTower/Chaleb/Cue_8.jbp`

  > {n}Eliandra wraps her arms around herself sadly.{/n} "Katair, my faithful guard! How can I keep myself from thinking about what you will have to go through, imprisoned by that monster? And I cannot help you — the care of our community and temple now falls to me alone."
- `e4c8d64e5f1895f4e9437f08c0352745` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Pulura_C3/Pulura_C3_main/RangerAlarm/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > … yet she's not fully on this plane either. However, there is one thing I can say with certainty — the creator of the sword is not dead. Her heart still beats."…
- `345a42439460a674680dcc2b8d6d38f7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Pulura_C3/Pulura_C3_main/Ranger_main/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > {n}Katair hesitates.{/n} "Eliandra won't like it if I take one of her stargazers. But... if he really killed an undead... he must have been training in secret. He is clearly determined to get his way. Such persistence should be encouraged. All right, so be it! He may join my unit, and go on a raid."
- `1ff9c2f36e7a2a94d894bed83a743bbd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Pulura_C3/Pulura_C3_side/WrittenInTheStars/Cristry/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "All right. I can't make the decision alone. If you don't want Eliandra to know, I won't go against your wishes. I would never do that. However, I'm sorry the Commander has encouraged your fears."
- `d7aa68e5d9d3d62448495373aaa376cd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Pulura_C3/Pulura_C3_side/WrittenInTheStars/CristryVestari/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "Good?! These rules aren't about what's good for the temple, they're about Eliandra's stupid fears! And what's left for us? We just have to bow and accept them? It's all so unfair... "
- `f89fdfdfcef564c489f5a2f46190a825` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > Eliandra, the high priestess of Pulura, steps forward. "I am sorry. But I must protect Pulura's stargazers... and what is left of Sarkoris. This is what my goddess has called me to do. When the power of Heaven flowed through all of us, we had a common purpose — we could have changed the course of this war. But now, we are simply allies... and we must all return to our former lives."
- `c1662db6b8446ee4285b7bac773a5d3d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > Yaniel looks tired and frail. It seems as though her age has finally caught up with her. "I don't have much choice. Your power was the only thing keeping me going... Now that it is gone, I feel the weight of my years like a proverbial ton of bricks. My fighting days are over. It's time for old Yaniel to retreat to the rear."
- `b17c8b226d147af48b7c06a1cbb52573` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > It is Targona who answers. "We felt the power of Heaven leave you! We felt... your death. The goddess told us everything — she told us what you had become. We are currently on a mission, but we had to see for ourselves... It is one thing to hear the truth, and another to see it with your own eyes. We trusted you, {name}! We believed in you. But now you are our enemy. If our paths cross again, we will have no choice —…
- `1f43bd32e0cc43649baea721cab28ee9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0037.jbp`
  > One by one, the Commander's former allies say their words of gratitude. Then the vision fades. Anyone who walks along a simple, straight path will have many traveling companions. But if they stray from the road, and follow a new, unknown path, few will choose to accompany them. Those who are not content with simple solutions, and wish to forge a path of their own... will often have to do so alone.
- `d1592cf40725dcb4086f63bb8d3d8fa8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/Mythic_Angel/LetsGoAbyss/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > {n}Tears shine in Eliandra's eyes as she offers up reverent prayers of thanksgiving.{/n} "The Worldwound is no more. It is gone! Oh, my beloved land, my fallen Sarkoris, you are free at last. There will be no more bloodshed... no more corruption."

I might just be a naive fool from the caves, but I don't understand how it's possible to have so much more and share so much less."…

- `5a871c0fefd96a74ca748701ec85e207` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q3_TheFinalDrop/HorgusRevealsTruth/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "I left Drezen as soon as the Queen took hold of the reins of government. Of course, I highly respect our dear Galfrey, but it was you I had followed into the crusade, not her. - `b17b4b9e823730143b1a0c56b58592a2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Galfrey/GalfreyAfterSex/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > {n}The Queen stays silent for a while.{/n} "I cannot lay claim to vast experience in such matters, but it seems to me that it would be best if we simply parted ways without talking — ending this connection with the subtle sense of words left unspoken. Do you see what I'm trying to say? Actually, never mind."
- `12ab9673be8cf16459963b8967682667` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Galfrey/GalfreyDrink/Cue_0034.jbp`
  > "I remember waiting for the night he would be stationed in the palace gardens. I remember my cheeks burning as I confessed my love to him. I remember our first kiss." {n}She sighs.{/n} "And then my father died, I became queen, and my knight chose to abandon his position and leave. He told me that he did not wish to die young in some senseless war."
- `94f8855ffba04dcca431883bd322a1e2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/GesmerhaMain/Cue_0004.jbp`

- `97a5e45a76524da6932a4a20d2919d5b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Ziggurat/ZombieGalfrey/Cue_21.jbp`
  > {n}Your minion, the ghost of Galfrey, looks at her counterpart with scorn.{/n} "You blame Commander for your woes? Not the demons, not your feeble servants, not the indifferent gods? They are all guilty!" {n}The Queen's final words rise to a mournful wail.{/n}
- `0156fe954b4a4a2e9318eecd0d6732d5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Storyteller/Cue_13.jbp`

Or the tale of King Daeran, dubbed the Lord of Vice, who ascended the throne after Queen …
- `e5335568aa624727a7f58189b083b7ba` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC2_LowFantasy/DLC2_Common/Abad_Personal/Cue_13.jbp`
  > "More than anything in the world. It is precisely at this moment, when all this outrage is happening in the city, that we, its defenders, must keep a clear head. The city lives as long as we continue to do our respective duties, each in our own field. To abandon them is to capitulate, to betray the {g|Crusades}crusades{/g}, to renounce the gods and the {g|Galfrey}Queen{/g}, to perish!"
- `09f1fad5e9644269bd88a3ea90beb56e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC2_LowFantasy/Map1_Theatre/Abad/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > "You think a blind old woman will be dead weight, don't you? You do realize that leaving helpless people in harm's way is a crime? You may do as you wish, but remember: when the {g|Galfrey}Queen{/g} arrives in the city, everyone will get their dues. There will be no hiding from it!"
- `dd519146e0e7f6f43a68b1d9af343b0b` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0249.jbp`
  > Shortly after the war, Queen Galfrey abdicated the throne of Mendev and departed Nerosyan to spend the rest of her long life with her beloved — the one who had helped her see that there was more to life than duty.
- `04cccb13979ff9a498af47668433f894` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0251.jbp`
  > After the battle of Threshold, Queen Galfrey returned to Nerosyan, where she died of old age shortly thereafter, having refused the sun orchid elixir that was offered to her on her deathbed. Her last words were "My duty is done."

  > "I am well past my prime. I have seen far too much death to be afraid of dying myself, but I don't want to get old. However, if one has enough money, one can bribe time itself. Take Queen Galfrey: I wish I could afford the magic potion she has been using to stay young for over a century."

- `3ba7a9344e9029748baa1c342e8ea0ff` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Drezen_C3/KTC_Flamelance_Nystra/Cue_0001.jbp`

- `bb392a1548e100f40a1c6517b0c77c23` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Lich/Zacharius_FirstProjection/Cue_0049.jbp`

- `b77ea2cd37dd52947b0d06e36d52cff9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/FreeGraveknightStaunton_Fallen/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > "Death hasn't changed you one bit! You're still blaming someone else for all your troubles — Minagho, Queen Galfrey, Iomedae, fate, the weather, the position of the celestial bodies — literally anyone or anything but yourself."
- `f91a90b03c66f7149ab22e38cb50c79c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/GalfreyAfter/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Galfrey leans on her sword firmly. There is a pensive look in her eyes as the rush of combat dies away.{/n}
- `d9d009964b3f0e945a0d509dc56db853` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/GalfreyAfter/Cue_0063.jbp`
  > {n}Galfrey's face flushes, her eyes flaring with anger.{/n} "It was written in a moment of weakness. I was mourning my friend whom I had treated unfairly. But now that I see what you've become, I can tell that my grief was misplaced."
- `6d3caa0cc4e3f544b9fa925a4df15a1f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/GalfreyAfter/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > {n}Galfrey's cheeks redden.{/n} "It was written in a moment of weakness. I was mourning my friend whom I had treated unfairly. I never even thought you'd find that letter."
- `bb9fa4ae21d8437499fe6b38c1eaadae` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/GalfreyOnTheEdge/Cue_0075.jbp`
  > {n}Queen Galfrey accepts her death stoically. She grits her teeth, refusing to grant you the pleasure of hearing her scream.{/n}
- `23a5c8da33bb8c941b7e179149ca737a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Devil/AbrogailVisit/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > …mpossible to hide from a devil, no? And since you have already embarked on this path, how could Cheliax stay on the sidelines and abandon its ally? We wanted to see the devil commander in person and be certain the interests of Cheliax, Hell, and Drezen align. Given our august sister Galfrey did not hesitate to address you in person, there is no shame if we do the same."…
- `f554e52d1f28403583dab07abcf1c9c0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Devil/DevilishTemptation/AfterRaid/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "Curse it all! How could it... It wasn't supposed to go like this!" {n}Galfrey looks mournfully at the fallen soldiers.{/n}
- `fd8751fad94640988b3d0d9cd1ec681d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Devil/DevilishTemptation/GalfreyIsReady/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}Galfrey looks disdainfully at the young woman's body and coldly declares,{/n} "She was a traitor. Traitors do not deserve to be mourned."
- `3569871301914c269e162adc1899f9ea` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Devil/DevilishTemptation/KTC_MephistoOffer/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > "You will also have Galfrey under your control. Along with her crown, throne, death-dealing sword, and timeless beauty."
- `a95b2a352c1fb4942bea09a40818e9ec` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/BeatedCounselor/Cue_0043.jbp`
  > "Nonsense. If anyone has been trying to stage a coup, it is this demented hag. She began scheming the day you left Drezen, trying to convince your servants to follow the banner of the Whispering Way. She wanted to assassinate Queen Galfrey and start a rebellion. I couldn't sit back and watch her poison seep into everything you have built. I sprang into action, and I'm not ashamed to admit it."
- `aba97bb18cd07cc48a9075cbe691809f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/Galfrey_Zombie/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > {n}Galfrey greets you with a brisk military salute. Not even death could diminish this woman's majestic posture, and she still moves with the same graceful precision she had in life. Her face is cold and ruthless, and her crimson eyes gaze at you with grim indifference.{/n}
- `26b71f8a8b624886a4b1af673c9ccaac` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/Galfrey_Zombie/Cue_2.jbp`
  > {n}The Queen's right hand grips a weapon, while her left hand holds {d|c3 lexicon galfrey}half of the Lexicon of Paradox{/d}, which she now has no need for.{/n}
- `d8ff9cee8ea2b914eafd85b5a080a970` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/GalfreyThreshold/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > {n}Galfrey nods, smiling.{/n} "After all the things I have said, I cannot just let you go into the final battle all alone."
- `f8c7044ab3e521248aa79d256b0bad9d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/GalfreyThreshold/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > {n}A maelstrom of emotions sweeps over Galfrey's face. She pales, then blushes, bites her lip, then takes a resolute step forward and kisses you — eagerly, almost greedily, as if trying to make up for all those years of sustaining herself on duty alone. When she pulls back from you, you see tears glittering in her eyes.{/n}

- `6799a61d88c449cd8b967e49c74769b7` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_46.jbp`
  > Gesmerha was true to her word. The Wintersun clan left the land of their ancestors. Once they had left the borders of the former Sarkoris, the clan split into several branches. One went to the wildlands of Numeria, others set off for free Varisia. A small group led by Gesmerha chose to travel to Andoran, where they were lost among the many refugees fleeing other lands and other wars. The Wintersun clan ceased to be.
- `f6a14eb6e8164dc88c4d0eca33114d18` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_48.jbp`
  > The Wintersun clan left the land of their ancestors. After losing their chieftain, the people fled their village in a disorganized exodus, haphazardly, without direction. Many perished on their travels. Those who did reach safe lands tried to forget their past and the horrors they had suffered.

- `64e4cc4a4aafbb34baa9549fe8942b4a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Wintersun/JerribethReveal/Cue_0035.jbp`

- `9e403ac418478bf41841833b47d1eeda` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/KTC_WintersunHelp/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > {n}Gesmerha looks the {d|c3 gesmerha chief}same{/d} as before, but far more weary.{/n} "So it is true — the Commander has returned from the dead. - `9ff80f52d28a09e4fa64ea6155a31d29` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nexus_Camp/HeraldDoubt_MythicAngel/Cue_0025.jbp`

  > "No soul can escape from here, be it a prisoner or a slave who gave their life for me. All — from the most worthless cultist to {g|Hepzamirah}Hepzamirah{/g}, whom I killed in the mines of Colyphyr — remain here... For they are all mine."
- `0cdc1a24d29c77f4490900d3a9418afc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/LabyrinthOfBaphometh/Prison_Baph/Cue_0121.jbp`
  > "You speak of Hepzamirah as if you're hoping your words will wound me. You've killed one of my descendants and her specter now wanders these halls — am I supposed to care? I could spawn hundreds, thousands more. Among them, there will be those in whom my blood is stronger, and they will surpass Hepzamirah. Believe me, I possess none of a father's sentimentality — for I am not a father to them, but their creator and t…
- `649df46a4d6317348afac57e44f113c7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Shamira_IMadeMyDecision/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > {n}Shamira nods her approval.{/n} "Hepzamirah's killer will fight for us! Is there anyone left who doubts our imminent victory? Today, we will feed the darkness with the blood of the greatest monster in the Abyss!"
- `7537c571f0541b94289984f7c439dd19` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/HepzamirahGhost/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}Hepzamirah glowers at you angrily. Her face is dark with rage.{/n} "My warriors abandoned me in battle. My father betrayed me — although I served him well! If you can give me a single reason why I should remain loyal to them, I will name you the most cunning of mortals, {mf|master|mistress}."
- `7d183c51a1aabb5479598e991a533e49` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/HepzamirahGhost/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > {n}Hepzamirah stares at you in shock. She studies your face, trying to discern if you are mocking her. Finally, she says incredulously{/n} "Freedom? I won't turn down this gift. You are generous... But this will not change anything between us!" {n}Baphomet's daughter vanishes, but you catch a glimpse of the fury on the ghost's face before she disappears.{/n}
- `409c892766254a847b1716e0325bd595` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/TrueEnding/Suggest/Answer_0016.jbp`
  > "You were second to Hepzamirah, but now she is dead. That makes you the new Archpriestess of Baphomet."

## herrax

Authored return/rescue predicates: `herrax.trickster.knife_restored`.

- `99b641a4bbe0a284b8dae9c4d71dcc86` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Camellia/Q2_HeartsDesire/HeartsDesire/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia recoils as if from a blow and sways on her feet. She grips her amulet in her left hand, her knuckles turning white.{/n} "This can't be! It can't be you! Why? How did this happen? Could Herrax have lied to me...? No, why would she? Is this some kind of joke? But what if it isn't? What if this is... what my heart desires?"
- `d6955c2acf6ae4141966111db0592279` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Herraxa_dialogue/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > "Indeed, I have several items that were.... forgotten here by our clientele." {n}Some of the 'forgotten items' Herrax is selling are stained with blood, and two of the rings bear the characteristic scratches of being cut off a dead body along with the fingers.{/n}
- `8c9a58ca27fd07d47829d08e69c575ae` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Herraxa_dialogue/Cue_0275.jbp`
  > {n}Camellia sighs and casts a sidelong look at the smiling Herrax.{/n} "Yes, I have, several times. I visited while our party was resting. I killed several pleasure slaves. But their deaths gave neither {g|Mireya}Mireya{/g} nor me any real relief. So I thought it would be good for me to come here with you — if I perform the ritual while you're watching, I hope that Mireya will accept the sacrifice."

## horzalah

Authored return/rescue predicates: `horzalah.trickster.returned`.

- `14a848411b2120144a009b2817ab9444` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Grimbor/Q2_Ch4_QuestionOfReputation/YozzDying/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > {n}A flickering light envelops Yozz's body, and he disappears. The ghostly shape of Horzalah emerges in his place, her translucent hooves stepping across the pools of blood left behind by the leader of the Assassins' Guild.{/n}
- `d482b87112ca05f479c0c7fc529eed95` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Grimbor/Q2_Ch4_QuestionOfReputation/YozzDying/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}The translucent projection of Horzalah hovers above Yozz's corpse. She watches his dead body with disdain, and whispers a contemptuous curse.{/n} "At last this carrion heap got what he deserved. Pretending to be a servant of this arrogant fool was so... humiliating. I'm glad it's over now."
- `8dd73f42441f5d241876e32a4db46494` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Grimbor/Q2_Ch4_QuestionOfReputation/YozzDying/Cue_0060.jbp`
  > {n}Horzalah looks at you condescendingly.{/n} "If you have truly decided to challenge my sister — you're already dead, so don't expect me to jump into the grave with you."
- `bfe4e3daffa08f74fbfca97063d78874` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Grimbor/Q3_Ch5_PriceOfLoyalty/Horzalah_Ambush/Cue_0061.jbp`
  > "It's not like he had any choice in the matter," {n}Horzalah crows triumphantly.{/n} "This halfwit belongs to me... and so does his guild. After you killed Hepzamirah, Baphomet gave me back my power. I was restored to my rightful place, and Yozz, who once considered himself my master, became my property." {n}She cracks her knuckles, causing the former leader of the Assassin's Guild to cower in fear.{/n}
- `49356164c129c784b9ffc28888132245` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FinalDungeon_Colyphyr/Hepzamirah_main/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}The ribbon comes undone with the slightest touch, and the box falls apart to reveal... a tiny dead canary. The bird spreads its wings and turns toward the demon. It opens its beak and Horzalah's voice rings through the air.{/n} "We meet again... sister!" {n}The bird suddenly launches into the air, transforming into a flaming spear that flies straight for Hepzamirah's face.{/n}
- `59ea9a683806c4e41b656359f9ed8daf` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Demon/Yozz_InTheGuild/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "I don't think it will do you any good. There's no love lost between Horzalah and Hepzamirah. They aren't exactly close. Do you know how many of her siblings Hepzamirah has killed to get Baphomet's attention? She even killed her own mother! Hepzamirah will not allow Horzalah anywhere near her. She's too afraid her little sister will set her up, and use her corpse as a footstool."

## irabeth

Authored return/rescue predicates: `irabeth.trickster.returned`.

- `52efcc300eaea3a4988811efc2730581` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Sosiel/Cue_0266.jbp`
  > …nce wore thin. When he beat up some poor fellow for blasphemy, Shelyn took away Trever's powers. That was a huge blow for him. He left the city, asking only that his disgrace be kept secret from his family. That was why Irabeth didn't say anything earlier."…
- `c9bbb4668cdb6754c93d843adf4eac69` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Woljif/Cue_0136.jbp`
  > …n't hear my explanations, she doesn't have the time. I even thought I'd hide the Moon somewhere, and then, when all the noise had died down, I'd go get it and make a run for it. I'm sick of the thieflings. And they never appreciated me anyway."…
- `a42ccbb87ee446fb904caf081b83d8ea` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Woljif/Cue_3.jbp`
  > "But even she's better than Sister Kerismei, our boss here in Kenabres. By 'our' I mean the thieflings' boss. If you get caught by Irabeth, it's all out in the open: you go to prison or you end up as worm food. But if you step on Kerismei's toes — poof! You're gone. Just like that. No one ever sees you again. All you'll hear is, 'He went to the family farm,' even though he's never had a family or a farm."
- `03f1b104efe0265439401504a9212e41` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_CartRescue/Cue_0020.jbp`
  > "I'm afraid not, good {mf|sir|lady}. I just left the Defender's Heart. Before that, I was holed up in there, too scared to leave. Although spending all that time under Commander Irabeth's nose sure was something. She's a strict one — whew! — and when she gets antsy..."
- `a397010b26af94743b504a873228a0d0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Sosiel/Additional_C3/SosielCards/Cue_0056.jbp`
  > "I'll be honest. I'll miss playing cards. That excitement when you have almost no chance of winning and the risk is high, even though the stakes aren't life or death, just a handful of coppers... But I'll try to find a less destructive outlet. Perhaps I'll speak to Irabeth and ask her to play chess with me! I wonder what will come of it." {n}Sosiel smiles.{/n} "Thank you for helping me clear my mind. Good night!"
- `d7a47e224a7f4664aa217beb3bd06126` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth growls furiously.{/n} "You've killed two men — there are witnesses! Do you even realize that such a crime is punishable by death?"
- `e535b1585cd75ee499d2dac026b15c45` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth hisses through gritted teeth.{/n} "Ha! Doesn't affect her? Please! She starts drunken brawls here every night! Every time she gets wasted, she starts picking fights! I had a bad feeling this was going to happen. I knew from the start that someone was going to wind up dead!"
- `c4c80525c923482a981409cc8c74ce3c` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Answer_0006.jbp`
  > "I remember you died a long time ago."
- `8b314d7fa7a343aaac826186f404d79a` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Answer_0007.jbp`
  > "You were dead a second ago."
- `532cd23a0cb24de991d42ee67290385e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "What news would you rather bring back to Anevia? Will you tell her that her wife is dead, dooming her to heartbreak? Or will you tell her that everything ended well? I do not know what force created me or for what purpose, but perhaps I deserve the right to live? Doesn't a living Irabeth have more to offer this world than a dead one?"
- `6051233551d44ca18ec5063e1a66c7e4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "What? You're mistaken, Commander! I..." {n}Irabeth's protest dies away under your piercing gaze. Finally, she nods wearily.{/n} "Fine. There is no use in dissembling. Yes, there is 'something wrong' with me. The fact is that I am not Irabeth."
- `fbe6aebfebcc4d6fb7de026c37d35a29` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth adjusts her equipment, and the wrongness of her movement is striking. There is no doubt that this person is left-handed, but the Irabeth you knew always favored her right hand.{/n}
- `cd3270bb52df474d9b5ff9db063e3e1b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "I was — The real Irabeth was with a scouting unit holding the defense here. They were killed. As she was dying, Irabeth fell on top of the bodies of the demons she had slain, and something that had been inside them got into her body and began to change it. That change is what created... me."
- `b2feed2aaaff4cd18c153d2309c2dc7f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_6.jbp`
  > "The Irabeth you knew was killed right there." {n}She points at the place where she lay just a minute ago.{/n}
- `543dadbc36934532805dadb14479d2cc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_7.jbp`
  > "Before you do, Commander, please bear one thing in mind. Yes, I am not the real Irabeth — I do not know what I am. But the real Irabeth is gone. I could take her place."
- `25ac2a2c86434b8b9ba7cbe52ce64fd4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Irabeth/Cue_8.jbp`
  > "And the unnaturalness that was inside them mixed with Irabeth's blood, and poisoned her, even in death. Ironic for a paladin who is supposed to be immune to all disease." {n}Irabeth laughs bitterly.{/n}
- `fdd7645daa464a6db80d712c7ab4cc0f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_39.jbp`
  > {n}With a quiet tenderness in her voice, Irabeth replies,{/n} "Every minute. When they hemmed us in near Iz, she nearly died trying to get to me. Now I'll lay down my life to ensure that not a single hair on her head is harmed."
- `003ea0ebd4db49d29e633a18dbe2eeda` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_72.jbp`
  > {n}The other Anevia rasps,{/n} "Irabeth is dead, and you say thank you? You unfeeling bitch."
- `49f872a411248ad4f926f39a471270a0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "After almost becoming a human sacrifice, I knew I never wanted to leave Irabeth's side. Desna knows, I fell for her instantly, and I fell hard. My misery was gone. And when Irabeth showed me what was in those papers — proof that the cultists had a nest in her home city — well, I offered to help without a second thought."
- `42a59c793e7b4614a9f76ea6a2da339c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0102.jbp`
  > {n}Anevia smirks.{/n} "And it'll keep waitin'. The Nidal executioners are at the back of the line of all the people who want my head, behind the demons and the cultists. I won't abandon the crusade, you know that. Not because I'm tryin' to atone for my sins, but because it's my mission now. Mine and Irabeth's."
- `e7d5895a5967111418741f382a7494bf` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0107.jbp`
  > "When I left the temple, I lived as a woman. Not exactly hard, seeing how I was traveling alone. Then I met Irabeth. You can't imagine how terrified I was of telling her the truth... But she understood and she accepted me. And she even pawned her family heirloom to help me. The medicine she got for me, the thing she pawned her sword for, was an elixir to change my physical gender. That's it. That's the story."
- `ec1219cf3a664baab8987200e0fe1aa7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_3.jbp`
  > "It's sad that Irabeth won't come into the kitchen, and I won't break her off a steaming, fragrant piece of my bread. I guess my dream wasn't meant to come true after all."
- `5196e10a981c13f45b344b5bfcfbe5ba` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Answer_0194.jbp`
  > "Where have Ramien and his priests gone?"
- `2b76be1405bb4ad098d474fb3e40444c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "He didn't look like a worshiper of Baphomet or Deskari or of anyone else we've ever fought. No one got a good look at the profane symbols on his clothes, but the rod he left behind clearly holds ritualistic significance. Which of the demon lords has mastery over ice and bones? I think I remember reading something about monsters like that... But for now we can only guess."
- `a0468b547f769c24d83374dcf7ef3a67` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0020.jbp`
  > …e I realized a simple truth: I serve {g|Iomedae}Iomedae{/g}, not these people. I don't have to prostrate myself before them. So I left — as a paladin, but not a knight."…

  > "I left Lastwall and went traveling. The goddess guided me, and my path led me to where atrocities were happening. I often fought in exchange for gold. But never for unworthy aims, of course. I wandered the {g|River Kingdoms}River Kingdoms{/g} for a few years, killing monsters and tracking down criminals. Sometimes I thought I was just wasting time, that my true place was in {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g}. But I pushed tho…
- `85be303d0128cab468a516df65c79ca2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > …en among the ranks of the paladins! After that, delaying my homecoming would be tantamount to desertion. Luckily, I was no longer alone. Anevia was as eager to destroy the cultists as I was."…
- `1f5578a8aac321243bb395ed8407e85b` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "After the Queen entrusted me with leading the order, Anevia and I practically built it from scratch. We got rid of dead weight, people who weren't committed. We organized ways to transmit messages and orders safely. We introduced reliable ciphers. We found tacit allies around the city — from crusader orders to street beggars."
- `e8d47cf9f2e22ca4f8b85bba72336c37` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0043.jbp`
  > …inst the call of one's blood and join the forces of good. Still, most only see them as the enemy. I once saw a knight bleeding to death because he wouldn't let a tiefling priest touch his wound. Alas, I haven't been able to change this in all my time in {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g}."…
- `33ac7d4b774c30349a94aca9116522c8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0047.jbp`
  > "We met in Tymon as I was wandering aimlessly from one ordeal to another. We returned to {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g} together, to expose the {g|templars}Templars of the Ivory Labyrinth{/g}. The day the Queen knighted me was the second happiest day of my life. The first was the day of our wedding."
- `9829e10436576654ba5f3c87c2724ef8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0055.jbp`
  > …y of handing over the reins, at first. I was afraid that everything would fall apart without Anevia and me. But I took a risk and left a very clever lad in charge — a tiefling, as it happens. He infiltrated the cult alone — they couldn't imagine a tiefling wouldn't be on their side! He exposed an entire cell. I personally knighted him, just before the city was attacked. Of course, there were many who weren't happy ab…
- `7037f7e67bd33f446bf19b5474a28809` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0056.jbp`
  > "He hasn't disappointed. He has built up the order's ranks again and is running everything so smoothly, it's as if the demon attack never happened. He's proven more than once that I left Kenabres in good hands... But I admit, it's still strange to think that such a small order as the Eagle Watch now has two chapters — one in Drezen and one in Kenabres."
- `c5ea05129d4d99743b02c042dc58cc17` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0073.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth's face remains stoic, but her eyes shine with joy.{/n} "You neutralized the threat and saved many crusaders from a fate worse than death. These days, we need good news more than ever, and the news that you crushed those foul demons and their spells — this has greatly boosted morale in the ranks."
- `55860c4ac85033040a16e20fc3e56305` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0078.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth nods grimly and speaks with a note of pain in her voice.{/n} "I've already been told of your victory. And about what the demons did to their prisoners... I take it they're all dead now? Well, you did what you had to. Death is better than endless suffering."
- `71c77df3ebd4b334dbeda730f03e806c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0100.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth nods.{/n} "Thank you for being honest with me, Commander. I will serve my duties as best I can... for as long as I have left."
- `9f986796e3dcf6245aea49135ac34e59` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0133.jbp`
  > "I did not see my informant's face. He slipped out of a dark alley, handed me the note and disappeared." {n}Irabeth hesitates as she tries to remember.{/n} "He had a cloak with a hood masking his face, but the horns — the long horns — didn't fit under it."
- `d43d9d770b1fba7498c3854d3bfeca19` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0144.jbp`
  > "In these dark days, the most unlikely allies join forces to fight the common foe. All tieflings are treated with prejudice in Kenabres, let alone the smugglers and the thieves of their kind. And yet, they are on our side, and I'll make sure their contribution is not forgotten after the victory. They'll be of little use in open combat, but they'll help you spot secret doors and warn you about traps."
- `cb1b7fa4c79b9f7438004adacabebbea` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0151.jbp`
  > …rabeth looks down.{/n} "This scabbard held the Solemn Hour — my family sword. My father once fought with it, and I took it when I left home to become a paladin. Alas, it was not stolen. I parted with my heirloom willingly, to help someone dear to me. Anevia needed expensive healing, and, having no other way to procure the money, I pawned my father's sword. I think he would have understood — I'm sure he would have giv…
- `1a5dbf9a1af27814495c960c10636928` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0152.jbp`
  > "I hoped to redeem the Solemn Hour eventually, but there was never enough money, and soon the sword disappeared from the pawnshop altogether. I have no idea who took it. Seems like I'll have to part with my father's sword forever."
- `c363b78a128423847b337fa49554d660` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0170.jbp`
  > "As for me... Then again, it doesn't matter. You've dealt with the threat and saved many a crusader from a fate much worse than death. The news of Drezen's long-awaited liberation has improved the army's morale."
- `a9a0663a430015f4882ed230dcfb8b95` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0180.jbp`
  > …ation. He ran afoul of the Count somehow — perhaps by lecturing him about his idle lifestyle and a lack of purpose. When Sir Lant died, the Count managed to buy a portion of his estate and ordered the knight's helmet to be reforged into a chamber pot."…
- `930ece7ac8a4dd2458a4331a2404d582` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0188.jbp`
  > …and feast in honor of Daeran's birthday, and all his relatives were invited along with a few other distinguished guests. But the young count survived — because of his healing ability, I suppose. Daeran does possess an unusually brilliant talent, even if the application…
- `8b047f5cce3aff64eb4c73c0bb9d63ab` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0195.jbp`
  > "Ramien had another vision, or conversed with the gods, or something. He seemed devastated, and he whispered something about dead adepts while cursing your name. Then he said that he would never help a murderer and he left, taking all his priests with him. Those Desnans, you never know what's going on in their heads."
- `d47bcd8d88f8ea149a596ca927e1153f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0197.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth replies in a steady, almost monotone voice,{/n} "What happened was the same thing that happens every time I'm around. The Queen took me with her to guard her. I failed, and now she's dead. What else is there to say? Just this: don't rely on me, Commander."
- `8784d082bc6fe8d4f9ffe3b5c958a8f9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_0210.jbp`
  > "Things are bound to be tough for you, too. Most common folk fear death and hate the undead even more than they do orcs. I'm sure you're no stranger to sideways looks and whispers behind your back."
- `0c6aaf1020e847e29ced793aa8f58ba7` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_4.jbp`
  > …f Anevia's scouts knocked a rod from the cultist's hand — the rod I gave to you. In revenge, the demon worshiper cast a spell and vanished, along with the same brave warriors who dared to fight against him."…
- `12c1efaccf6948bdbe484c7865616a28` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Irabeth/Cue_6.jbp`

  > {n}Irabeth's armor is covered in blood, and her battered sword dangles loosely in her trembling grip.{/n} "Where were you? While you were gone, we were being cut to pieces!"

- `3409e8d444d5397488388ee8263f31c6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/GreyGarrison_ChapterStart/Irabeth_FirstMeet/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth slowly drags her hand over her face.{/n} "The city's gone. Most of the defenders, including the dragon {g|Terendelev}Terendelev{/g}, fell in the first few hours. The civilians either fled or died in the chaos. The place is overrun with cultists and demons."
- `9d986d87e40f7ec4486aa067ca5330fe` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/Hurlun/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > "Who now? Tirabade? That upstart — warden of the city? And I suppose I've already been written off for dead? This isn't sedition, this is outright insurrection! I am loath to abandon my watch, but I can't turn a blind eye to such a blatant usurpation of power. We have no hope of defeating the enemy if there is disorder within our own ranks. I'm going over there right now. I need to see what this Irabeth is up to!"
- `5b99e124d6f14544b829ed59c2c4f84d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/HurlunRamien_Late/Cue_0031.jbp`

  > {n}A shadow of something like fear flashes across Irabeth's eyes.{/n} "You'd leave me behind? So that I'd have to mourn the dead, my entire order, all of my friends? That's worse than dying along with everyone else!"

- `bfa6fad6b01ab234bb4b1443f4fccbd7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Aeon/AeonJudgment_c3/Q10AeonWintersunRavon/AeonWintersunRavon_Court/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth is hesitant.{/n} "We can't let a deserter get away with this, but... he didn't just abandon the army. He also gave up his title and wealth for this god-forsaken village. I think he is a noble, sincere man who made a mistake."
- `b6ba72e05a80a5544b17aedfb5747ac7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Aeon/AeonJudgment_c3/Q9AeonDrezenSadistic/AeonSadistic_KTC_Irabeth/Cue_0001.jbp`

  > "You dared to go against the goddess — and she forgave you. My mind can't comprehend it..." {n}Irabeth laughs awkwardly.{/n} "I was worried your impudence would be the death of us all... But now I think that you are strong enough to stand against the Abyss even without Heaven's help."

- `501529cbeb728ae42b8f028131f7aec4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/DrezenMain_C5/KTC_AneviaStart/Cue_0097.jbp`

  > …supposed to serve the Queen, but our hearts are here. Besides, you are still the commander of the crusade, and I have no right to abandon you — even if there's almost no hope left. I followed him here to make sure he didn't accidentally hurt himself." {n}Irabeth smiles happily.{/n} "I'm glad that he proved t…
- `f7d10f44770ad3247bd65f1e426a8b3f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethDies/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}The half-orc before you, wearing armor adorned with {g|Iomedae}Iomedae's{/g} golden swords, is clearly drained of all her strength. She can have only minutes left to live. There's soot on her face, and a pool of fresh blood is growing on the ground around her. Her hazel eyes are clouded, but her voice, accustomed to giving orders, is still firm.{/n} "Your Majesty. Commander. Irabeth Tirabade... stands relieved."
- `1f6f05c729cf7274390fa5c640436dcc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethDies/Cue_0013.jbp`

- `6c730e6c7f41bc24e8230638f3cecb00` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethSurvives/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}All the blood has drained from Irabeth's face — her green skin looks gray. Her breathing is heavy as she glances about like she doesn't understand where she is. She forces herself to focus her eyes on you.{/n} "The Queen... is dead."
- `33038b17ba9e8fe46b692ff8eff81e99` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/IrabethAnevia_Threshold/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth's stony gaze sweeps over the walls of the demon citadel with grim determination.{/n} "I can't believe I survived to see this day. So many warriors died who were braver than me, more worthy than me... It is time to exact revenge for all of them. This century-long nightmare seemed endless — but today we will end it. Forever."

  > "But mostly — I was wrong about a lot of people. About Jannah, who lost her nerve and abandoned her friends. - `e65e4b85197e6aa42a40d34abcea889c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/HideoutIntro/Cue_0006.jbp`

  > "Jannah found the strength to change. She didn't break... she became a better person. To be honest, I've never seen her more proud and confident than when we defeated Sunhammer."

## kaylessa

Authored return/rescue predicates: `kaylessa.trickster.returned`, `kaylessa.wasps.note_returned`.

- `9710c2e7eaad51742ab3c04ce37a1f5f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/DrowLeader/Cue_0057.jbp`
  > {n}Asty, her face a deep red, shoots Tran a furious look and... flashes him a surprisingly nasty expression.
