But you blink, and the illusion is gone.{/n}
- `dbb95cfe505e23f48b9f29bd4f9ac2ac` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Answer_0029.jbp`
  > "Forn, have you gone mad?"
- `ac209b415dd58bd4ab9aaeb8063bd9b5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "After the death of Kaylessa, the cultist I came here to hunt, I swore to track down her dark legacy and destroy it. To the last apprentice, to the last page of her unholy writings. That chase was what brought me here. I found a whole den of her vile followers. I was wounded and now they're following the trail of my blood."
- `bceafa08241bb8548a0a9b451ae4f1ea` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > {n}Forn rises to his feet. His movements make it obvious that his injured leg was nothing more than a ruse.{/n} "That is a shame. If you had just believed me, this would have been over more quickly and less painfully. It's unlikely that my... associates will grant you an easy death now." {n}Not hiding his contempt, Forn nods in the direction of the dark elves that have appeared out of the shadows.{/n}
- `103ad8c5ff67aef4d9766c350a43ff92` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > {n}Forn shakes his head wearily.{/n} "You held Kaylessa's note in your hands. It spoke of secrets that are too dangerous for you to know. Reading that note was your death sentence. I am sorry, I truly am."
- `bfab14e84cdc6b04ca914029e263cdf6` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > "My kin, if I could explain the truth to you, even you would see that your death is necessary. Alas, I do not have the right to do that. Please, just believe me, and accept your death with an easy heart."
- `3e3f9c3374f91f3419a7e8a4dd4407d8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}His voice trembles tragically.{/n} "I did not seek this perverted alliance, yet it was inevitable. These extreme circumstances forced me to end your life. Anemora also sought your death, so joining forces was mutually beneficial. If things were different, I would be the first to rise up against her, but... it just so happens that you have suddenly become a greater threat to my nation than she is."
- `1a60b47148c97a3419271cbced7a27ad` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_Ambush/Cue_0070.jbp`
  > "I would have preferred not to have involved you in this matter, and to put her to death myself. Then... I wouldn't have had to kill you as well." {n}Forn sighs quietly.{/n} "But after my first clash with Kaylessa, I realized I didn't have the strength. Thus, I decided to use you instead. And I provided some motivation — I set up those skirmishes with the drow so that my words would seem more plausible."
- `e6ed15d5f96550f438c7b320a9081919` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Answer_0034.jbp`
  > "Kaylessa is dead."
- `4c391137aacfb004e8b7d007e998fcbf` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Answer_0092.jbp`
  > "She left alive and unharmed."
- `80f13bb856d9ec84da2ecf796f1037ff` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > …ke caution. She has turned many innocent souls to the path of evil, and darkness has rewarded her with many gifts. Her appearance alone will tell you that — it is warped. The agate skin, the malicious stare of her blood-red eyes, the bestial teeth... She's more monster than elf at this point."…
- `e2204d61ab8e3ee46ac63d60fa0c112e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}After giving you a saddened look, Forn stares off into the distance.{/n} "Your decision is regrettable. I'm afraid that in doing so, you've condemned her many future victims to death. I suppose it just means that bringing this hunt to a close falls upon my shoulders."
- `92423fda5c34e054094321623560cf5c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0049.jbp`
  > {n}It's clear that this woman is a faithful servant of darkness — the marks that Kaylessa's masters have left on her appearance say as much.{/n}
- `263af0c886f0a1148894773610bfcef4` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "A drow? Unlikely. It's more plausible that her dark service has left a brand similar to the one that twisted the entire cursed drow race. A coincidence, I would think."
- `ce8cea6447085c54a8a34cc5b10def00` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0063.jbp`
  > "Other peoples tend to believe that identity is the sum of a person's actions and the events that happened to them. For them, I am one of a long line of warriors of {g|Kyonin}Kyonin{/g}, who, since his youth, has dedicated himself to hunting evil and injustice. For them, I am a successful and capable huntsman who has tracked down and slain a great many monsters."
- `5d0bd9fc44039eb49958770b1566c336` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > "Because I am the son of a noble and proud nation. We are sometimes criticized for our arrogance, but no one dares doubt our honor. When a crime is committed, we cannot turn a blind eye. If we were to abandon our obligation to oppose evil, if we were to surrender this obligation to other races, we would also relinquish our preeminence."
- `4eaa42251d99dcc40927c214742c5b9f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0097.jbp`
  > "Even though the miscreant I came to Mendev to find is dead, I've decided to stay here a while longer. There is enough evil roaming these lands, and not all of it comes openly to the battlefield. Many criminals are hiding in the shadows, and I will be tracking them down, granting them peace as best I can."
- `71138975f1e4b21498573fcbe2029a92` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0110.jbp`
  > …lt has been stamped out, that there is no remaining seed from which it could sprout once again. If there is even a scrap of paper left of her words — please, give it to me. I will commit it to the flames."…
- `143916f811dbe9b479658fac79944a90` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Forn_main/Cue_0111.jbp`
  > … that I love so deeply. I am shaped by what I find beautiful and what makes my heart quicken. By the fears that come to me in the dead of night. By the things I find so intolerable that at the sight of them, my very core darkens and fills with poison. Thus, to tell you who I am, I would require many days and much candor. Which is more than you and I have at our disposal."…
- `f1901db44e9496d4d95ae7d9c161d558` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Answer_0030.jbp`
  > "Why do you seek death?"
- `da2a0229cc112514887abdf4ad6fd89e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > {n}Even in the face of death, Forn maintains his melancholic tranquility.{/n} "I knew I would die like this. On the edge of the world, defending the threads of nobility that weave my race into a single, glorious nation. Well, at least I do not die in vain."
- `0528c6b30254f66488f7049c2fc10cbc` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > {n}He just smiles sadly.{/n} "You think that, since I'm at death's door, I will reveal the secrets of my superiors? You're mistaken. I, and others like me, know how to make our hearts stop beating before any treacherous words can spill from our mouths. I will remain loyal until the end."
- `e53eafc842a7cf54586d7f8b3bf8fe43` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > {n}With a mournful look, Forn says,{/n} "You wouldn't understand. You are not of my people."
- `ece3223d05841d24cb57a910634fb787` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "Forn wasn't a darkhunter, he was just a hunter. He served the Winter Council, a powerful elven organization. It wants me dead because I've learned of a very dark secret. That's why they sent Forn to kill me... and everyone I could potentially have told about it. Meaning you, soldier."
- `f252350f3d7c9c546b310a68d79efcab` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > "Now I ask one last favor of you. I still have friends left in {g|Kyonin}Kyonin{/g}... real friends, ones I can trust. Send a letter to Avennara, to the leader of the border defenders. Recount my tragic tale so it doesn't die as the Winter Council wants it to. Since I... won't have the chance to speak this truth myself."
- `6155ceb39e0f5e44ba587750134c18b1` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > "When I decided to tell the world the truth about the Dark Fate, the bane of our kind, I signed my own death warrant in the eyes of the Winter Council. They don't want elves to be tarnished by their association with the ignoble drow. They're too scared of being held responsible for the crimes of their dark cousins. It's better to hide this dangerous truth and destroy everyone who knows it."
- `38cba3c43ff5f55438395c08981ae8d8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0042.jbp`
  > {n}Kaylessa smiles mirthlessly. Her face shows the immense fatigue of the months of wandering, fighting for survival.{/n} "Such cruel irony. The elves were trying so desperately to convince everyone that they have nothing to do with the drow — the most vile, horrid race in the world... and it turns out that the distance between a normal elf and a drow is but a single step."
- `186e34cfcbfbed247bba592503855430` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_Reveal/Cue_0049.jbp`
  > "You can't give up! Don't listen to her, {name}! Even when the wounded beg for death, a healer must keep fighting! For every life!"
- `e5d64e54aeaed8b43b30b21d7fd3fbab` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_main/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > {n}Giving you a scrutinizing look, she says harshly,{/n} "There's no point in you knowing, soldier. Trust me, the answer would only bring you misfortune, and it still wouldn't be of any use to you. I'm a wanderer you won't ever meet again."
- `1988bcd88fe35424ebf4371eec3f96c5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_main/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "To find someone." {n}Kaylessa lets out a resigned huff.{/n} "His masters want me dead, which means I will die sooner or later." {n}After a pause, she narrows her eyes and spits out,{/n} "But that doesn't mean I'll lie down and accept my fate. I am no lamb to the slaughter, and I won't be waiting for a kindly executioner to come for me, knife in hand... never again!"
- `1a44532f0f3f8a849b8c5284fd19ed40` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_main/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > {n}With a bitter smile, she spits out,{/n} "Would you be satisfied if I said I won't bring any harm to you, your people, or your cause? I'm looking for an outsider here who couldn't care less about your crusade. If he lives, you won't gain anything. If he dies, you'll lose nothing."
- `5f306afcd4d305b41a611a59cc35e274` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Kaylessa/Kaylessa_main/Cue_0084.jbp`
  > {n}In a lightning-fast motion, Anevia knocks something from the woman's hands — an amulet that seemingly appeared out of nowhere. It falls to the ground and disappears in a flash of magic.{/n} "What's the rush, sweetheart? Stay awhile. The Commander and me, we've got a couple questions for you, too."
- `dd7bbcc0f32840f4ba581b6d4acd7daa` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/Anemora/Cue_0145.jbp`
  > … was so amusing to deceive them and slowly deprave them, setting them on the path of the swarm. When they realized how far they'd gone, it was too late. All those fierce champions of justice, vigilantes, paragons... All were now nothing but frightened orphans and common criminals. And none had the nerve to refuse when they pledged their fealty to me. None... except for that blasted Kaylessa."…

Kiana won't love me solely for the gifts I bring her!" {n}Elan makes no attempt to conceal his disappointment. Nodding goodbye, he lowers his hea…

  > "And I'm the one who gave a demon-ridden ring as a wedding present! If it weren't for me, poor Kiana wouldn't have been left without a soul. Seelah, you are such an idiot..."

Each venue has its own theme, but I won't tell you which is which, it will be more fun to see for yourselves! Both venues have food and drinks, and a little contest where you can win prizes. Kiana is greeting guests at her venue, and I'm leaving for mine now — I …
- `e65e4b85197e6aa42a40d34abcea889c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/HideoutIntro/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > …oul{/d}. He rushed off on his own! He told me to stay here, and wait for your arrival. I've been so worried. He should never have gone in alone..."…
- `aebbc1845e827dd4da4e28014e7b4162` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/KianaAloneAftermath/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Kiana appears to be in shock. Her hands are clasped to her chest, and there's a dazed look in her eyes.{/n} "I was a wife for less than a minute... and now I'm a widow."
- `a2903816f2b71254b91277557273ab37` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/SeelahInDoubt/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "I am responsible for Elan's death... I didn't uncover the demons' plan at the wedding, I didn't protect Kiana, and I didn't notice how desperate Elan was... I wasn't there for him in his darkest moments."
- `3f29d60b9a30bbb49bc9d56eaae1f643` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_ElanCalls/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}A shadow falls across Elan's face.{/n} "Kiana's lifeless body is there now... with the bodies of all the other victims. I've barely left her side since it happened. This is the longest I've been away... I know it's just her body... a shell, and that her soul is somewhere else, but..."

- `d4ee97d9d1100b846ac0a98b505b93ad` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/Hurlun_Tavern/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > …rmed into demons and rushed toward the {g|wardstone}Wardstone{/g}, slaughtering everyone who tried to stop them. Sixty-two people died in less than a minute. None of them dared to go near it, the light of the goddess burned them all — so they threw the blood from afar, spattering the Wardstone from every direction! And the lead demon — an eyeless beast…
- `5ecf8dd747e7d1e43ad790901893f0d3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/LostChapel/Boss_End/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > "Right... Right! Let's fly away, quickly!" {n}Holding Staunton close to herself, Minagho disappears.{/n}
- `382a7ca78c4a9304e97ca1e15b4ec44e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Drezen_C3/Hulrun_Dresen/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > …rmed into demons and rushed toward the {g|wardstone}Wardstone{/g}, slaughtering everyone who tried to stop them. Sixty-two people died in less than a minute. The demons used their mutilated corpses to desecrate the obelisk. None of them dared to go near it, the light of the goddess burned them all — so they threw the blood from afar, spattering the Wardstone from every direction! And the lead demon — an eyeless beast…
- `c2177313256e59241b6b5b447e7bc8a6` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Barricade/Answer_0006.jbp`
  > "It's over, Minagho. You're dead."
- `7621734e96e4dfe41aa38dbc3937c1a8` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Barricade/Answer_0014.jbp`
  > [Attack everyone] "Death to demons! At last I can make you pay for everything you've done, Minagho."
- `99858b3b66fc1e84c9e8723b025dc4d8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Barricade/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}The body of an ash giant is convulsing in its death throes while its killers are readying their next victim — a growling minotaur that is fighting to break free. Issuing orders from the corner of the room is none other than the lilitu Minagho. Large drops of blood ooze from the mark on her face and she dashes the blood away with an agitated swipe of her hand.{/n}
- `b2d3c9084d63844428a2ebf447fc301c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Barricade/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > "How about doing me a favor and just dropping dead?" {n}Minagho hisses, but despite her venomous words, her voice wavers.{/n}
- `f81823609efebad48b60f882166cd30f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > …no less disturbing for the comparison. All the bodies are strangely withered, almost mummified, but their eyes are wide open. The dead — dead? — stare of a blue-eyed half-elf girl hanging on the central hook is fixed on the Commander.…
- `171e57efcd3f2884494d163252974550` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > "Another step — and they all die!" shouts the demon Minagho from behind the structure. "These are my husks! One snap of my fingers and they'll all be dead! And any strike you direct at me will instead be turned on them!"
- `bcfd9c001c8a33a41906a9a95ab81e1a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0024.jbp`
  > Without further hesitation, the demon hisses the words of a curse and disappears behind a wall of power. The husks continue to thrash in agony, their screams turning to death rattles one after another.
- `fe5d25292fe743544a238b655aafd701` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > The Commander's attack reaches its target. Minagho screams with pain but manages to finish her spell and disappears, nursing her fresh wound and hoarsely exhaling the words of a curse.
- `c798d91c3807c484185fa95a4472d82e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0034.jbp`
  > Minagho disappears, her malevolent, slightly hysterical laughter echoing after her. But the Commander is no longer concerned with her — {mf|he|she} is busy helping the victims.
- `8d20b87113a10094b87b2ea08d579447` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0035.jbp`
  > "Do not abandon me, Inheritor, and give me the strength to aid my brothers and sisters in their hour of need," the old woman whispers softly. Her hands once more begin to glow with healing light, and she rushes to help the Commander — to heal the other husks. The Commander now sees her face — it is the face of Yaniel, the hero of the first crusades.
- `b383bf726fdb1af4ebd3e632ad8db323` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/MinaghoAndAzata/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > …'ve hunted the paladins of the good gods for centuries. You could make a burial mound out of the tin heads of all the idiots I've killed or tricked! But you — you're nothing like them. You never do what others expect, and you always appear where you're not supposed to be."…
- `7c1becd899efd054e8644ab40ae91d1e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/TrueYaniel/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > …hat everyone had forgotten me, then she came up with a story about everyone cursing me as a heretic. She told me that Iomedae had abandoned me — but I knew it was all a lie. I kept praying to my goddess — and now, she has sent you to help me."…
- `9962281c1101b2a4ca6ad981251e2243` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Aeon/Joran_In_Present_Main/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "Yes, I spoke with her. She came to see me before leaving the city." {n}Joran looks down and adds softly,{/n} "You know, {name}, I was head over heels in love with her when I was young. For so many years I thought she was dead, and it turned out that scum Minagho had been torturing her the whole time..."
- `7d69854b23344424f88234f8727f96db` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Demon/Alushinyrra/Minagho/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "You're always getting in my way. Since you're mortal, why don't you drop dead already?" {n}Minagho scoffs angrily, and hurries off, clearly eager to have the last word.{/n}
- `d891fe0a895604347be9295e11a2545a` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Ziggurat/StauntonZombie/Answer_0037.jbp`
  > "Minagho, who was your undoing, is dead."
- `94d328a293da2154389e4f5b184bd439` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FinalDungeon_Colyphyr/Mutasafen/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > …o, Darrazand, and of course, Hepzamirah. No one has ever found it! If I conquer it and bring it to ruin, the victory will be mine alone. And I have something very special planned for those stargazers..." {n}The demon licks his lips.{/n}…
- `810713e03a4468948b9ed27342d68405` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/GateGuard/Renown18.jbp`
  > "Chivarro tried to sink her teeth into you, didn't she? Her efforts must have been wasted, because she certainly {d|c4 chivarro dead}didn't like{/d} the consequences. She should've stuck to whores instead of messing with a serious player, am I right?"
- `44851e06dd3870b42897adf879ea03cc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/GateGuard/Renown20.jbp`
  > "They say Minagho had a score to settle with you. And where's Minagho {d|c4 minagho dead}now{/d}? Ha! Looks like she shouldn't have messed with you!"
- `99854f97811aa0b4eac54ccd352decbc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/MinaghoAfterCombat/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > "I can't bear it anymore... Enough... What have I done to you? Stop chasing me!" {n}Minagho's hand reflexively reaches for the wound you gave her in the Midnight Fane. Wailing and mumbling, the demoness disappears behind the door.{/n}
- `d1cb8997a01eb1c49a44a3158428b53e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/MinaghoAfterCombat/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "I will never forgive you! Die! Die already!" {n}Minagho spits out one last curse and disappears behind the door.{/n}
- `ead107a60c7e7604a901917160245c10` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/Nezirrius/Answer_0023.jbp`
  > "Minagho sent you to your death. I have laid waste to armies of demons. You cannot hope to overpower me!"
- `bd9080f3c356efb41ab47925d0ef16d6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/Nezirrius/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "I am Nezirrius. I kill." {n}The monster's whispered hiss sounds more like an icy blast of wind than human speech. It is impossible to say whether it is boasting, threatening, or simply stating a fact.{/n} "Minagho the lilitu. Purchased your death. Incomprehensible. You are a mortal. Killing you is easy. Why did she not kill you herself? Why me? A trap? An opportunity? An enigma."
- `7b9558e8d8df4e047bf96615edb24d29` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/Nezirrius/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > "Now I understand. Minagho is weak. Weaker than the mortal. She bought your death. I will kill you. Then I will kill her. Free of charge. I want to kill the lilitu."
- `aa4fe192155748444863a5f9b7058399` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/Nezirrius/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > "Why do you want to know? You are about to die. Your death has been paid for. Something is wrong." {n}The monster seems lost in thought for a moment.{/n} "Minagho said you were weak. If she lied, let her die as well."
- `668df2d0f0dee2e4094a490131ebb094` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Minagho_Desperation/Nezirrius/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > "If you fend off death at my hand. If you give me my death. Then Minagho's death is yours by right." {n}A house in the Lower City appears in your mind. It disappears after a moment, and a dry chuckle breaks the silence.{/n} "Now you know. But this knowledge is useless. My gift to you was useless. You will die. Now."
- `80b1bd76a717c934ebba2861ee31ba64` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Aeon/C4Aeon_InThePast/C4Aeon_AreeluPast/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > …, the week before I was captured, someone paid a visit to my laboratory. They appeared out of nowhere, said a few words, and then vanished without a trace. It was a most curious encounter... and it became even more curious once I discovered the truth of your origins. Once I put two and two together, I knew that my plans to open the Worldwound would succeed — after all, someone from the future was traveling back in ti…
- `9f3e984f55dea164c8ca7978a799c8e5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > …s that this monster Minagho escaped from the Midnight Fane alive. She ruined Staunton. He made the same stupid mistake I did — he left to be a hero when he should have been thoughtful and cautious. There are many demons in the Abyss, but I still hope to find that eyeless beast, and give her the justice she deserves."…
- `4413cba0ac497bb4798d862491f4354d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0039.jbp`
  > "I was captured on the day that Drezen fell, but I've changed captors twice. First, the traitor Areelu Vorlesh kept me in her laboratory, where she healed me... after a fashion. Then she gave me to Minagho. It was a subtle mockery, for Minagho was to blame for the death of my city, and for the fall of my friend Staunton."
- `4fb64c9dcf254974f8679bd3bd0214b3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "Minagho set up her lair in the Midnight Fane, and that is where she kept me. She told me that she'd make me her 'shell.' That's how lilitus torture mortals, and take their appearances as their own. Minagho had an entire collection. I even saw the elf woman she used to seduce Staunton. Poor girl. Minagho killed her in front of me. 'I'm bored of this look' — that's what she said."
- `d135030bf6ea87e43b84c0f17393e686` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Answer_0204.jbp`
  > "I recently met Minagho here in Alushinyrra. She tried to attack me again, so I killed her."
- `791040da477de81489c51acb419b01c5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Cue_0099.jbp`
  > {n}Chivarro's voice sounds baffled and distrustful but holds a slight hint of hope.{/n} "You let her go? But I thought Minagho was dead... Why hasn't she come to me? Perhaps she didn't want me to see her at her most vulnerable? Or was she afraid she might draw Baphomet's wrath onto me after she failed at his task again? Oh, damn it!"
- `ba0cad31fc44ec84eb82b6a3f758ac92` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Cue_0174.jbp`
  > …inagho organized a feast for her allies and lackeys in the Ten Thousand Delights. Pleasure reigned, but what a monstrous mess was left in its wake. So roll up your sleeves and get to work. Scrub everything thoroughly. When you finish, I will be willing to answer your questions."…
- `f748211432bd2e54883179f72c6777a5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Cue_0199.jbp`
  > {n}Under your furious gaze, Chivarro pales, but she presses her lips together in determination.{/n} "Don't you dare threaten me... This is my domain. I alone have the right to make threats here!"
- `27b0b4422b12558419fce9d6c82e7905` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Cue_0232.jbp`
  > "What about you, prince charming?" {n}Chivarro smiles ominously.{/n} "What I see in you is... a larva. The cocoon of a beast that will one day be claimed by one of the evil planes. Or do you think you're different from us? That you alone are capable of feeling something other than this thing you've just dismissed as an 'illness'?"
- `1a63a2e6946244940afd7293d6598ee2` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Herraxa_dialogue/Answer_0046.jbp`
  > "Chivarro is dead."
- `656f6b37c2de6764fac7ce75f60ba112` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Torlek_dialogue/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > "Of course, I knew Chivarro was a crazy bitch capable of anything, but this is extreme even for her. The last thing I want is to be dragged before Nocticula for killing her new favorite. I won't be part of this foul business. If she wants you dead, she can do it herself."
- `d1f171d4794f4d1f8b388e97085814c0` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/FreeGraveknightStaunton_Fallen/Answer_0001.jbp`
  > "I already killed Minagho."
- `aaecf51abda47bd468aadb8f01de0b70` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/FreeGraveknightStaunton_Fallen/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}The dead man answers fervently, with a spark of insanity in his voice.{/n} "On her! The temptress and deceptress who is the reason for all my suffering! Minagho is her name! And I have vowed to shed her blood!"
- `b77ea2cd37dd52947b0d06e36d52cff9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/FreeGraveknightStaunton_Fallen/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > "Death hasn't changed you one bit! You're still blaming someone else for all your troubles — Minagho, Queen Galfrey, Iomedae, fate, the weather, the position of the celestial bodies — literally anyone or anything but yourself."
- `063185f155302f747a6f7c5909cb935e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/LabyrinthOfBaphometh/Aponavicius_PrisonBoss/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > "I took Orengofta and went to do what... what Minagho failed to achieve — to destroy the Wardstone. I was hoping to be rewarded, but you destroyed me back there... time... time mends... itself..." {n}In an instant, she disappears without a trace.{/n}

## nenio

Authored return/rescue predicates: `nenio.trickster.returned`.

- `4cc9e9402e1b2374ba05cdd485dc1d91` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0084.jbp`
  > "Of course, some part of Golarion's population died as a result of her experiment, and the crusaders are still wrestling with its consequences to this day. But the very essence of what Areelu managed to do was a breakthrough of cosmic significance! Both figuratively and literally."
- `52d8016d90410b0408f992916b63f518` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0156.jbp`
  > "Angelic self-sacrifice in the face of certain death! And angelic concern for lesser creatures. You certainly have something of an angel in you."
- `86c9016bd1ea91d438310805c09baa4d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0195.jbp`
  > "I haven't. I never saw the point of it."
- `9b129e348d434dd4e9357774313f4d69` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0199.jbp`
  > "Not at all! I'll do the drinking alone. Your role in this experiment will include standing here and providing me with a visible reaction to my inevitable temporary degradation."
- `b57ee6df67a70e54cadb3d8bb4473d53` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0234.jbp`
  > …And, what is most striking, this fact doesn't bother me at all. It feels like all the thoughts in my head just went — poof! — and disappeared. My thirst for knowledge, my desire to prove myself — they're all gone. My mind is so calm, it's actually a little frightening. I simply must describe this condition for my Encyclopedia, but... I'll get to that later. I just don't feel like it right now."…
- `4d359989111ceab479ac6db13e7b24d5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0274.jbp`
  > {n}Nenio looks at you with disapproval.{/n} "You're trying to say that I made it for nothing? I've developed a perfect methodology that involves all the mechanisms of friendship between two individuals, but by all means, sure, let's just abandon the list!"
- `b3f6960c43e3f0f4bae5634fc7317c9d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0383.jbp`
  > "However, I have no interest in that. I see my loneliness as a sign of my uniqueness. The one who leads the way is always alone."
- `b8c227996fd487e498f1ea36bb03899b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Nenio/Cue_0398.jbp`
  > "Here's the deal. If the mysterious entity decides to gobble me up — run as fast as you can while it's distracted. Tell all of Golarion about the heroic death of its greatest daughter. Tell them she perished, but that she never strayed from the thorny road to knowledge." {n}Nenio sniffs and wipes a tear from her eye.{/n}
- `7df98ccbed6d4c438b4e13e0dddfe477` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC5_StoryEnds/DLC5_Postgame/SithhudIsFinallyDead/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > "Demon lords have the ability to resurrect themselves in their domain if killed. We have very recently observed this phenomenon in our dealings with Baphomet. Still, Sithhud lost the title of demon lord as well as his domain ages ago... Hmm..." {n}Nenio scratches her nose thoughtfully.{/n} "Could it be that this corner of the Abyss retained some properties of Sithhud's former domain? That sounds like a plausible hypo…
- `fbf88ab6b8e5d4c439a4357fdd918906` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0421.jbp`
  > …ans if he happened to be teleported into their midst (he couldn't), discover how many women in Absalom screamed at the sight of a dead mouse on their pillow (approximately thirty-four percent), and see what two armies would do if all their weapons disappeared the night before the battle (they would make peace).…
- `b6c0fb4c102cfb84f83a772e2dbb8a14` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0423.jbp`
  > Soon after the war, the tireless researcher of all things researchable embarked on a new journey. She had a sudden urge to know how much time it would take to visit every nation on Golarion. She {d|Nenio no friend}never returned{/d}. No, she did not perish — she simply got carried away with another experiment and forgot to come back.
- `9297d55318952d0428eb33601d763b68` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/EstrodTower/Faxon/Cue_0048.jbp`
  > "One, two, three threats, and all of them already on my list." {n}Nenio checks the page and sighs.{/n} "This specimen of demon worshiping is utterly devoid of ingenuity. I am not certain that his superiors would even notice the death of an underling as lacking in imagination as he."
- `b5d918bef7738f941ba638daca1c1206` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/MeetNenioSE/NenioJoin/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > "Eh? What?" {n}Nenio focuses her wandering eyes on you.{/n} "Oh, yes, the dangers and these... battles. Of course, I will follow your orders. I place my life in your capable hands — so I can focus on the things that really matter."
- `17477aab46157b94b84cce4b76e80739` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/AreeluLaboratory/MinionFirst/Cue_0037.jbp`
  > "Lady Vorlesh was here — and now she's gone. What terrible luck! What a horrid confluence of circumstances! The questions I have for her already run to a dozen pages." {n}Nenio sighs.{/n}
- `0e5552f478d7a834c9fdd61731c457ca` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/IvorySanctum/Xanthir_after/Cue_0104.jbp`
  > "Areelu Vorlesh has been sentenced to death for her crimes." {n}Regill stares down Nenio.{/n} "Along with all her devoted followers."
- `b02622fb993e5e146a4e13afd8980c84` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Dragon/HalFirstMeeting/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > "Nenio, here is an idea for your scientific research. Estimate the approximate number of cultists saved from death by this worthy gentleman. Calculate the approximate number of murders that each cultist has committed since they were saved. Multiply the two numbers, and then compare the total with, say, the total number of victims of an average balor. When it comes to causing the death of ordinary people, I simply won…
- `af8e15d0ef76f1c41adac138ea169ae5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FleshMarket/ObsidianMask_dialogue/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > "It looks like an ordinary mask — but is it really? Let's conduct an experiment!" {n}Nenio carefully examines the mask on the face of the dead demon.{/n} "Smells of decomposing flesh, quite specific. Tastes like... ugh. Tastes like rotten meat."

## nocticula

Authored return/rescue predicates: `nocticula.trickster.returned`.

- `df4145f7fd97e934ea3c05278e0647fd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Daeran/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > …o you mean the demons themselves or their followers? The former deserve to be cleansed with heavenly fire for their unholy origin alone, and the latter for their idiocy. I don't understand why a sane person would submit themselves to Deskari or Kabriri. All right, Lady Nocticula almost seems like a bearable enough mistress, yet there are so many other cultists... They've tried to recruit me twice already, and I'm not…
- `bc4edaa9edb0ae24eb8c0c6bc430cdee` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Ember/Q3_Worshipped/FinalSermon/Cue_0060.jbp`
  > "As if! Do your own fighting!" {n}With a contemptuous snort, Nocticula disappears.{/n}
- `bef2d948f3ed9554bbd041aa27c61255` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/JewelerFinal/Answer_0007.jbp`
  > "Well, I saw Nocticula kill Baphomet. Your lord died at her hands."
- `f1daa7051a3d48c4496cac6a59ce8246` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Woljif/Q3WoljifAbyssMoonRising/Q3WoljifAbyss_Voetiel/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > … of their very birth! Lady Hepzamirah, the strongest child of Baphomet, hunts down her unworthy brothers and sisters, because she alone should stand before the throne of her father, the Lord of Labyrinths! - `5ddd35e9e87d79c4c8723e2008b4201f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nocticula_main/Cue_0344.jbp`
  > {n}Chuckling, Nocticula waves it off.{/n} "Fine, we'll drop the matter. In truth, his death is a trifle and not worthy of our attention."
- `3d9824ed7cc194c48b595e4186be5e3c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nocticula_main/Cue_0345.jbp`
  > {n}A malicious smile appears on Nocticula's face.{/n} "Incidentally, was it really necessary to bother the dearly departed Alinythia? The shriek she let out upon returning to her grave interrupted my train of thought and quite spoiled my mood."
- `434a35250a767764b8ef3421ff6f2d78` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nocticula_main/Cue_0365.jbp`
  > {n}The works of Areelu Vorlesh vanish from your hands and appear in Nocticula's grasp.{/n} "That was the correct choice. I appreciate your willingness to cooperate and offer you a gift in return for parting with this treasure."
- `acb7f960b240fc84d86aa182e2a520c6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nocticula_main/Cue_0439.jbp`
  > {n}Nocticula shows no hint of sadness.{/n} "I am aware of this, of course. It is lamentable in a way, since his fear of me was strong enough to make him impeccably loyal. However, his death is but a single move in a much larger game. In games like this, losing a few pawns is unavoidable."
- `beff972bfd2f82349b800e778d13bb4c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Nocticula_main/Cue_0454.jbp`
  > "He tried to make me his property — and I would not forgive anyone for that. I had to remind him who he was and who I was. I did not kill him — just taught him a lesson and threw whatever was left at the steps of his ridiculous Cathedral. He's been angry with me ever since. It's very amusing to watch his desperate attempts at taking revenge. Oh, my silly, silly brother..." {n}You hear pity in Nocticula's voice.{/n}
- `3fa38df5d16ae194e973a1999619a826` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Second_Date/Cue_0081.jbp`
  > … Nocticula made it clear that they were under her protection. Varsa the Nosecutter tracked down his victims in the Upper City and killed them with his glaive. Then he robbed them, and cut off their tongues and noses..."…
- `034075f60a3b6d74a9535632cc4d4dd2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TavernBadLuck/InnKeeper_mainDialogue/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > "The Middle City is all about trade. The Ten Thousand Delights offers sex, the Fleshmarkets deal in slaves and weapons, the mercenaries and assassins sell the death of enemies. The most successful businessperson of all is Shamira at the Harem of Ardent Dreams. Her merchandise is Nocticula's favor and the favor of the powers that be."
- `f1ef55d868bda00459490498372f946e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TavernBadLuck/InnKeeper_mainDialogue/Cue_0127.jbp`
  > …e noticed that Alushinyrra was not built for your kind. It would be easy for you to get lost in our streets and die an inglorious death if you don't know how to fly or teleport. But why should I want you to die while you're still drinking? Take this. If you lose your way, take it to any arch and it will transport you here. Nocticula's idea, to aid guests from other planes such as yourself."…
- `f45e5f49128135f44bbef16f274c6b7e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Cue_0166.jbp`
  > {n}The fact that Areelu was invited to Nocticula's palace seems highly suspicious. Why did they quarrel, and why does the Lady in Shadow want Areelu Vorlesh dead? You could try to get to the bottom of this mystery, if you only knew where to look...{/n}
- `8f202c39537ae134d87cfec61feddbd3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Chivarro_dialogue/Cue_0167.jbp`
  > {n}You already know a drop of blood will make visible any secret signs left by Areelu. And now you know where to search. Perhaps you'll find some clues leading to Areelu in Nocticula's palace. Yet it seems highly suspicious that Nocticula would send you to dispose of someone she had personally invited to her palace. What is being hidden from you?{/n}
- `127c74ff9059b2348bc8a5719753e048` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/MagicDoor_dialogue/Cue_0045.jbp`
  > {n}The lock clicks and the door comments on this in a shocked and outraged tone.{/n} "What's the world coming to? Nocticula takes strangers under her wing, and then they wander around the city, sticking their noses where they don't belong and bothering decent folks!"
- `656f6b37c2de6764fac7ce75f60ba112` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TenThousandDelights/Torlek_dialogue/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > "Of course, I knew Chivarro was a crazy bitch capable of anything, but this is extreme even for her. The last thing I want is to be dragged before Nocticula for killing her new favorite. I won't be part of this foul business. If she wants you dead, she can do it herself."
- `909f6c75bd0169047806f1493f971e8a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/Goddesses_Summit/Cue_0045.jbp`
  > …" {n}Fury rings in the voice of the goddess.{/n} "You never told {mf|him|her} that closing the Wound won't save {mf|him|her} from death. For this lock will destroy the key to which it is bound. The one who closes the Worldwound will die along with it!"…
- `f56b37ebfaf4da34fba286e83d124d6c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/Goddesses_Summit/Cue_0310.jbp`
  > "Our annoying guest has departed. I am alone with you again." {n}Nocticula gives you a playful wink.{/n} "It would have been so foolish for you to reject your powers — so many victories, so many achievements lie ahead for you... Well, go now, capture Areelu and prove which of you is making the best use of her invention! I'll be watching you." {n}The demon lord blows you a kiss and disappears.{/n}
- `d1c26ac02f4546846814e0a1b5246577` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/Goddesses_Summit/Cue_0311.jbp`

- `300af708319631844a2bdc1d43aa99c1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/LabyrinthOfBaphometh/Alderpash/Cue_0059.jbp`
  > …he's been the same ever since he was created. All he cares about are his grudges — Asmodeus wounded him, {g|Lamashtu}Lamashtu{/g} left him, Deskari doesn't see him as his equal, Nocticula refused to bed him... He's a walking collection of scars — his wounds are his essence. So you take heed now."…
- `c38456a90027c514b81525de7e0b5cc9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/LabyrinthOfBaphometh/Prison_Baph/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > "Is it your own folly that has driven you to my realm, or do I sense Nocticula's hand in this? Ever since war broke out between us, she has gone to ground, preferring to send her pawns to fight in her stead. You must have outlived your usefulness to her if she has sent you to sacrifice yourself in such a pointless manner."
- `1fdd8d282526d1947b8d4a4100335f2d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Conspirators/Zeklex_Consp/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > "The Ardent Dream wants my gladiators to storm the Lady in Shadow's palace. I should have just told her no... at least then I would have been killed quickly and painlessly. Nocticula won't be so merciful... I dread to think what she will do to us."
- `611b88c578884be43956cf49956de0d1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Nocticula_WeHaveBetrayal/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > {n}Your inner beast answers to power, and power alone. It thrashes in terror, and you feel as though your heart is about to burst. If you rebel against Nocticula, this beast will crush your ribs, break free of your chest, and fall at the Lady in Shadow's feet.{/n}
- `f642294da6457db4183cd50308fd651d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Legend/ktc_MythicImpressarios/Cue_0027.jbp`
  > …ng rejected, especially so publicly! The thought of how upset and hurt she is fills me with... genuine 'sorrow.' For that feeling alone, I am going to help you."…
- `fd4f6c1d6397fa94db7aaf08de7dfeca` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Trickster/Nocticula/Answer_0011.jbp`
  > "Your brother sent me to play a prank on you... and Shamira's death is part of the prank."
- `4913a0c32fe01474ba7b4c6dde7f45bc` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Trickster/Nocticula/Answer_0020.jbp`
  > [Conceal the true purpose of your visit] "I killed Shamira."
- `900c45dc39736cf4487017e2b096e107` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/FirstFloor/NocticulaThreshold/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > {n}Nocticula's voice fades into silence, but a presence still lingers. You hear a faint sound... a noise like a soft sigh. Is that Iomedae? You wait for a moment in silence, but the feeling passes. The watchful presence is gone.{/n}
- `26d7f77c64919724ca0a79a75a59bdf6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/FirstFloor/NocticulaThreshold/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > "A goddess allied with a demon lord. It is extraordinary... but necessary. Do you see what you have done, {name}? You have forced a demon and a goddess to work together. Inheritor, this situation calls for a divine smiting... and I'll think up a few new tricks and traps myself!" {n}Nocticula's voice fades into silence, and you are left to ponder her ominous words.{/n}
- `f6691055caf27d94f8e3ee584dd50165` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/FirstFloor/NocticulaThreshold/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > …alls silent, but you hear a pure, clear voice — the voice of Iomedae.{/n} "I greet you, Commander. Our parting in Drezen may have left you bearing a grudge against me. Perhaps you are still angry. I do not approve of your decision to embrace these Nahyndrian powers, but I do not want you to fight alone. I cannot abandon you to your fate, especially now that I have watched you resist the Lady in Shadow's influence."…
- `ffe07bac357762b4bb173d2edc6d56fb` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/TrueEnding/Suggest/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > …rm a new Nahyndrian crystal. Prior to my alterations, the crystals would only form inside the bodies of the demon lords that were slain in the Abyss, a process that took centuries."…

## nurah

Authored return/rescue predicates: `nurah.papers_recovered`, `nurah.trickster.returned`.

- `057538de8e6f4e6f92c3ad1919600478` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_87.jbp`
  > Nurah Dendiwhar {d|c5 nurah redeemed}vanished{/d} for a time but soon reappeared to present her book, "A Traitor's Repentance," to Golarion, wherein she laid bare all the deception she had seen and participated in against both demons and crusaders. The book sent shockwaves through society — some felt sorry for the former cultist, while others wanted to see her hang. Neither side could find her.
- `c19c1887ee8c48840b635ce66aa75134` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah-Prison/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > …ous figure said to be more of an ideologue than an actual commander. All the cultists I know answer to a woman named Jerribeth. I never saw her myself, but I heard that she is dazzlingly beautiful, and when she speaks, you believe her every word, even if you know that she's lying through her teeth... I have absolutely no idea where to look for her. But I know there's someone much more powerful and dangerous, someone …
- `bf3f348f2e2f3bc419252bd1ce48f028` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah-Prison/Cue_0046.jbp`
  > …ous figure said to be more of an ideologue than an actual commander. All the cultists I know answer to a woman named Jerribeth. I never saw her myself, but I heard that she is dazzlingly beautiful, and when she speaks, you believe her every word, even if you know that she's lying through her teeth... I have absolutely no idea where to look for her. But I know there's someone much more powerful and dangerous, someone …
- `23197a6b1b7cdc941a5d0f75b7a2b092` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah-Prison/Cue_0096.jbp`
  > "He had them run drills every day, and every day they had to scrape someone off the ground... You're lucky that dwarf chased him off — {g|Darrazand}Darrazand{/g} alone could hold off a small army! He'll be back, and all of you here will be as good as dead."
- `90cf31b344e54db49a529e7981c5c749` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > "I was lucky — I've always been clever, ever since I was little, so they sent me to school instead of making me do the grunt work. They taught me to count and write, and I was sold as a secretary to Lord Axilar Trezbot. He's the one I left Isger with — never to return, I hope!"
- `b3cf7fbb0307a1644ac35be7999455a8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > "In his crusade, Lord Trezbot performed many brave feats, but even he could not stand against the {g|Worldwound}Worldwound{/g} alone. In his final battle with the demons, when my lord realized he was doomed, his final act was to cover my retreat, so that I could tell the world of his great deeds! Some people have said what he did was not heroic, just an arrogant moron puffing himself up one last time — but I say curs…
- `be68617f7ef879f4d8521dff6aa8a923` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0056.jbp`
  > "It happened the year {g|Aroden}Aroden{/g} died — 4606, a little over a century ago. No one knows if the timing was coincidental or if there's some connection with Aroden's death... But I'll tell you what we do know."
- `dcc43247533fc324c8eed0e16958de6c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0067.jbp`
  > "Instead of scattered gangs, they faced a large and organized army. Drezen was besieged, and the crusaders lost all the lands they'd won back before. They had to retreat, and all that remained of {g|Sarkoris}Sarkoris{/g} was left to the demons."
- `0b8c507a12969e84ea57736608e7d893` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0098.jbp`
  > {n}Nurah snatches the wand from her belt, waves it quickly, and disappears into a portal.{/n}
- `403978fe050b7254ab6455ba2eac59fd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0100.jbp`
  > "But... How did someone like you come to lead the crusaders? Just imagine..." {n}The shock on Nurah's face gives way to vengeful joy.{/n} "Ha ha! By all the gods I've long cursed... How about that! Really, burning is too glorious a death for them. Let's put fool's caps on them all, and let them dance! I don't know how you'll do it, but somehow I know you can!"
- `d1a1f5abb91416e49bfc3e50b5d3c19d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Nurah/Cue_0121.jbp`
  > "It most likely is dangerous, but... You are under the protection of Iomedae, Commander. She granted you mythic power, as they say. Surely the Inheritor would not leave you to face this alone? Have you, perhaps, noticed that the wound opens at moments when you waver in your faith? Perhaps it is triggered by doubt?" {n}Frowning, Nurah looks at you with sincere concern.{/n}
- `4f64e0864ddef1b4bac3b8b25058a4f1` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/Nurah_After_Battle/Answer_0006.jbp`
  > "Thanks for your help, Nurah. And now... I never saw you here. Go wherever you want, but run!"
- `a8f5baca6f8323d489f70e7f7a3475fc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/Nurah_After_Battle/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Breathing heavily, Nurah stands up and wipes her bloody hands on her clothes.{/n} "Well? What are you waiting for, you scum? Come on, finish me off! Kill me like you killed Staunton — and be glad you've defeated the villains! Just make sure you don't wonder whose fault it was that he and I were on demons' side. Thoughts like that could make you lose your appetite!"
- `1cfc9b154920a034d99f95efa6a217d7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GargoyleAttack/Nurah/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > {n}Nurah pats her body as if she's making sure all her parts are still there.{/n} "I think so... But it's a nightmare. These monsters are everywhere! Everything is burning, everyone's dead... What do we do?"
- `c29dc499d70233a4791ab01fc14f7ce3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/NurahDialog/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "Ha! Easily done!" {n}Nurah leans over the magic symbols painted on the stone.{/n} "I'm no mage, unfortunately, but I'm in the habit of thoroughly documenting all descriptions of rituals and symbols from magic books, especially demonic ones. Someone very recently opened a portal to the {g|Abyss}Abyss{/g} here, and summoned someone. It's not difficult to figure out who they summoned. She's lying dead right there!"
- `0258c2a124d2dd446bd98bf35591ddd8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/SwarmProblem_BE/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > The Commander and {mf|his|her} party disappear among the rocks, preparing to slip down into the canyon, while the swarm is distracted by their bait. A tight-lipped Anevia shows the way. Behind them, the final preparations are being made.
- `6d4fa662ea5954045b91946acdc923bb` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/SwarmProblem_BE/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > …ind the first body — the mangled corpse of a refugee from {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g}. A few straggling vescavors wriggle inside the dead man's gaping chest. Tiny larvae in greenish shells, all shuffling legs and hinging jaws — they don't seem menacing at all. But then, the rasping noise stops for a moment, and a high vibrating note pierces the air with new force. A blur of a blackish-green cloud rises above the canyon.…
- `e63e9a41f858df241a630f8c2ba6bda7` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/SwarmProblem_BE/Cue_0042.jbp`
  > …ne insect is burned with acid, five others circle and strike. A moment later, Regill's troops are a living graveyard, his knights buried under a layer of wriggling and buzzing beasts.…
- `66df3b129e5421f4caa72c25e4f6f4cd` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/SwarmProblem_BE/Cue_0060.jbp`
  > "To the demons with this!" One of the soldiers yells as he turns tail. "I'm not going to let some mongrel lead me to my death!" Giving in to panic, three more follow him. Lann never gets a chance to say a word, before the cloud swarming overhead stretches out a tentacle of a thousand of twisting vescavors. They descend upon the first of the fleeing soldiers, and swiftly clean his bones.

## seelah

Authored return/rescue predicates: `seelah.early.pack.returned`, `seelah.revived`, `seelah.trickster.death_returned`, `seelah.trickster.returned`.

- `355b4141fc73f95418fe1cc4c00a04de` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Answer_0036.jbp`
  > "If we want to win, we can't be mourning our deceased day and night. Plus, joy heals the soul!"
- `4daf1986422f7f24e8e62a67cc6a78d5` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Answer_0037.jbp`
  > "There's wisdom in the traditions of mourning, and one shouldn't reject them. All the more so if inappropriate merriment could offend other mourners. Many people's loved ones perished in Kenabres."
- `bbdf6662a07d6c34098866992b474d20` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Answer_0067.jbp`
  > "That's a good story. He could have died in disgrace on the gallows, but instead he died a hero."
- `c14e90090263f684db3013068b18bd25` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "Back then, Solku was a city of orphans, widows, and widowers. Gnolls decimated the city regularly. My parents were among their victims. People there heard about new deaths before they'd finished mourning their previous losses. One day a group of knights of Iomedae brought a glimmer of hope to the city. But while others saw them as saviors and protectors, I was busy pricing up their noble horses and their gleaming mi…
- `50c68a552d32d3741af44e0fd18ac652` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > …ke all those others she'd fearlessly driven from the gates of Solku. That one blow was fatal, but who was really to blame for her death — the gnoll attacker or the young thief named Seelah?"…
- `ca24b7ef8a4dd4d41ad3a2f78bb16023` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > …ed by nobility of spirit, some by a desire to fight injustice. I became who I am through penance and a debt unpaid. The day Acemi died protecting my city, I wanted to kill myself for the terrible guilt I felt. But then I realized it wouldn't make the world any better. And I decided that instead I'd try to take her place. I'd become who she had been, and protect the innocents that she would no longer be able to protec…
- `ada0b95295b418642b3a88d2c0c63846` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > …ly never have been opened. Who knows what would have happened to me if it wasn't for {g|Acemi}Acemi{/g} and her bravery — and her death? Maybe the world would know Seelah as a bandit, not as a knight and protector of those in need. There's a saying I like to remember — 'People rarely find redemption on their own. Someone must show them the way.'"…
- `ec1d667caa54c5d4fa313c30caa0cbb8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > "It's complete chaos. It's lucky we're here to handle it, right?" {n}Seelah smiles brightly. Then she gives you a worried look.{/n} "Does the way I talk bother you? I know I can seem... blasé. Many good people, and our protector {g|Terendelev}Terendelev{/g}, died here in Kenabres. A paladin of Iomedae is expected to be properly respectful and solemn."
- `a8b0259c7e7e90a468dcbb0ec9859563` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0059.jbp`
  > "Was that a hint of reproach? If you're only just asking, then no, I don't think so. You have to know when it's time for jokes and when it's time for mourning; the right moment for joys and for sermons. And sometimes a good traveling story can be all those things — if it entertains and purifies the soul. That story about the chupacabras is one of the good ones."
- `6d480f05821c5bf478601d7b43828570` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0062.jbp`
  > "There's a big difference between imminent death and probable death with a glimmer of hope, wouldn't you say? So here we are traveling through the desert — me and my friend, a priestess of {g|Sarenrae}Sarenrae{/g}, along with the little ratfolk fellow who'd asked for our help, and the two cutthroats. We get to the ratfolk village and see their bodies lying around, all drained dry. We figured we'd have to fight vampir…
- `b2bff99593bef40439e8ab422faa8e13` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0063.jbp`
  > "But we never got a chance to look around. Black shadows, quick as the wind, attacked us from all sides. Not vampires, but chupacabras. One of the lads we pulled from the gallows got killed right away. The second one ran for his life. That left me, the ratfolk, and Kyra — that was the name of the priestess — and we all steeled ourselves for the fight of our lives."
- `9b409f67a0fd2724eb99e556957641a0` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > …oks like. Well this was it. A moment before, we were lost, and a moment later, we had it back under control. The chupacabras drop dead, one by one. The little ratfolk is reunited with his family, who were hiding in the basement. The priestess and I close the eyes of that boy from the gallows who saved us. It was a shame he didn't survive the fight. May the gods keep his soul in its journey through the afterlife."…
- `02a3368727fddcf4a97f6cd4dcf3a6e5` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0069.jbp`
  > "Had it not been for that mercy, Milos — that was his name — would be hanging from a noose, and me, Kyra, and all the ratfolk would be dead in that village. Someone told me that a kind deed done by an unkind person is a hundred times more precious. By the same token, an evil done by a noble soul is a hundred times more horrifying. That's what I really wanted to say. The chupacabras were just a good starting point."
- `fee79a5fa92c56d49b611437d3463dc8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0071.jbp`
  > "And I'd fancy the stunt earned him some points at his final judgment. It's smart to remember our journey doesn't end in death."
- `f4a62f6d32724c84bbb706a9a8c048e2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0094.jbp`
  > "I... feel alone. Nobody else is going to sit with me and celebrate saving a beer cart. And I'd like to regain — if not the friendship, at least the trust of the other knights. Especially those I respect and consider my friends, like Elan."
- `1a4cabcaa0dace742b34b484e88a7b86` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0095.jbp`
  > "I always liked being a wandering knight because any moment I could get up and go help a poor ratfolk whose family had been kidnapped. Or an old woman whose favorite dog had gone missing. So here's my answer — I don't have some big, important goal, but I'll be happy to help those who need it — a little bit here and there."
- `234b7720015c4c848ab71a8f7f2ce9d1` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0121.jbp`
  > "I'm hanging in there. I don't feel good in this place — the air stinks like something died, and I'm having trouble sleeping. I keep waking up at night — I think I hear someone being tortured nearby, pleas and cries echoing toward me. So I wake up and listen — but there's nothing..."
- `316eed5aaab4f8d43bf74783a309db0e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0136.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah is tense and scowling, clearly lost in thought.{/n} "Elan went to get the abducted souls alone. He might not make it on his own... Damn it. Yes, {name}? What did you want?"
- `625dbcb1d6a345c4993b93a72510a615` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0138.jbp`
  > … worthless. My strength is not in my sword, but in my heart. If I lose my sword, I have lost a tool. If I betray my heart, I have died."…
- `c4bd1640d9c1a294e823d135bab0ad9c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0139.jbp`
  > "I will have faith in the Inheritor. I will channel her strength through my body. I will shine in her legion, and I will not tarnish her glory through base actions. I am the first into battle, and the last to leave it. I will not be taken prisoner by my free will. I will not surrender those under my command. I will never abandon a companion, though I will honor sacrifice freely given."
- `4f4066f7f9fe6684fa08e7c09e1887d6` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0140.jbp`
  > …ives. I will never refuse a challenge from an equal. I will give honor to worthy enemies, and contempt to the rest. I will suffer death before dishonor. I will be temperate in my actions and moderate in my behavior. I will strive to emulate Iomedae's perfection."…
- `03f1b104efe0265439401504a9212e41` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_CartRescue/Cue_0020.jbp`
  > "I'm afraid not, good {mf|sir|lady}. I just left the Defender's Heart. Before that, I was holed up in there, too scared to leave. Although spending all that time under Commander Irabeth's nose sure was something. She's a strict one — whew! — and when she gets antsy..."
- `170fca9d0a0b75f47bb243cc42e3a128` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_CartRescue/Cue_0039.jbp`
  > {n}Elan looks sternly at the cringing halfling.{/n} "I am not your commander, Curl, and I can't punish you or even condemn you. I suppose lots of people in {g|Mendev}Mendev{/g} think that the war justifies anything. But think about it — that dead fellow might have a family somewhere. His property and mementos should be returned to their rightful owners. They're bound to have sentimental value."
- `b7b722e7989a0a54883cff0789fa179d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_CartRescue/Cue_0049.jbp`
  > "What did you expect? Almost everyone is dead. The city now belongs to the demons and the vermin — rats, crows, and creatures like this one."
- `a1d3fd7fb2cf8ad4082a7fa55c4c43be` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_CartRescue/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > "I'd like to point out that if someone is gathering the belongings of the dead for purely scientific purposes, it is not pillaging — it is a posthumous charitable donation! And as such, there is absolutely no need to chase such a person halfway across a burning city, promising all sorts of dreadful punishments. However, in this case we are most likely dealing with conventional pillaging."
- `a57c9857b493f104ba53b2ddeb83ed2c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_CartRescue/Cue_0055.jbp`
  > {n}The half-elf smiles warmly at Seelah.{/n} "Hey, friend! Glad to see you're all right! When we got separated by the crowd at the celebration, I knew right away that something was wrong. And when demons appeared all of a sudden, I realized that was the reason why I'd been so anxious. But I couldn't find you among the dead in the square, so I kept my hopes up, and I was proved right!"
- `4e2640651ce975c488ba898e2fa63e51` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_SeelahMeetsFriends/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > "Allow me to declare my support for Seelah's decision as well. Experience tells me that we should shed no tears until after our victory. We'll bury the dead after the last demon has been driven from Kenabres."
- `506155d1d8ac9054fb5deefd8d3e1bc8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_SeelahMeetsFriends/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > …right! I'll explain. The life of a crusader has given me more than just a purpose and a chance to serve a righteous cause. - `cdd54bb0548998c4da8a8ef449b01c93` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_SeelahMeetsFriends/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "My friends and I killed two large demons attacking the camp, then rode straight to the city without spending any more time cleaning up the rest of their band. Our camp was attacked at the same time as the Wardstone in the main square, and I haven't been back there since."
- `f9dfb67fe5b06c348b3e9524e3d67570` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_SeelahMeetsFriends/Cue_0039.jbp`
  > "I just did what everyone else was doing. I grew up in the slums, where everyone stole a little, or maybe smuggled, or guarded stashes, so..." {n}Curl stares into his mug gloomily.{/n} "But I never killed anybody and I never did anything really bad!"
- `7de721cde6c515e438c9e37114f62fc2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_SeelahMeetsFriends/Cue_0046.jbp`
  > "So in the end I decided to choose another path. Now, if I'm going to lay down my life, at least it will be for a good cause. By tradition, it never has more than twelve members, and a new member is only knighted after one of the elders dies."
- `f2d1348c62f6af341be49baf3b1f0017` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/Ch1_SeelahMeetsFriends/Cue_0048.jbp`
  > "We patrol the lands along the Wardstone line, and provide aid to travelers and settlers. Unfortunately, death is common among the Houndhearts. I became a full knight two years ago, after laying my mentor to rest. But I'm content with my choice. My place is among the crusaders."
- `da671fced713af6419502ca50bdeb94c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q1_LittleTroubles/KnightCamp/Cue_0017.jbp`

- `b8d847c6ced21514489b9e3713e6dd68` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q2_TillDeathDoUsPart/WeddingIntro/Cue_0007.jbp`

- `3246cb05fd4a3ba42baaf9baa2341f4b` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ElanDying/Cue_0014.jbp`

- `27c8f5cbd443d0b4b9233b513f564a0e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/JewelerFinal/Cue_0021.jbp`

- `6034f972c463f4c4999238147697ca4c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/JewelerFinal/Cue_0055.jbp`
