- `aebbc1845e827dd4da4e28014e7b4162` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/KianaAloneAftermath/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Kiana appears to be in shock. Her hands are clasped to her chest, and there's a dazed look in her eyes.{/n} "I was a wife for less than a minute... and now I'm a widow."
- `e0abe01a6531ec6499f21e44cc81afe6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/KianaAloneAftermath/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "No, Seelah, don't look at me like that. I don't blame you for anything. I'm glad Elan had a friend like you. I'd like you to be my friend too. You must come find me when you return from your next heroic journey! But for now, I must ask you to excuse me... I need to be alone for a while."
- `e925066c7454c6341bc8ddfd3e49be8b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/SeelahInDoubt/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > …worthless — my strength is not in my sword, but in my heart. If I lose my sword, I have lost a tool. If I betray my heart, I have died.'"…
- `a2903816f2b71254b91277557273ab37` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/SeelahInDoubt/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "I am responsible for Elan's death... I didn't uncover the demons' plan at the wedding, I didn't protect Kiana, and I didn't notice how desperate Elan was... I wasn't there for him in his darkest moments."
- `fb301b49ff3c2f04884ef748bcda3913` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/SeelahInDoubt/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "Jannah found the strength to change. She didn't break... she became a better person. To be honest, I've never seen her more proud and confident than when we defeated Sunhammer."
- `fcec90eb8b08eec4896db0be72a6a444` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/SeelahInDoubt/Cue_0037.jbp`
  > "Yet in the end, she found the strength to change. She didn't break... she became a better person. To be honest, I've never seen her more proud and confident than when we defeated Sunhammer."
- `01a1c98b38a78fd4abeaa0c09f3a5be9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/SeelahInDoubt/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > …rn the weight of my sword'... and I've done those things. So maybe I should stop worrying about what Sunhammer told me before his death... He was just being spiteful, right? His words were full of poison... not truth."…
- `c393a1c5c641fd84e8506b7536998964` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_DeserterJoins/Answer_0025.jbp`
  > "How can I trust you? How do I know that you've changed? The last time we fought demons together, you ran away. You abandoned us, and you deserted your unit."
- `e8b6e28dbd288c44898407a47d71a39a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_DeserterJoins/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > "There's not much to tell. As I'm sure you remember, you chose not to punish me — you allowed me to go free. So I went to Kenabres... I figured I'd forget all about the crusades, have a fresh start... but it didn't work out. I heard the news about you and Seelah — that you had descended into the Abyss. And, well... I couldn't stop thinking about everything I had left behind."
- `1c742c23bbed1bd49b4d3f54432550ca` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_DeserterJoins/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "And that's where I've been. Once I heard that you'd returned, I asked if I could go meet with you. I'd made a good impression in my unit. I'd killed quite a few demons, and had begun to earn their trust. So they let me leave. I'm on parole, of course. I had to promise to come back if you turned me away."
- `cfcafcf2712bc674a8f13f9a1b8a4546` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_DeserterJoins/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "Because I can't stand by and do nothing!" {n}Jannah exclaims vehemently.{/n} "This entire story began with us — with our League of the Inspiring Cart... then I got cold feet, and abandoned you... and then you saved me in the Molten Scar. If I don't help you now, I'll feel like I've been written out of my own life story..."
- `3f29d60b9a30bbb49bc9d56eaae1f643` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_ElanCalls/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}A shadow falls across Elan's face.{/n} "Kiana's lifeless body is there now... with the bodies of all the other victims. I've barely left her side since it happened. This is the longest I've been away... I know it's just her body... a shell, and that her soul is somewhere else, but..."
- `81f0222e6856efd4bbdbd5dea796716a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_ElanCalls/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "Suit yourself. But I can't abandon those poor souls."
- `613485017b96c3840a2f9eea886deff1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Seelah/Q3_WeightOfMySword/ktc_ElanCalls/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah hesitates for a moment, and you can sense her inner turmoil. Then she shakes her head.{/n} "You have plenty of soldiers and companions, {name}. But the trapped souls of Sunhammer's victims... they don't stand a chance without me and Elan. I don't want to leave you, but it seems I have no choice. Farewell! Let's go, Elan. We need to prepare for our departure."
- `0fbadc3c22154ecbafb9806583bf66aa` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/Ziggurat/ZombieGalfrey/Cue_16.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah salutes the fallen knight.{/n} "He may have been created by Valmallos, but he left the world as a true paladin should."
- `0612767cb68c42398c43c83f9c4d8c56` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Anevia/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah gives Anevia a reassuring nod.{/n} "We don't abandon our own. We'll get them out, won't we, Commander?"
- `55af91b4f2fc42f7a6b3e9fb2899d006` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC6_ADanceOfMasks/KenabresFestival/Seelah_Event/SaveThePet/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "I don't know!" {n}Seelah shouts, barely holding back her laughter.{/n} "I've never seen anything like it, but it's definitely not a puppy."
- `85ebafae951a4b89b3341096be7bc63c` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > The death of his beloved broke Ulbrig's heart. He lived a long life, and many sought to win his favor — men, deities, and even fey among them. Ulbrig refused everyone, staying forever faithful to his last love.
- `a6d9962c732e4298a01ce06c75959adf` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > After the victory, Ulbrig refused to give up, hoping there still were remnants of the old Sarkoris left somewhere. He spent the rest of his days wandering the lands of the former Worldwound and their surroundings, searching, but in vain.
- `cb038844fe8c42542a71c71ce385e03b` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0382.jbp`
  > Once again, Seelah heard the call of the open road. Even after gaining mythic powers and coming face to face with a goddess and demon lords, she never stopped being a paladin and a wandering knight whose purpose was to {d|Seelah Good}help{/d} others.
- `3bbb39e73b29d0749b07a72aced789dc` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0383.jbp`
  > Seelah departed Mendev with a heavy heart. All the things she had {d|Seelah Bad}gone through{/d} had left her more confused and saddened than inspired. The paladin went off to seek her path and a way to find inner peace.
- `aab5b28d8ac14b97b87a8ca23bed9853` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_72.jbp`
  > …heir neighbors, and slowly nurtured the tiny settlement into a prosperous town. Ulbrig would often grumble that the townsfolk had abandoned the old ways and turned their backs on old Sarkoris, but still he worked tirelessly to protect the new, young Sarkoris.…
- `6114afd18c024d86ab71ed0c7637174a` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_74.jbp`
  > …ing more than an unremarkable outskirt of Ustalav. Once he realized his dream of restoring Sarkoris would never come true, Ulbrig left the town and wandered the land in search of a place he could call home.…
- `0b948b30c1f347feb0a2a8bdc3e13a2f` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_75.jbp`
  > No matter how busy they were or what distances lay between them, {name} and Ulbrig would often spend time in the Garden of the Gods — sometimes alone, sometimes in the company of friends, holding feasts that would amaze the Sarkorian gods themselves.
- `0a655e8e825956343a0d94bb59865f83` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MeetCamelia/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "He didn't get these wounds from the fall..." {n}Seelah's eyes warily scan the area.{/n} "Be on your guard. Whatever killed him likely hasn't gone far."
- `24489883451abeb4ca0643ae181dbb72` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MeetLann/Cue_0058.jbp`
  > "In {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g} they're called the mongrels. People say that they come up to the surface at night and eat anyone foolish enough to wander alone after midnight." {n}Seelah turns to Lann and Wenduag.{/n} "To tell you the truth, I thought you guys were just a tale to tell kids at night."
- `2bf108ed06e96124891a9783aef2a7bb` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah laughs.{/n} "I have this weird feeling, like I'm rescuing a fellow warrior from a dungeon. We can't just abandon it, even if it's no use to us. It's no use to anyone down here. But what if it could be repaired?"
- `fb9461af4e4bd0b4b81ebb102f001039` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah frowns.{/n} "Now... nothing, I guess. But this sword was legendary in its day! People say that when Yaniel held it, the blade would glow, striking demons left and right. Soldiers would see Radiance's light from afar and take heart, rushing into the fray and winning. But I don't know what's wrong with it now, or how to restore its power. All I can sense is that they made a mockery of it."
- `d555d2517cd2b9d4895683b6745c6aee` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "She died as she lived — with pride. She was one of the people defending {g|Drezen}Drezen{/g} to the very end — that's where she perished." {n}Seelah frowns.{/n} "Don't take this the wrong way, but I really hope that she died quickly — a hero's death. Because if she didn't... that means she was taken prisoner and had to endure unspeakable horrors. I wouldn't wish that on anyone."
- `56a08e3d9ab1ddb4c81680ae55286e5e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/DefendersHeart/Irabeth_DefHeart/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > "And one last thing. The Eagle Watch has lost a lot of soldiers recently — some were killed, but others simply haven't been seen since the attack. In the chaos that is now {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g}, it's next to impossible to confirm anything for sure." {n}Irabeth clears her throat.{/n} "One of the missing fighters is Jannah Aldori, a new recruit in the Watch. She got along well with Seelah and she often went drinking…
- `1dd1a5c1ac4e067498ba3a21587c1982` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/GibberingSwarm/AneviaDialog/Cue_0073.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah looks at Daeran in confusion, and a second later bursts into giggles.{/n} "Have the fumes gone to your head, Count? Look at you, cracking jokes like the worst kind of bawd!"
- `dc1a2f2a9d2409d44aa803ad2604d0b1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/AreeluLaboratory/SecretWish/SeelahWish/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > "Everything was different for a moment. My life was... different. As if {g|Acemi}Acemi{/g}, the paladin who died because of me, was still alive. She trained me and we traveled all over, and so much happened..." {n}Seelah grits her teeth.{/n} "It was an illusion. Let's just drop it."
- `ca849cd8887691f40a1a61d8985a473b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/CompanionsVSMythics/KTC_SeelahVSLich/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah strides boldly up to you and gives you a grim look.{/n} "Listen, I'm not happy to have to say this to a friend, a friend who crawled through caves with me, a friend who took Drezen with me, but... You know I'm not one for beating about the bush, so I'll just say it: the more I see of this power of yours, the less I like it. It reeks of death and evil. I can sense these things."
- `4bd7864ee07b59a4794195c7f6bd6596` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/IvorySanctum/Xanthir_after/Cue_0094.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah frowns.{/n} "What's with all the questions? We already know enough about this freak to destroy him once and for all. The best thing for everyone is if the knowledge of how he came to be like that dies with him."
- `d29f733cb900f0840a34368bd57a7eb4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/TempleOfDelamere/KyadoPityPriest/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah sighs bitterly.{/n} "Fear and mistrust have killed many worthy people."
- `e55f5989994e6964e8ec94ecbb186e09` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/TempleOfDelamere/Kyado_main_dialogue/Cue_0121.jbp`
  > …hands on her and... have a chat. Or maybe I'd just reach for my weapon right away — it speaks a language all its own. But a quick death is too good for cultist scum like her — after all the fear and torment and pain they put people through!" {n}After this furious outburst, Seelah calms down again and speaks to Kyado:{/n} "Stay strong, kid. You're not alone — you have allies. And your faith."…
- `3eef402a8e56c6146a723d95964fe27b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/TempleOfDelamere/Kyado_main_dialogue/Cue_0123.jbp`
  > …hands on her and... have a chat. Or maybe I'd just reach for my weapon right away — it speaks a language all its own. But a quick death is too good for cultist scum like her — after all the fear and torment and pain they put people through!" {n}After this furious outburst, Seelah calms down again and speaks to Kyado:{/n} "Stay strong, kid. You're not alone — you have allies. And your faith."…
- `de2557a7149ff1047be5a6c8971bfd79` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/TempleOfDelamere/ZanedraInTemple/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah is muttering promises of a slow, painful death.{/n} "Let the boy go, and you will have the chance to survive this day and stand trial for your deeds. One chance."
- `39ec68ad0f020544eae0e2a7a03412e2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Wintersun/BlindCarver/Cue_0049.jbp`
  > "Demons? You're saying that he leaves dead demons? What madness is this? But at least we know now who is responsible for the bodies in the shrine." {n}Seelah's eyes are ablaze.{/n}
- `a74d256ed124d76428addef950af10a6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FleshMarket/RabbleSlaver_dialogue/Cue_0084.jbp`
  > "Wow, that's quite a journey you've had... But you will get to Nerosyan and see your family again, I swear on my life. You hear me? Hold tight, and tell the others not to lose heart." {n}Seelah turns to you and says gravely,{/n} "We must get them out of here at all costs. We can't abandon our own. If we did, we'd be worse than scum."
- `fad9408c262552c4cb47c353b68002da` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TavernBadLuck/Blind_Halfling/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > "You scum! So many good folks died because of filth like you!" {n}Seelah clenches her fists, her eyes burning with rage.{/n}
- `bb01aaeade2e78147ae0f0828afd9e55` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TavernBadLuck/Blind_Halfling/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > {n}Woljif gives a sweet smile.{/n} "Pay her no mind, brother. It's just Sister Seelah here still ain't got over the shock of what went down in Kenabres — those scummy crusaders massacred our side, y'know? Seelah lost so many of her brothers and sisters in evil, she did, all of 'em killed by that butcher the people of Mendev call 'the Commander.'"
- `8ddfc824800c89f499aee09238428e10` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/AneviaDrezenPartisans/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > …earts have been guarding 'em all this time. The demons have attacked a few times and given the knights a thrashing, only Elan was left standing. But none of the victims came to any harm. By Desna, I hope their souls are found so all this won't have been for nothin'."…
- `ca0f2d19274b66744a3d48caf14edbe8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethSurvives/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > "If I betray my heart, I have died," {n}finishes Seelah.{/n} "But you're alive. And your Nevi is alive! And the goddess hasn't abandoned you. Come on, sister, snap out of it. You must fight and live!"
- `085e5ceeddcee3f4089917015f2feaf3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/Patrol_Hulrun/Cue_0045.jbp`
  > "It isn't bravado, my friend." {n}Seelah gives Lann a surprisingly serious look.{/n} "It's the reason why I'm still alive. If you let gloom get hold of your soul, even for a moment — that's it, that's your soul, and the strength to keep fighting, gone."
- `929483f47534b0f4eb97d7c405b5ff61` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Locust/Companions_Reckoning/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "{Name}! Stop!" {n}Seelah stands before you. She is wearing a new set of armor that has been polished to a shine and decorated with Iomedae's sacred symbols.{/n} "Do you even remember your name? Is there anything left of the person you once were?"
- `ee08de093bcaca64e8b8ce417f52abb4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/CompanionFarewells/SeelahFarewell/Cue_0003.jbp`
  > …coming battle! But... I think I'll stay here awhile, help the world to return to a life of peace and catch any demons that aren't dead yet. And I don't want to leave all my friends either! When we have peace, there will finally be time to spend with everybody. And after that... well, stick a pin in a map. There are still many places in the world where people are in need of help. Wherever the road takes me, I'll find …
- `e2c3d8d237c40c746b0718b38bc78db0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/IrabethAnevia_Threshold/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "Of course. It was a long time ago now, but it's not the kinda thing you forget. I was almost buried alive. If it wasn't for you and Seelah, my skull would be lyin' in some cave right now with only rats for company."

## shamira

Authored return/rescue predicates: `shamira.trickster.returned`.

- `70ba19ac848447a3aa2537d14861ad6a` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Answer_11.jbp`
  > [Lie] "You should know the truth. We are all characters in a lousy novel written by an amateur scribbler. You are based on an awful red-haired grocer's assistant who is always quarreling with the customers. In the end, you die a foolish and comical death."
- `1f7d9e2e6fe54554952e083a7fd453a7` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "The penalty for betrayal is death? How fortunate it is that we did not enforce that rule before now. Wouldn't you agree, Wenduag?"
- `a66d4df0941c43efa958294302fe07a4` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "On the Commander's advice, I prepared for this fight without resting, so I think I'll still find a way to surprise you!" {n}Yozz's words sound cocky, but he looks less than impressive. It seems his preparations have left him exhausted.{/n}
- `f21da7925c7c431b864a62c5f4136b45` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > "On your advice, Commander, I prepared for this fight without resting. Let's see if I can surprise you." {n}Yozz's words sound cocky, but he looks less than impressive. It seems his preparations have left him exhausted.{/n}
- `69b0f68650b74333b3880c41b8dfface` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Cue_11.jbp`
  > {n}Shamira looks at you as though you are death itself come to take her, and terror creeps into her soul. The flames of her wings grow dim and gutter like candles in a gust of wind. No one dares to break your sepulchral silence.{/n}
- `88c9ce15cd854d18b93d3057ab88ebfc` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Cue_18.jbp`
  > "You may be a pathetic copy, but the pain I'm about to inflict on you will be very real. The penalty for betraying the {mf|master|mistress} is death!"
- `953e55d6140e4567a112a395f8e1066a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Yozz/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "Shamira the Ardent Dream has abandoned Alushinyrra and the Abyss. She has come to Golarion. Now that beast is roaming the mortal plane, killing all in her path, and she is coming for your head. I plan to help you to get rid of her, but... I need your advice about how best to deal with her."
- `e79a04609b434f5e8c13342caab92562` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Yozz/Cue_25.jbp`
  > {n}Your benevolence envelops Yozz, causing something to twitch in his black heart. Could it be his conscience, which has never seen the light of day?{/n} "You forgive me? Really? Lamashtu's burp, Shamira would never do that. It looks like I'm in more danger with her than with you... What reason do I have to betray you, then? None."
- `8a80a7b2470e03b46a3959dcaec12863` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FleshMarket/BatteSlaver_dialogue/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > "I am Wirlong Black Mask. I was the champion of the arena, and I fought and killed in the name of my mistress, Shamira. I worshiped her. I was her slave and her bodyguard. She was flattered by the many killings dedicated to her honor and by the shouts of the crowd chanting her name. But then I had a dark revelation — I realized Shamira was not worthy of my worship. She owned me, but my true mistress was the one that …
- `966bda017e3fa89499862dd47275a611` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/GoodSamaritan_SideQuest/Latverk_main/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > …perate with the evildoers from the Ten Thousand Delights? Yet I suppose even that miserable lot is better than a slow and painful death in Shamira's clutches. I swear I'll go there and buy their freedom the very moment I have enough gold in my pockets!"…
- `31a7ee95beda4b74f9282c54d966940f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/HaremOfArdentDream/SecondBeggar/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > "Well, maybe there was a fella with such a name. Or maybe there wasn't. I don't know, and the others don't know either. What's your problem with us? Go ask around in the Middle City. You can even search for him in Shamira's dark hole, for all I care. Just leave us alone!"
- `b1ee8a9707fdc4e47b2db4d91dde5c53` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/HaremOfArdentDream/Shamira_dialogue/Cue_0101.jbp`
  > {n}Shamira gives you a withering glare and hisses through clenched teeth,{/n} "What are you doing here, aasimar spawn? Do you have a death wish? Or maybe..." {n}A trace of slight interest appears in the demon's voice.{/n} "Or maybe your sins and crimes caused your own kind to cast you out, and now you need a patron to protect you?"
- `fcdc9551e3d0513498bddbdd712915fe` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/HaremOfArdentDream/Shamira_dialogue/Cue_0206.jbp`
  > {n}Shamira eyes Trever with interest.{/n} "Aha! Isn't this the gladiator who fought his way to freedom? So, this is how you look without a helmet. Quite impressive... But no longer so interesting. The secret is revealed, the intrigue is gone. It was much more satisfying to watch you from the stands while imagining what was under the visor."
- `74a3306b1728d1244ace6e3adabf9386` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/HaremOfArdentDream/Ziforian_dialogue/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > "So be it, I say. Whoever loses this fight, I'll be the winner, and I'll savor the outcome. You, Shamira, Nocticula — may you all drop dead, tear out each other's throats, and strangle each other with your own intestines. You're all scum, and I'll piss on your corpses as they float by on an ocean of your blood."
- `3fa38df5d16ae194e973a1999619a826` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Second_Date/Cue_0081.jbp`
  > … Nocticula made it clear that they were under her protection. Varsa the Nosecutter tracked down his victims in the Upper City and killed them with his glaive. Then he robbed them, and cut off their tongues and noses..."…
- `24a6bb65aa6e8aa498d9bd3a9c6f6cbc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Second_Date/Cue_0084.jbp`
  > …es furiously at the bloodied, beaten figure.{/n} "The mortal who stands before you is a descendant of Sharzal, a slave trader who died recently. A week ago, this tiefling was offered the chance to be an accomplice in the murder of his progenitor, Sharzal, in exchange for a portion of Sharzal's slave holdings."…
- `ac11d175834e51e4b9fa8defac0b115d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Second_Date/Cue_0092.jbp`
  > …ger! We find such actions to be criminally insane and dangerous. Therefore, false justice demands that we sentence this mortal to death. Sharzal's property will be declared destitute of an heir, and henceforth accessible to anyone who wishes to take possession of it!" {n}The tiefling is too weak to protest, and at a signal from Shamira, he is executed by the guards.{/n}…
- `034075f60a3b6d74a9535632cc4d4dd2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TavernBadLuck/InnKeeper_mainDialogue/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > "The Middle City is all about trade. The Ten Thousand Delights offers sex, the Fleshmarkets deal in slaves and weapons, the mercenaries and assassins sell the death of enemies. The most successful businessperson of all is Shamira at the Harem of Ardent Dreams. Her merchandise is Nocticula's favor and the favor of the powers that be."
- `91107d5ef56ed1c409841fcc4643e13a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Conspirators/Zeklex_Consp/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "Shamira's lackeys will slit my throat if I try to leave. She knows I don't want to be here... that's why she's keeping such a close eye on me. Oh... why did I overthrow Irmangaleth? If I hadn't killed him, it would be his neck on the line right now instead of mine."
- `7c8791657d1ad594b909b3dd1ec6c23b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/KTC_YozzSoDiappointed/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "And the rumors don't stop there! There are whispers that Shamira wants to return to Nirvana and beg for Sarenrae's mercy! I've even heard rumors that Our Lady in Shadow wants to abandon the Abyss and join the ranks of the gods! Of course, it's not true... But it doesn't matter. The entire Abyss is laughing at us! You've made a mockery of the Midnight Isles!"
- `d8f3868d67d8b6d48866c9b14efff00a` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Nocticula_YouAreMyBestServant/Answer_0004.jbp`
  > "Ha! I killed Shamira because I didn't like her!"
- `445da66388bd3bf4f8179b7522a92eea` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Nocticula_YouAreMyBestServant/Cue_0039.jbp`
  > "Tell me, how should I reward you for Shamira's death? You can ask me for anything."
- `5ca49a9f75e9d3c479183f2dc3544458` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Nocticula_YouAreMyBestServant/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "But there's more. You killed Shamira. Everything she once owned now belongs to you. Follow me."
- `eef67f34807811f48baf1f0b54559c09` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Shamira_BetrayNocticula/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}Shamira's relaxed, arrogant demeanor has disappeared. When she addresses you, you can hear the tension in her voice, but you also detect a subtle note of triumph.{/n} "Finally, all my allies are here. You are the last one to arrive, mortal."
- `649df46a4d6317348afac57e44f113c7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Shamira_IMadeMyDecision/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > {n}Shamira nods her approval.{/n} "Hepzamirah's killer will fight for us! Is there anyone left who doubts our imminent victory? Today, we will feed the darkness with the blood of the greatest monster in the Abyss!"
- `12bb07f467c2c9a4a8c98cf9f7050c87` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Demon/Shamira_YouAreNext/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > {n}Everyone falls silent, but Shamira bursts into triumphant laughter. She points to you and yells,{/n} "Let this mortal's death be a lesson to all of you! I am your ruler! I will kill anyone who dares to challenge me!"
- `f642294da6457db4183cd50308fd651d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Legend/ktc_MythicImpressarios/Cue_0027.jbp`
  > …ng rejected, especially so publicly! The thought of how upset and hurt she is fills me with... genuine 'sorrow.' For that feeling alone, I am going to help you."…
- `fd4f6c1d6397fa94db7aaf08de7dfeca` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Trickster/Nocticula/Answer_0011.jbp`
  > "Your brother sent me to play a prank on you... and Shamira's death is part of the prank."
- `4913a0c32fe01474ba7b4c6dde7f45bc` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Trickster/Nocticula/Answer_0020.jbp`
  > [Conceal the true purpose of your visit] "I killed Shamira."

## soana

Authored return/rescue predicates: `soana.late_returned`, `soana.trickster.returned`.

- `6843443949974cb3879ee67965a292a2` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/Iz/SoanaMain/Answer_0004.jbp`
  > "Soana? I thought you were dead."
- `1c27dcef5249a3f48b1a8d76b90332f6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Wintersun/SoanaBeforeBear/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > "Soana the Seer, running to Gundrun with her tail between her legs! No, I could not leave. Let others hide behind the walls of their cities. This forest is my life, my chapel. Even if I could have made it through the Wound and gone south... no, out of the question! How could I look my children in the eye? Their foolish mother who tried to fight the Worldwound alone and lost! No, that will never happen!"

## targona

Or to punish those responsible for their deaths."

  > "I'm not one of your 'celestial comrades,' so you can talk to me whenever you want, or even if you just feel lonely."

  > "He fought until his last breath to {d|c0 salvation}save{/d} his mortal comrade. And he left his sword to be raised up by another in the future."
- `9f972e9b8e3568744b89bdcfd10b0897` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Angel/Targona/Answer_0033.jbp`
  > "He valiantly {d|c0 retribution}fought{/d} evil until his last breath. And he left his sword to be raised up by another in the future."

- `0a5654314707d084e83955978852ce29` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Angel/Targona/Cue_0012.jbp`

- `1a34be77f10855f438cc4527dc563ed5` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Angel/Targona/Cue_0013.jbp`

- `b3d3661f843059a41b8fcde29694ca2d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Angel/Targona/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "Wait!" {n}Targona hesitates.{/n} "I didn't dare ask you before, I wasn't sure I could take the answer. But please, tell me how my brother Lariel died. I miss him so much... I want to know what his final minutes were like."
- `b7f983c86b3c801468d06cbb4285f6ad` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/Mythic_Angel/Targona/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > "I don't know. She never mentioned it. But something about me piqued her interest. Many of her prisoners were unusual in one way or another. And, when she left and ceased her experiments, I think that too was an experiment of sorts. Perhaps she even continued to observe us somehow..."
- `e4c8d64e5f1895f4e9437f08c0352745` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Pulura_C3/Pulura_C3_main/RangerAlarm/Cue_0003.jbp`

  > {n}Targona's eyes glisten with tears of grief and rage.{/n} "Wicked fiend! You have killed Iomedae's most faithful servant. You will not commit such an injustice with impunity!"
- `74015da8b218a8e4e98b4984cfdb5c66` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/FinalDungeon_Colyphyr/NocticulaAfterBaph/Cue_0187.jbp`

Finish your business in this cursed place, and then come home to Golarion!"
- `c6fef3f82698c9c44b10e21625270aa1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Devastator/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "My brother... my brother..." {n}Targona whispers.{/n} "How could this be? We thought you were dead..."

  > "Do you not remember me, monster? I am Targona the Silver. I thank Heaven for our meeting, for I will soon behold the death of the one who robbed me of my brother, {g|Lariel}Lariel{/g}!"
- `f89fdfdfcef564c489f5a2f46190a825` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0012.jbp`

But now, we are simply allies... and we must all return to our former lives."
- `c1662db6b8446ee4285b7bac773a5d3d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0014.jbp`

## terendelev

Authored return/rescue predicates: `terendelev.trickster.returned`, `terendelev.trickster.watch.claw_returned`.

- `ec1d667caa54c5d4fa313c30caa0cbb8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Seelah/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > "It's complete chaos. It's lucky we're here to handle it, right?" {n}Seelah smiles brightly. Then she gives you a worried look.{/n} "Does the way I talk bother you? I know I can seem... blasé. Many good people, and our protector {g|Terendelev}Terendelev{/g}, died here in Kenabres. A paladin of Iomedae is expected to be properly respectful and solemn."
- `e6c6ea01eedaa3d45ad222303034950d` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0536.jbp`
  > Even death {d|dargon terendelev}could not{/d} stop Terendelev from being a protector of mortalkind. And even though she could not go to Kenabres, she would often circle over the city and its environs, keeping travelers safe from any harm.
- `d19119c46cbb6f742b81508c3174455c` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0537.jbp`
  > No one knew for certain where the dragon Sevalros disappeared to. But there were rumors that he had been spotted flying west, toward Varisia and beyond, across the ocean. Perhaps he is somewhere in Arcadia or Tian Xia now, living a {d|dragon sevalros}new life{/d} under a new name.
- `0c255a6c57476d44b99164b1f507f7f3` — slide; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0541.jbp`

- `21b10801b6c2b194d92506a137ef1307` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/StoryTeller_MainDialogue/Cue_0765.jbp`

- `09b4f14f1e318234dbac72017ac2634b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/StoryTeller_MainDialogue/Cue_0775.jbp`
  > "Terendelev... has come a long way. Her unit was ambushed by demons once, and she was infected with foulness. The Terendelev you met in Kenabres had gone through many trials and regained her former purity."
- `c68d9b3a2b887f645ac539f996a63a92` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/StoryTeller_MainDialogue/Cue_0777.jbp`

- `fabb0f4a300ac7b4389c7bee48b07c77` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/StoryTeller_MainDialogue/Cue_0779.jbp`

Terendelev will live! A new Terendelev, whose eyes a…
- `3409e8d444d5397488388ee8263f31c6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/GreyGarrison_ChapterStart/Irabeth_FirstMeet/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth slowly drags her hand over her face.{/n} "The city's gone. Most of the defenders, including the dragon {g|Terendelev}Terendelev{/g}, fell in the first few hours. The civilians either fled or died in the chaos. The place is overrun with cultists and demons."
- `a73e2dfa834c9d5408b454e67e98a81f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/BookEvent_Aeon/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > …ent by moment simply by looking at the ruins: from the chimneys torn down by powerful wings in a sharp dive, to the bloody tracks left behind when the demon dragged away the noble reptile's broken body. But it is no hypothetical scout gazing upon the ruins, it is {name}... And {mf|he|she} is not alone.…
- `192d4b7ab7ed1b24686362e5c1265ea3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/HurlunRamien_Early/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > …'s on your say-so, then that must mean there is someone down there! Your associates, no doubt! And they're just waiting for us to abandon our posts before they slink out and try again to..." {n}The old man notices your approach.{/n} "And you... I remember you! You appeared in my city the day the demons attacked and {g|Terendelev}Terendelev{/g} died! What are you doing here? Answer at once, or I'll have you strung up …
- `e325be7b8abf1234a9c64d79e6a80826` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/HurlunRamien_Early/Cue_0083.jbp`
  > "How very dare you! Of course they're not all like this. We also had a protector, the silver dragon {g|Terendelev}Terendelev{/g}... Oh, yes, she died, if I recall."
- `714e65ce9aec4fe4cb59f739f1a14a0d` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/OraclePartyHouse/Daeran_FirstMeet/Answer_0003.jbp`
  > "Kenabres lies in ruins, Deskari killed Terendelev, the Wardstone has been captured, and you're asking me why demons gatecrashed your party?"
- `202fd787cfb7e094cb0d7388e02f207a` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/DeskariFight/Answer_0041.jbp`
  > "In the name of Terendelev, slain by your hand! While you've been cursing Iomedae, an opponent whose strength rivals your own has appeared on Golarion! Leave willingly, and I will spare you!"
- `674166c9c5c273a45ac8f5dcb10f5293` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Aeon/TerendelevCompanion/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "I feel worried! I haven't left Kenabres in such a long time... This must be what a young mother feels like when she flies off hunting for the first time after her children have hatched." {n}Terendelev smiles.{/n} "But the townspeople of Kenabres are not helpless children. If disaster strikes, they will hold out long enough for my return."
- `5b74379924aae7b49aab041126c4e92d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Aeon/TerendelevPresent/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > "Even though I knew the attack was going to happen, I did not expect the onslaught to be so fierce. I would never have survived without you. After you disappeared, I kept fighting until reinforcements arrived." {n}Terendelev lowers her eyes.{/n} "I felt the chill of death, and I nearly faltered before the foe. But your determination gave me the strength to keep fighting."
- `12fa3cd5f6c04e7894052d1f355ec996` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/SevalrosDying/Answer_0001.jbp`
  > "Terendelev died a hero. She fell while defending Kenabres. I met her in Iz as an undead creature and convinced her to serve the crusade once again."
- `823aec5e3ed348641a1fb6c4fa3d325c` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/SevalrosDying/Answer_0003.jbp`
  > "Terendelev died a hero. She fell while defending Kenabres. Her selfless act will never be forgotten."
- `b8c3e7e756ee3cb4c98d62d883e6d071` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/SevalrosDying/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Sevalros is breathing heavily, but his eyes are no longer clouded. His gaze is clear and bright.{/n} "Traveler, you... Please, one more thing... tell me... What happened to Terendelev? Please tell me she hasn't ended up like me — twisted by hatred... lying on a heap of rubbish... left with nothing but broken hopes and shattered dreams... Tell me she's all right..."
- `20303625492cc3946a300cb5b98f84d0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/SevalrosDying/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > …like this... Tell me, how is she? Please tell me she hasn't ended up like me — twisted by hatred... lying on a heap of rubbish... left with nothing but broken hopes and shattered dreams... Tell me she's all right..."…
- `4fcc37913dcf624488e308bafa6e9166` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/TerendelevUndead/Answer_0037.jbp`
  > "Awaken, Terendelev! I call to your pain! Feel it once more, follow it, and rise from the dead!"
- `722f0e1dd5faf274c98f38b8a5929821` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/TerendelevUndead/Answer_0041.jbp`
  > "You can't fool me, Terendelev. I can feel your pain, your struggle with the corruption. Even now, after your death, it's still eating away at your soul."
- `bf754037cb7965748b7cf6195b8ffc42` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/TerendelevUndead/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Terendelev's empty eye sockets stare at you from the recesses of her skull. Her skeleton is engulfed in flames. It is difficult to believe that these bones once belonged to a majestic silver dragon, but you do not feel like Terendelev has departed the Material Plane just yet.{/n}
- `045f1890c191ec840b555db45cb5f377` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Dragon/TerendelevUndead/Cue_0043.jbp`
  > "So what? Why does it matter? I am a corpse, I am bones, I am a memory! Leave me alone!" {n}Terendelev's voice is filled with fury.{/n}
- `21ffae2a289a3a048b1c3cd6869cf8e2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/DeadTerendelev_lich/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}The magic that once filled the bones of the silver dragon with unlife has been dispelled. But you feel that Terendelev's soul has not yet departed to face Pharasma's judgment — and that means you can bind it with your spells, and enslave the corrupted creature.{/n}
- `def5d9e3426ac6c478489aa4d87bdaea` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/DeadTerendelev_lich/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > …sh them. It is a shame that I did not possess the gift of foresight when we met for the first time back in Kenabres. I would have killed you right then if I had known you would commit such blasphemy. I would have burned you to ash — you, and the whole lousy city."…

## tirabade

Authored return/rescue predicates: `anevia.trickster.returned`, `irabeth.trickster.returned`.

- `0692b723c5cf8614ba6c979bce6d6a62` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Sosiel/Q02_Impostor/Impostor_Prison/Cue_0080.jbp`
  > "Trever betrayed everything we believed in. All our vows. He went to the war to protect people, and in the end he tortured them... Commander Tirabade was right: I shouldn't have tried to learn what happened to him. Forgive me, {name}, I need to be alone now." {n}Without looking at the imposter, Sosiel leaves the prison.{/n}
- `02c3704dd1ec41de83556ac482838f3a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC2_LowFantasy/Map1.5_Catacombs/Mercenaries/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "You don't know, do you?" {n}The sergeant looks away.{/n} "Anevia Tirabade is dead. She went missing along with everybody else when the demons came."
- `74e7adc14fbfe86478643e83f83765d5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/Anevia/Cue_0114.jbp`
  > "Don't even ask. That name has meant nothing to me for years — it's just sounds. There's probably nobody left alive who knew that person. I'm Anevia Tirabade, that's all there is to it."
- `9d986d87e40f7ec4486aa067ca5330fe` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/Hurlun/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > "Who now? Tirabade? That upstart — warden of the city? And I suppose I've already been written off for dead? This isn't sedition, this is outright insurrection! I am loath to abandon my watch, but I can't turn a blind eye to such a blatant usurpation of power. We have no hope of defeating the enemy if there is disorder within our own ranks. I'm going over there right now. I need to see what this Irabeth is up to!"
- `5b99e124d6f14544b829ed59c2c4f84d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/HurlunRamien_Late/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > "Who now? Tirabade? That upstart — warden of the city? And I suppose I've already been written off for dead? This isn't sedition, this is outright insurrection! I am loath to abandon my watch, but I can't turn a blind eye to such a blatant usurpation of power. We have no hope of defeating the enemy if there is disorder within our own ranks. I'm going over there right now. I need to see what this Irabeth is up to!"
- `f7d10f44770ad3247bd65f1e426a8b3f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethDies/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}The half-orc before you, wearing armor adorned with {g|Iomedae}Iomedae's{/g} golden swords, is clearly drained of all her strength. She can have only minutes left to live. There's soot on her face, and a pool of fresh blood is growing on the ground around her. Her hazel eyes are clouded, but her voice, accustomed to giving orders, is still firm.{/n} "Your Majesty. Commander. Irabeth Tirabade... stands relieved."
- `1f6f05c729cf7274390fa5c640436dcc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Iz/IrabethDies/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "Anevia. Tell her I love her. Take care... of my Nevi." {n}Her eyes roll back. Irabeth Tirabade is dead.{/n}

## vellexia

Authored return/rescue predicates: `vellexia.fight_survived`, `vellexia.returned_picture`, `vellexia.trickster.returned`.

- `2ef87e7664a2e6341a88f9bc6de3292e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/StoryTeller_MainDialogue/Cue_0448.jbp`
  > …silent for a short while, as if listening to something.{/n} "I can't smell anything but sewage. I can hear some demon's snores or death rattles. Hidden by a cocoon of spells, I keep invisible watch in a dirty alley in Alushinyrra's Lower City. Two winged shadows sweep past overhead and a terrible, stone-splitting shriek rolls like a tide through the streets, but it can't hurt me. I know the ways of this city and have…
- `9e0c5a84db7c66f41b79bb1e81898f26` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_First_Date/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > {n}Vellexia watches Atselm closely. She muses thoughtfully,{/n} "But every warrior will meet their death eventually. How do Golarians face the end of their life? Are they scared of death, do they despise it, or do they welcome it as a friend?"
- `9c0d977a04189fb45bfb8ade769cf669` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_First_Date/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > {n}Vellexia looks at you with newfound respect.{/n} "Your words distinguish you from a multitude of fools who think only about themselves and give no thought to the future. They fear death, and they tremble at the thought that their pathetic lives will come to an end."
- `9dcb74d97db5b5b45876f425b63520cd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_First_Date/Cue_0075.jbp`
  > "Ah! The smell of death is in the air, and it always makes me wax philosophical! I beg your forgiveness! I hope you don't find me too boring?" {n}Vellexia giggles, making it clear she doesn't believe such a thing is possible.{/n}
- `02f2252cb700d2c4fb5bca7583115f2e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Main/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > …! I've heard rumors that I kill them, that I keep them in my dungeons, and even — just imagine — that I rape them until they drop dead of exhaustion... It's all an extraordinary load of rubbish. I haven't done anything like that for at least a couple of millennia."…
- `17201573a5de0b940b5854fbc709ced0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Main/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > "Vellexia. We have clashed in battle many times. Her claws were always stained with the blood of those she killed on the battlefield. Now, she looks... different. But I am certain that her true nature remains unchanged. She is still violent and dangerous. You must be on your guard, Champion."
- `16daec55d13e5fb4ca9ffc6ab4d0c963` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Main/Cue_0059.jbp`
  > "Seriously? You used to swan around grand palaces and rub shoulders with the movers and shakers of demon high society? You were basically a hoity-toity noble, but you left it all behind to sit in some old ruins on Golarion? And you call Vellexia insane?"
- `76aac39c73f95864b81c218db7e89324` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Second_Date/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > …on, sweet {name}. Don't stay away too long. I'm looking forward to your next visit." {n}Vellexia gives you a dazzling smile, then vanishes into thin air.{/n}…
- `99d697e04be811341957b3d091436f48` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Second_Date/Cue_0066.jbp`
  > …oon, dear friend. Don't stay away too long. I'm looking forward to your next visit." {n}Vellexia gives you a dazzling smile, then vanishes into thin air.{/n}…
- `2f70553ac59c3a343a5723d00da52e40` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0048.jbp`
  > {n}Vellexia gazes at you with a mixture of pity and revulsion, as if you were a kitten that's been run over by a wagon.{/n} "What need have I for your chivalry? Have I been abducted by a dragon? Or perhaps the evil ruler of a neighboring kingdom wants to steal my virginity? If I needed a knight in shining armor, I would have gone to the Fleshmarkets and bought myself a dozen of them."
- `7e3fefb4f3ac8c1459362f6bd04908f0` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0050.jbp`
  > {n}If looks could kill, Vellexia's arrogant smirk could certainly inflict a mortal wound to anyone's self-esteem.{/n} "Don't flatter yourself, little Golarian. Your short, meaningless life and your stupid, painful death will go unnoticed in both Alushinyrra and the Abyss... The millennia will erase your name from history, and all traces of you will turn to dust..."
- `8a142cc07fa2d3446a676e54c58f923a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > {n}Vellexia's face contorts violently. Her beauty disappears, and her features transform into something wild and animalistic. Her jaws expand, revealing her horrible teeth.{/n} "Guards! Kill {mf|him|her}! If you can't amuse me in any other way, at least you can entertain me with a fight!"
- `42429350764f92140b293b24039b89ef` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0079.jbp`
  > …the density of mahogany. Her eyes, which reflect her dark and insatiable soul, become larger and larger until they are all that's left of her. A moment later, a magnificent mirror stands before you, its surface shrouded in a dark, mysterious haze.{/n}…
- `55b0e77523709a8468c5e5e982ba6952` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0097.jbp`
  > {n}Vellexia's elegant manners vanish as she lets out a shrill, bloodthirsty howl.{/n}
- `7b33813505066434085981c8fc69299b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0114.jbp`
  > …ce grows quiet, and you realize that she is no longer projecting her words into Vellexia's mind. This message is intended for you alone.{/n} "I've helped you escape her wrath this time, crusader, but if I continue to interrupt your conversation, I will only share in your grim fate. Stop being so predictable. If you behave more erratically, you might be able to stave off Vellexia's boredom, and survive the reception."…
- `0a37f00893dd85d46986770c41c6f994` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/RaptureOfRupture/Velexia_Third_Date/Cue_0115.jbp`
  > …ce grows quiet, and you realize that she is no longer projecting her words into Vellexia's mind. This message is intended for you alone.{/n} "I've helped you escape her wrath this time, crusader, but if I continue to interrupt your conversation, I will only share in your grim fate. ## wenduag

Authored return/rescue predicates: `wenduag.trickster.echo.abyss.rescued`, `wenduag.trickster.echo.abyss.returned`, `wenduag.trickster.echo.abyss.returned_available`, `wenduag.trickster.returned`.

- `3293dc01b8539bd4db487ba2da609758` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0086.jbp`
  > "Are you ashamed that we killed Lann in a dishonorable way?"
- `6d04f29fd0b8e99449aac0f7298cde14` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0107.jbp`
  > "What do you make of Dyra's disappearance?"
- `0de26d772cb6040499d967c693a72dd0` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0108.jbp`
  > [Show her the book you found] "This book got Dyra killed."
- `5a629eeb5b74cf445a5520f171d1e68d` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0114.jbp`
  > "Wenduag, I think you killed Dyra. You must answer for your crime!"
- `794322736d911344cb9f95e5a78faad7` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0116.jbp`
  > "It's a dangerous book. Dangerous, because Dyra was killed for it. Take it and keep it safe. If anyone else's blood is shed because of it, it will be on you. Got it?"
- `7f05aeda98771ac4b83eec9b8590138d` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0163.jbp`
  > "Nothing. Because if you had decided to defect to, let's say, Savamelekh, I would have already killed you."
- `d46fefe5818803749ba7566c584915c2` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0199.jbp`
  > "What will you do now that Savamelekh is dead and the mongrels are free?"
- `efd87266625d0ec409cf761df3dae433` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Answer_0299.jbp`
  > "You used to behave differently when we were alone."
- `0803edb6b2eb43949bc28956068cc18f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > "I was only serving the demons because they promised me power — and only for as long as they had something to offer me." {n}Wenduag chuckles, revealing her sharp teeth.{/n} "But Hosilla can't offer me anything now she's dead, and I was never going to risk my life for her. You are a true leader, one worth following!" {n}the huntress says in a fawning tone.{/n}
- `fd5d2041a388288429e757c4fd31702e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0048.jbp`
  > …et soft and you die. No, I always pushed myself further, challenging myself, taking myself to the very edge. Many times, I almost died, but I always came back stronger. I earned all my strength on my own, and if anyone didn't respect that, I just grabbed my knife! That's why I became the best hunter in the tribe."…
- `f961d641c094a394cae6cc03027f58e2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0091.jbp`
  > {n}The huntress laughs breezily.{/n} "He's the one who should be ashamed — for being so stupid as to show up to the fight alone. Honor is just a made-up thing, it doesn't exist. There is only victory."
- `997c96dacb3e90e4da7a220e3a6db979` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0096.jbp`
  > … attack the demons. I think he has always hoped that the fated day when neathers would return to the surface would come after his death. He is worried that he will lead the tribes to their deaths. That's why Sull is going to cling to the rock he's hiding under with all this strength. We have to drag him out from under it."…
- `ff3bd47e77f2b6d4bb524b83c5b06f57` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0109.jbp`
  > {n}The huntress shrugs indifferently.{/n} "She probably ran off somewhere. She's always been a bit crazy, not like other neathers. She was always drawn to the surface and uplander pleasures — she didn't like hunting or bloodshed. I think she abandoned the tribe and went traveling. And good riddance — my tribe can't afford to carry any dead weight."
- `76f06e4a3937dee409e3dd69e6acbcf5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0121.jbp`
  > "Shut up, old man. Yes, it was me. Dyra kept meddling in my affairs, so I killed her." {n}Wenduag looks at you, her eyes narrowed dangerously.{/n} "So what? Dyra was a nobody. {mf|Master|Mistress}, I can't believe you want to punish me for her death. Me, your loyal and invincible warrior?"
- `e74597bd176e165468083514de7cd401` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0123.jbp`
  > …taught them to obey, you'll see! And you, {mf|master|mistress}, will regret rejecting a useful servant like me!" {n}A ring you've never seen before appears on Wenduag's finger. With a quick twist of the ring, the hunter disappears with a soft clap.{/n}…
- `3eb97c939786e344d8528ce5761078d7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0147.jbp`
  > "We went to watch gladiator fights, I bet, I lost and... I think killed someone. At least I still have my money. Then there was something else... Next thing I knew, I was in a scummy little place called the Bad Luck. And that's when I left to rush back to you!" {n}Wenduag stretches gracefully and shamelessly, like a cat that has been caught stealing food.{/n}
- `03126f836b781e74d838242e0d509376` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0180.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag reaches for a heavy gold ring on her finger, a ring which you have never seen her wearing before, twists it, and disappears with a soft pop.{/n}
- `b4338ede1df95b24392bfecde834855d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0186.jbp`
  > "Of course, {mf|master|mistress}! I don't have a death wish! Betraying you has never even crossed my mind!"
- `9b8453ea0197575499b9db4d5008ca34` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0200.jbp`
  > "For a start, I will help you destroy all the demons left in the Worldwound. As long as they're here, neathers will never be safe — we are just tools to them, things created by one of their own. As for what will happen after the victory... Another war, I think. Neathers deserve their own land, their own piece of the surface. But no one is going to give it to us without a fight. So I'll have to use force to get it for…
- `98f5ef10b45d49c4cada889ab97ec4f9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0236.jbp`
  > {n}You see the apprehension behind her sweet smile. Her bristling fur and tail whipping back and forth are dead giveaways.{/n}
- `b128f0dc00f92bf4198892ba90c23997` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0243.jbp`
  > {n}Bristling, the huntress scoffs aggressively and shakes her head.{/n} "What support? What did that old sod tell you? Some tear-filled nonsense about me being a lonely little girl? Stop listening to that old fart, he doesn't know what he's talking about. I am a warrior, I don't need any support!"
- `74113dd608b35e4409fd0600ca8e0b36` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0248.jbp`
  > {n}Snarling, the huntress shrieks,{/n} "And I'm telling you — leave me alone!"
- `33f26961f9c25244f9f1e38eded21288` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0252.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag laughs distrustfully.{/n} "{mf|Master|Mistress}, you don't think I'm going to believe that, do you? You are the ruler of the undead, your heart is dead, and any attachments it once held are gone. But why you are trying to deceive me, I wonder? Do you need another sacrifice? In that case, may I please settle for the role of the servant who hands you the knife, rather than the victim?"
- `6bbd3285a31979c4fabec309ce8b1dca` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0254.jbp`
  > "You can see what happened. My tribe is on the verge of extinction. I led them to battle — and now I'm alone. Even if this threat does not wipe us out, another threat will come after it, and then another, and another... I'm afraid for my people." {n}Wenduag's voice holds not even a hint of her former giddiness and bravado — just blind, grim anxiety.{/n}
- `939efe1548b7bc248a1acd598bac3c4b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionDialogues/Wenduag/Cue_0296.jbp`
  > "So this entire burden will rest on my shoulders alone? Hmph, this is the first time I've ever seen being chief as a burden, rather than a prize." {n}Smiling cheerlessly, Wenduag stares at her feet.{/n}
- `3b4a67c459d0c874cb5d0f397e7f7b84` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Answer_0006.jbp`
  > "Unfortunately, Dyra is dead. I let Wenduag escape."
- `666a95129faf53340816fc0d76304fa5` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Answer_0010.jbp`
  > "Unfortunately, Dyra is dead. But Wenduag has paid for her death with her own."
- `577fb875dd2b8204e91e0a7c625d9ec7` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Answer_0030.jbp`
  > "Wenduag is dead. She has paid for her villainy in full."
- `488a62f0dc5dd6d41ade8afee3d5612a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > {n}Sull shakes his head.{/n} "What? Wenduag'sh gone? But why did you — hmph, uplandersh! What do you care about ush and our problemsh?"
- `1236879fb243fe04b8edc13a74d5dfa3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "Don't be sad, Chief. Your tribe is no vigilante mob. If you want to give Wenduag a piece of your mind, you'll get your chance. She's not going to leave us alone — I can feel it. Wenduag is of this tribe. She'll be drawn back to us, no matter how hard she fights it."
- `6962f312c7d41cb4797cccf028c2fce1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > {n}Sull shakes his head.{/n} "What? Wenduag'sh gone? But why did you — hmph, uplandersh! What do you care about ush and our problemsh?"
- `d7c3fddee9f596940a1a914131e3d709` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SulWaiting/Cue_0033.jbp`
  > "Don't be sad, Chief. Your tribe is no vigilante mob. If you want to give Wenduag a piece of your mind, you'll get your chance. She's not going to leave us alone — I can feel it. Wenduag is of this tribe. She'll be drawn back to us, no matter how hard she fights it."
- `6ff92d01dfe0e294a9d21079c26aac49` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SullNeatholmNews/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}The Chief coughs.{/n} "Gather who? There'sh no one to gather... Wenduag took everyone... and the onesh who shtayed... They're hersh too... She wantsh to torture me, to make me shee who'sh in charge here. If shomeone could find her ... find her and punish her. But there are no warriorsh left here at all."
- `72eb6ce453fcb0a4b9bf53b64b685e63` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SullNeatholmNews/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "She killed shome, frightened shome... Everyone went with her, those who could walk after she did her talking. And those in the village... are either guarding me or sent to taunt me." {n}Sull looks up.{/n} "Maybe Wenduag was right. Who did you bring here, Lann? The uplander? The one who brought death to our tribe?"
- `cd3a4898d4e27a441874d96c648b4871` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/SullNeatholmNews/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > … listened. She didn't like that. And the next day, {g|Dyra}Dyra{/g} went out to gather healing mossh. Wenduag took her alive. She left a message for ush, warning ush againsht trying to get out, or else..."…
- `5f5168b1e572cc54c96c3a825a7e1fcf` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/WenduagCave/Answer_0039.jbp`
  > "Leave the mongrels alone, Wenduag. We've come to bring these poor folk back to the surface."
- `b32c41f2e07c95f47a898ac1c486f99d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/WenduagCave/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "I make sacrifices to {g|Savamelekh}Savamelekh{/g} in exchange for his blessings. But this fool — oh, her I killed for pleasure. I clawed her heart out with my bare hands." {n}Wenduag smiles.{/n}
- `7a01403db8f9ef842b7536cc4657de2e` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/WenduagCave/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "I'm not alone, Lann. My master has not left me. Even now I feel his cold breath on my neck." {n}Wenduag's voice drops to a whisper.{/n} "He is watching."
- `91f1b8cfd3a34c04c89eb6d830df6494` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/WenduagCave/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag chuckles.{/n} "So now you want them, want me... Back in the Maze, I understood your feelings, your ambitions correctly. But this spring has dried out for you, uplander. Now only death awaits you."
- `d6fd4094a7e58cd419abd0fd595e264c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/WenduagDefeated/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > "You... you are stronger than me, yes... this is the right of the strong — to decide who lives and who dies..." {n}Wenduag cringes.{/n} "You think you have the right to condemn me because some higher beings noticed you? No. Your strength gives you the right to decide... only your strength!"
- `8159024f70325bc49b234f354c98bba4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q1BackToTheRoots/WenduagDefeated/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag chuckles.{/n} "Big mistake — not wishing the enemy dead! Or maybe you will become my friend and {mf|master|mistress}?"
- `6f48ad08928b3344e8f4165889df6c4b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Lann/Q3The Last Resort/WenduagHelloAgain/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag silently walks up and folds her arms across her chest.{/n} "What do you know, you're still alive, Lann. And here I thought you would be lying dead in a ditch somewhere."
- `e7848c49599497e47b2ab3c8b66324c0` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/KTC_Wenduag_ComeNeathholm/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > …promised to bring the underground crusaders to the surface to help the crusades, but it seems he forgot his promise as soon as we left. Or maybe he's doing his favorite thing again — waiting for the right moment. Come to think of it, who knows what's been going on in Neathholm? Maybe the demons attacked again, or Sull died of a heart attack, leaving the tribe without a leader. That wouldn't actually be a bad thing — …
- `655ba34d16539524a8ce0b08ecbf1657` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_Duel/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > {n}Lann shrugs indifferently.{/n} "If I lose, I'll be dead and no longer a thorn in your side."
- `ad63b2fbc6afd3a4ba455b980bdaceaf` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_Duel/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "We will, and you will die alone."
- `f8e24525640fba24c9995e21df51b223` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_Duel/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > {n}Lann, looking desperately at his failure, cringes painfully.{/n} "You ruined everything! I was a step away from curing them... And you killed us all!"
- `66cafb1b7f0ed3c40a258f4bf02fe7fa` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_Duel/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "Thank you. Believe me, our tribe hasn't forgotten its duty as underground crusaders, but... right now, the tribe is in trouble. Mongrels are sick, and they can't go where there is bloodshed and death. And I can't go either — there's no one else to take care of my kin. I'd be willing to become your soldier in return for your mercy, but I have a responsibility here."
- `b17b5805cd281b248a6a7c2a99579ccc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_DuelLost/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag hisses angrily.{/n} "You are such an idiot! You have a death wish!"
- `ceaad6ef191d1a942bd7df279777629f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_DuelLost/Cue_0030.jbp`
  > …e obtained through violence, no matter how much you'd like to believe otherwise. You know it: the tribe eats only because someone dies. We'll have to fight for our place under the strange sun we never knew, we'll have to fight for our right to exist. Today the crusaders need us, but tomorrow, when the demons are defeated, we'll be suspicious strangers who are better avoided. That's why we are going after our own powe…
- `7b2fc8e9928f7ff4b9631c246abbba3e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_DuelLost/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > {n}Lann gives an aggrieved nod.{/n} "You're right. Turns out I am the enemy. And an enemy doesn't belong in the tribe. If I'm standing in the way of my people, my duty is to step away. Tell Sull I left, and I won't bother the tribe again. May their fires be hot, and their prey abundant."
- `c3b61fd86fc4a694cb4162824cc7d5bd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Lann_DuelLost/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag replies with mocking laughter.{/n} "I just kept you close because you were the next most skilled hunter in the tribe. It doesn't mean I felt anything for you, or considered you a friend. You're a dead weight dragging me down. So get out of my sight! I don't ever want to see you again, alive or dead!" {n}Wenduag strikes a blow with a desperate grunt.{/n}
- `3d2f09ab53308084890da31b3febe761` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/MongrelVendorWendu/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > {n}Hunching, Dyra looks away and blurts out,{/n} "Lann wanted potions. I go up to Kenabres sometimes, I buy potions and powders I've never seen in our caves from their wizards... They trade the potions for the prey our hunters kill in the caves — lizard skin, bile, chitin. Lann promised me that he would give his kills to me for a hundred gongs if I got warrior strength potions for him on the surface."
- `402c02e080003ec48bb81ad78b6ddfd9` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Answer_0037.jbp`
  > "Lann is dead."
- `f9c4b96e62b790840b39ea7883acac1f` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Answer_0047.jbp`
  > "Lann is dead."
- `a76607387008ebc4c835092bfbec38b2` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Answer_0069.jbp`
  > "I think you should know... Wenduag died."
- `b06f809f8b8379849932f41bf8d66082` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Cue_0044.jbp`
  > {n}Sull looks down with a painful grunt.{/n} "Horrible... What have you done to poor Lann...? Please, give ush what'sh left of him, we will bury him properly."
- `174a9940c9ccdc94d8409caf3e0b52e8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Cue_0059.jbp`
  > "Those of ush who are sentient will gather all the food and water we have, so that your wild warriorsh won't starve to death on the way. And we'll depart right away... I can sense the thirsht for blood in my fellow tribeshmen. They shouldn't be kept waiting for too long."
- `a830975d9fd4d044f9abca5347c4ca95` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Cue_0065.jbp`
  > {n}With a start, Sull looks at you with hope.{/n} "Really? He made it? I am so happy I won't have to see the death of my own tribe, the tribe entrushted to me by my ancestorsh. Neathholm will live... The neathers will live!"
- `4b72eb691d8438c4397e461264a259ff` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Cue_0067.jbp`
  > "Fight to shurvive and hide. Neathers musht not interfere with uplander mattersh — you jusht touched our tribe, and we are already a shtep away from death. I sh'pose we'll have to leave the village and go deeper into the cavesh, where no one will bother ush. The ancestorsh were wrong when they promised ush a return to the shurface — they didn't know what terrible fate would wait for ush there."
- `5ffb9a53e69b53f49ae09264eab26757` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/SullLannIsNeutralized/Cue_0070.jbp`
  > {n}Sull's eyes become dim like a soot-stained glass.{/n} "Dead? Well... I sensed this was going to happen. There washn't any place for kindnesh in her heart — not for othersh, not for herself. But I shtill loved her, and she was shtill one of ush. We will bury her remainsh, we will mourn her spirit, no matter how evil and cruel it was."
- `20a2201ef831d304bb5b4c2f96335a8c` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0009.jbp`
  > …shome whishpers in their headsh, calling them, luring them... As if their blood is calling them shomewhere. Those who were weaker left and never returned. And as they were leaving, they mumbled that 'he' would come soon, and all of ush would be his." {n}Sull shakes his head hopelessly.{/n} "I don't know who they are talking about."…
- `a1faa3f5f8c1f3044bb38a628fe71216` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0035.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag says softly,{/n} "I thought Lann would bring us trouble. We'll have to get rid of him. The cave he was talking about isn't far from here — we used to hide away from the tribe there, to spend some time alone. It's a good, quiet place... It will be easy to take care of him there without involving the rest of the tribe."
- `77a008e921be2404fa5734b119f717da` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > {n}Staring at you with devotion, Wenduag gives you a servile nod.{/n} "I will bring his severed head to your feet, {mf|master|mistress}!" {n}You notice that the anticipatory excitement is absent from Wenduag's eyes.{/n}
- `cd030a0f4b44d4e4ea6b8e6be7c60615` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0042.jbp`
  > "We don't have the time to fight every idiot with an opinion. Let Lann's death be a terrifying example for everyone else. Besides, Sull will understand that we are losing patience." {n}The hunter's face wears its usual bloodthirsty expression, but her voice sounds pensive and anxious rather than menacing.{/n}
- `f843d78d6d2dc7241869a64bfaf32f70` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "We are dying out. I hope you never have to see your family, your kin perish. The weak are shtarving to death in their hutsh, and those who should take care of them are rampaging in a blind bloodthirsht..."
- `aa73debd85be7c447bc70a8abe6d51be` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > "Look for yourshelf and tell ush, do you see an army here? I only see a bunch of old neathers, women and children, shtarving to death. All the huntersh, all the warriors, where are they? They were ruined by the demonic madnessh... Lann ish trying to cure them, he ish hiding in a cave nearby, fighting for their mad soulsh, but what can one lad do, even one as recklessh as him?"
- `82ea5ac7d471bf44383022c931bfb787` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_0056.jbp`
  > "Do uplandersh care about ush? And who would I send? Those few huntersh we had in the tribe are gone... They said they heard an ominoush whishper in their headsh. They said it called them to the cavesh, and they could not resisht. And they went, all of them, one after another. And they alsho said that shoon 'he' will come — and all of ush will be together again. And all of ush will be his." {n}Sull shakes his head ho…
- `ac9592cc405e4a91b2f0dbc8c422bedd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/Sull_IWantMyMongrels/Cue_3.jbp`
  > "I wanted to, sure, but not like this." {n}He points at the smiling Wenduag.{/n} "Just look at her. She'll either get us all killed or make us into demons' slaves, and I don't know which is worse."
- `84c150cf8b46c87428d35fe9efddb05d` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Answer_0064.jbp`
  > "Have you gone to anyone for help?"
- `2cd194d820e84184a804b3e0fa6b6f6b` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Answer_0071.jbp`
  > [Show the incriminating note] "Wenduag killed Dyra. We must execute her for this."
- `df0137027cd6f444facc8955468bd7e4` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Answer_0079.jbp`
  > "I found Dyra. She's dead."
- `2ee4e27381c1d704ba604fff7510c131` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Answer_0084.jbp`
  > "Looks like we'll never find out who killed Dyra."
- `7baa57201b4366142b27ccbba8b70236` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > {n}Sull's voice is stubborn, but with a hint of cautious.{/n} "You are young. You are shpiteful. You've seen little and know little. I'm shtill responshible for the tribe. And shtop threatening me, becaushe I am mightily old, which meansh I'm not afraid of death!"
- `ed0db7bec04a92b4eb391c309ff61822` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "You are wasting your threats, Wenduag... I'm old, death ish at my shoulder. I will crumble before you can even hurt me." {n}Licking his lips nervously, Sull looks at his former charge with deep-seated fear. She has placed her hand on her hunting knife, as if by accident.{/n} "All right, don't be mad at an old man, young Chief. Soon I'll die and shtop bothering you with my advice."
- `ab5eadbf4bf95474eb28a98a9a8b568a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > … around, looking about, as if she sensed a threat. She carried a bag with her all the time. She said she'd go to outer gates, and disappeared... And she didn't have the bag with her! Maybe that was why she was so scared? Did she shteal something? No, that can't be... Oh, you must look for the villain that killed poor Dyra!"…
- `ad3e3f8ed103d314f87d9fc0ef1a9101` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0050.jbp`
