  > "Shut up, old man. Yes, it was me. Dyra kept meddling in my affairs, so I killed her." {n}Wenduag looks at you, her eyes narrowed dangerously.{/n} "So what? Dyra was a nobody. {mf|Master|Mistress}, I can't believe you want to punish me for her death. Me, your loyal and invincible warrior?"
- `c04f08ae1806ab941864a97da25b90d3` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0052.jbp`
  > …taught them to obey, you'll see! And you, {mf|master|mistress}, will regret rejecting a useful servant like me!" {n}A ring you've never seen before appears on Wenduag's finger. With a quick twist of the ring, the hunter disappears with a soft clap.{/n}…
- `74d0c8cfde10b444e8f40af76d097e50` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0066.jbp`
  > "Yesh. Wenduag wouldn't look for her, so I went to the guardsh. They heard me out, nodded, said they'd look for her. But I don't know if I did the right thing. Perhapsh we should have gone directly to their chief? Or brought them giftsh? Godsh know what the cushtoms are among the uplandersh."
- `374eff2caacc8be4d91977183ed39b60` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0068.jbp`
  > "Of course she doesh... She said Dyra ran back to Neathholm, so scared she wash. But this ish not true. Dyra might not be the bravest of ush, but she ishn't afraid of the shurface as much as some, and she would have never left the tribe." {n}Sull frowns sorrowfully.{/n}
- `0aa3e21552d59cf418058e8bd0a553b7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0072.jbp`
  > {n}Squinting near-sightedly, Sull reads Dyra's scribbles slowly, almost syllable by syllable. With every word he reads, his face grows paler, and his lips start trembling. Finally he straightens up and cries out in a high-pitched, trembling voice,{/n} "Wenduag! Traitor! You killed one of your own!"
- `856cc053e90abf440a6dfaad2ab42417` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0080.jbp`
  > {n}The old man sighs sorrowfully.{/n} "Dead? It can't be! But how...? Who would dare harm her? Oh no... There will be no songsh by the fire tonight, there will be no shtories or laughter. Today we shall mourn... But what happened? Dyra never hurt anyone."
- `b95cd4967554fff43b3edaaf76e32cbb` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0082.jbp`
  > {n}Sull sighs grievously.{/n} "I don't know. She disappeared, no one saw her. I sense that shomething very bad, shomething evil, has happened..."
- `4bc2a4664f59d8b488a369a72e8c1e91` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduAndSull/Cue_0085.jbp`
  > {n}A tragic grimace appears on Sull's face.{/n} "There will be fewer headsh by the fire... Fewer shtrong huntersh, wise herbalishts, loud children. We will melt, like ice in a pot. Disappear. We are already disappearing. Well, thish must be fate. Thank you for at leasht trying to find the culprit."
- `69f9faf3dbd244348ac31a29f640f3f1` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduTrainingIntervention/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > "That's better... You will remember who your queen is. You will only listen to me and obey without thinking. And to anyone who stands in our way — death and blood!"
- `e9c227b3aa672f844a1ec72ebe4f18a2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduTrainingIntervention/Cue_0027.jbp`
  > "The chief's hand must be firm. I need to remind my warriors why I have the power of life and death over them. If they doubt my right to lead them, even for a moment, the tribe will fall."
- `4b10d5efcf0debb4fb979f606c4f65b4` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduTrainingIntervention/Cue_0031.jbp`
  > {n}The mongrels glance at each other in surprise. They look at you warily now, and all their guarded aggression disappears, replaced with quiet obedience.{/n}
- `9e1162d12a2d9c147a7b50724bcbd247` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduTrainingIntervention/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "Blood and death!"
- `55040177faa5a13479b02937d02d063a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduTrainingIntervention/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > "Even you, the strongest warriors of the tribe, don't dare challenge me. Because you are my army! And you know that I bring blood and death to my enemies." {n}Wenduag ostentatiously ignores the mongrel standing opposite her — large and wiry, covered with scars.{/n} "Those who do not obey me..."
- `05138fe786241bb4985fde19847a6738` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q1_UndergroundArmy/WenduTrainingIntervention/Cue_0060.jbp`
  > "Warriors, kill {mf|him|her} if you can. It's time for me to seek new allies." {n}A golden ring you haven't seen before gleams on Wenduag's finger. Twisting it, the hunter disappears.{/n}
- `bc68c42b2c6ff60408358dfd9ebc34c1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/AfterBattle_SavamelechsManshion/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag wipes away the black streaks left by her tears.{/n} "I'm feeling better, {mf|master|mistress}. My mind is clear."
- `564b864b68dbb914eba93171b331d273` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/AfterBattle_SavamelechsManshion/Cue_0020.jbp`
  > "You're probably right, he won't come to Drezen openly... If he dares to take my army, I'll shove his own stinger down his throat!"
- `ad3cbe630b2edc744a424be8919a3b4e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/AfterBattle_SavamelechsManshion/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > "No, {mf|master|mistress}, we neathers don't fight to commemorate our dead. They are in the past, and we fight for our today and tomorrow."
- `d903579e47b01cc408062def3f2eaf87` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/AfterBattle_SavamelechsManshion/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag jerks her chin up, saying decisively,{/n} "Death comes for all. Life shouldn't be wasted on weeping for the dead."
- `38f9642c83e1ee84584ba82714bc82b9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/AfterBattle_SavamelechsManshion/Cue_0037.jbp`
  > "Just like many others — he went hunting and disappeared. That kind of thing is common in the caves. We didn't know it was because of Savamelekh's servants, not wild predators. And then, when I served Hosilla, I wasn't... let in on the secret." {n}A grim silence hangs after Wenduag's words, filled with the promise of a cruel death.{/n}
- `58d935390d9efc842814c1568ee72901` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/AfterBattle_SavamelechsManshion/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > {n}With a sudden smile, Wenduag nods.{/n} "Of course I did. Can you imagine the reward Savamelekh would give me for your death? Too bad I couldn't deceive you, but... I'll get to you... one day." {n}Twisting an unfamiliar gold ring on her finger, Wenduag disappears.{/n}
- `12cc7f29179c0094381468a2505d6b39` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/SavaAndWendu/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > "You are strong, much stronger than your father was. You'd make a good tool... But if you are willing to serve me, why didn't you come sooner? Why didn't you come alone, without this company of strangers?"
- `efb539e53978fd648a1f75113a395cfd` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/SavaAndWendu/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "No, no. You've killed enough of my servants, Commander, and I will not let you take another useful tool from me. I am claiming her as I will one day claim all the others."
- `ab8618ae2041c6247ac5f39fa5114d89` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/Savamelech_Welcome/Answer_0010.jbp`
  > "Where did you get these mongrels from? Didn't you flee Neathholm alone with your tail between your legs?"
- `721daaf7b66e87f4b9bbe03573f7663b` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/Savamelech_Welcome/Cue_0025.jbp`
  > "So stupid. He is a good servant, one of the best. You know how many mortals he has killed for me? How he tore them into pieces with his bare hands? He has good blood. And his daughter does too. They both will be mine, as those before them were, as those after them will be."
- `e4d2fcd1fc8494a42ae59c61e2490ca2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q2_CallOfBlood/Savamelech_Welcome/Cue_0037.jbp`
  > "Oh no, Commander. While I did receive mythic power from my master, I'm not in the mood to fight now. It's time for me to visit my children in Drezen, the city you abandoned. Without me, they are wandering in darkness. But I will give them power. Too bad you won't be able to follow me. We'll literally be worlds apart!"
- `5a4ae8daa55606c40a53f52b6c8a1fde` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/FinalSava/Answer_0019.jbp`
  > "Savamelekh, take your mongrels and let's just leave each other alone."
- `091eb57d01d0bae439d5dc079485c4b2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/FinalSava/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > …panions. My spy." {n}The demon turns to Wenduag.{/n} "My daughter, you have performed your task well. Now there is only one thing left for you to do. Kill your former {mf|master|mistress} and bring me {mf|his|her} head — and you will gain my dark benevolence. You will be foremost among my creations, and I will give you a place at my side."…
- `d0ce0b6f00b33f348bc22337168deb9a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/FinalSava/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > …mble to their deaths. It is the smile of the raging ocean when a drunken sailor drowns in its depths. It is a smile that promises death.{/n}…
- `a86def7ea4387344dbbd8ffcec68bf0d` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Answer_0010.jbp`
  > "What will happen to the mongrels now that Savamelekh is dead?"
- `df6e283fe2adb0448b19ebfc33baf278` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Answer_0047.jbp`
  > "Your master is dead. The mongrels no longer have a leader."
- `02b067cfb99db2f4a891a53ea08a75ce` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Answer_0080.jbp`
  > [Attack] "I should have killed you long ago. You keep getting in my way!"
- `cb902a44eb528464a8545aad951c1ccf` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Answer_0095.jbp`
  > "If you cared so much about your tribe, why did you abandon them when they left Neathholm? You weren't there for them when they needed you most."
- `6562b6014ea6a704faf961457f6de04c` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Answer_0159_FinalRomWithWendu.jbp`
  > [Stay alone with Wenduag] "Fine."
- `5256f953a5c63544ca946c5f65e45091` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > …looks down wearily at Savamelekh's mangled corpse.{/n} "You thought we were your playthings. So how does it feel to be stabbed to death by your little tin soldiers? We will no longer allow others to treat us with contempt and disrespect. We know what it is to feel pain, fear, and loneliness. We have suffered and bled... and now we will choose our own fate. No one, not even our 'creator' can decide our destinies for u…
- `111542303d1b01344bc4dd8ced20b481` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag stares at Savamelekh's mangled corpse. The huntress looks scarcely better than the dead demon. Although her wounds are not fatal, she is badly hurt.{/n} "It looks like you failed, creator. In the end, you still weren't strong enough to save us. Soon the neathers will follow you into oblivion..."
- `b1ade5aea490e1d41922c58ed364d277` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "I don't know, {mf|master|mistress}. Savamelekh made us who we are... but now he's dead, and all his plots and plans are gone with him. We'll have to survive on our own now — without the protection of the Shield Maze. But we are no longer cursed with short lifespans. So who knows? Perhaps we'll manage to claim a piece of land on the surface. Maybe we can settle there, and create a kingdom of our own."
- `36b7587a1a5390944a067ac33befed84` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0049.jbp`
  > …ld. I've been scheming since day one. All this time, I've been plotting to overthrow you. I trained my fighters to obey me and me alone. I eliminated anyone who posed a threat to my plans. In Alushinyrra, I secretly contacted Savamelekh and made a deal. This whole time, you had no idea that I was working behind your back. Your blind trust made you weak, and it was only natural that I should try and exploit that weakn…
- `877c4b6c3a1547c42ad50e39020bd015` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0050.jbp`
  > …tool, a means to an end. You may have been my {mf|master|mistress}, but that doesn't mean I owe you anything. We all live and die alone... I look out for myself, and no one else."…
- `eb0a3b6237411434294d79c25ace2238` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > {n}The huntress smiles bitterly.{/n} "I'm no longer a threat to you. How could I be? My plan failed, my patron is dead, and I have no one else to turn to... Yes, I know how it sounds. But it's the truth."
- `3d713907f31c0f7428fc5091cf9c3a6f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0081.jbp`
  > "After I left Neathholm, I started hunting demons. I know it sounds stupid, but I thought I was doing something important... Although, hand on heart, I was really just looking for an enemy to chop my head off. But then I heard the news that Savamelekh had abducted the people of my tribe from Drezen, so I set out looking for him. Luckily, it's not too difficult for a skilled hunter to track down an army of angry mongr…
- `4c78cb6b5664173448bb5d80d351d78f` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0082.jbp`
  > "So you came here to 'save' your tribe? You thought you could take down Savamelekh all by yourself? You're so stupid, Lann! If we hadn't shown up, you'd be dead! Aside from appeasing your conscience, you would have accomplished nothing..."
- `e7872dcfac7b8ce47a36ce960c413646` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0086.jbp`
  > "Now Savamelekh, our dark father, is dead, which makes us orphans, I guess... I will stay with my people and protect them, no matter what fate they choose. Whether they decide to march beneath your banners, hide in the caves, or choose another patron, I'll stay with them until the end."
- `0fad66380e0b32a4c8c2a0d409da8596` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0091.jbp`
  > "As you wish. I will never abandon my tribe again, but I will leave you alone. Farewell, and thanks again for saving us."
- `06fea04ef92547c4696853b9f0a1ead6` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0099.jbp`
  > "No, I wasn't. I abandoned my tribe, and the blood of everyone who died today is on my conscience. But I am trying hard to make amends, and I'll keep on trying. I hope that someday, they'll forgive me."
- `e8cc16747336c3340a80a68aceddbe49` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0101.jbp`
  > "We're {d|c0 angel}going{/d} back to Drezen immediately to continue our service. This time, we won't disappear on you."
- `dccc7513c9e64734fa60cf97161cd946` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0127.jbp`
  > {n}The huntress turns her gaze on you, and a wry smile appears on her lips. She doesn't seem to notice her wounds, and when she speaks, her voice is surprisingly calm.{/n} "If my plan had worked, {mf|master|mistress}, think of how much power your death would have brought me. I risked my life for my ambition, but it was a risk worth taking."
- `e0a0bc80863383a4cb2e7ded6adc4541` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0138.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag scowls darkly.{/n} "Are you trying to teach me a lesson, or are you just too squeamish to stain your hands with the blood of a former companion? But fine. I won't hold a grudge. I promise to stay away from you. You'll never see me again. Farewell."
- `4e6da841870f79e41bb440ff478482d4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0144.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag hisses in frustration as she watches the members of her tribe depart.{/n} "You stupid, useless cowards! I'm ashamed to call you my kin! I've wasted so much time and effort on you, and now you flee, with your tails tucked between your legs!"
- `15b73a56ef6526a46ae97200c014d867` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0147.jbp`
  > "I've been training the Neathholm fighters from the moment they joined your army. I taught them to obey me and me alone. When the time came, they were going to turn on your crusaders and stab them in their sleep. Did you know about that? No, you didn't."
- `670a32b702f87ac4c8cc80b47fc63b00` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0149.jbp`
  > "I had to slit several throats to keep my schemes a secret. {g|Dyra}Dyra's{/g} disappearance upset the tribe and the guards made it impossible for me to hide her body. I had to leave her corpse in the middle of the street. At the time, I was worried that I would be discovered and my plot would be exposed. Luckily, however, you weren't interested in investigating the murder."
- `34c82b7ed73909347a58e0a2d2a17f30` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0153.jbp`
  > "I guess I'd be dead then. Better to be dead meat with a clear conscience than to be alive with a heart tainted by treachery."
- `ff0e531559ebf994ca40883649e7bd2d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionQuests/Wenduag/Q3_TraitorsBlood/SavaIsDeadNow/Cue_0156.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag smiles triumphantly as she looks at Savamelekh's corpse. When she speaks, her voice is low and hoarse.{/n} "We... Can we stay here a while longer? I'd like to talk to you... alone."
- `a4c7d27ec3117114683fbec7c5b7b413` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Jealousy/Answer_0025.jbp`
  > "The very best should belong to me and me alone. Wenduag, you are the very best, and you are mine."
- `570ec5939426bc4429347eed57184fd7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Jealousy/Cue_0004.jbp`
  > "{name}! We need to talk!" {n}Wenduag snarls, baring her teeth seemingly without even realizing it.{/n} "No more uncertainty! It was irritating, and then it was infuriating, and now it's driving me out of my mind! You know that I am the only one worthy of standing at your side! The rest of them — they're all weaklings! So tell them that! Make them understand that you choose me and me alone!"
- `3235af45abc085f45a8a37ac851ad790` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Jealousy/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag snarls, baring her teeth seemingly without even realizing it.{/n} "No more uncertainty! It was irritating, and then it was infuriating, and now it's driving me out of my mind! You know that I am the only one worthy of standing at your side! The rest of them — they're all weaklings! So tell them that! Make them understand that you choose me and me alone!"
- `9ba13c8de198cac46b356e21dd29966b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/AfterSex/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}Among the variety of battle marks, a rather gruesome ragged scar on her left collarbone catches your eye — it's an uneven line that goes along her shoulder, skirting it and ending at the shoulder blade. Several long scars stretching across Wenduag's back suggest she has been whipped.{/n}
- `8d171811e901fea47a1077af04859379` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/AfterSex/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > "Punished? Mmm, you could say that, yes. I was a very naughty girl, and I was punished for it. But I've also left my fair share of scars on others."
- `6df7eed5a86c21c42b8abd0749334f72` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/AfterSex/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > "Ha, no. He never stood a chance — I saw the rat chew right through his spine. But he did have a nice axe and a decent bow on him when he died, and the tribe can't afford to lose good weapons. You need weapons to hunt for food. So, I didn't go after the rat to save a friend, I went to get back the axe and bow. And that's what I did."
- `c32211ac952012945bfe7a8a0c4ba5c0` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/AfterSex/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "It jumped at me, jaws snapping — and the next thing I see is my whole arm disappearing into its maw. My spear got smashed to pieces, and the chunk of wood that I was holding in my hand got stuck inside its throat. The beast was wheezing, writhing, hopping around me on its hind legs, trying to bite my arm off, but I just held the wood of my spear tighter while I groped for the knife on my belt. And as soon as I did —…
- `07f07d3ef49d84f4c98993300d90eb69` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/AfterSex/Cue_0054.jbp`
  > "He squealed like you wouldn't believe... All the young rats in the caves were mourning the loss of those gray family jewels that night." {n}With her eyes half-closed, Wenduag savors the pleasant memories.{/n} "It was dying for half a gong, tried to get on its feet again six times, the long-toothed beast, but I just knocked it back down on the rocks till it finally kicked the bucket. And only then could I get my arm …
- `69876b62dfd008c4e844a491625bf835` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/AfterSex/Cue_0056.jbp`
  > "You think so? I think it looks quite attractive. You can tell right away that this scar was left by a formidable enemy."
- `4e313a69e7f288940afd3cf47109013f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/Autostart/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Stretching sinuously, Wenduag says with affected displeasure,{/n} "The cold up here on this surface of yours is even worse than back in the caves. Fall asleep in cold like that and you might not wake up again... What a stupid and inglorious way to die — freezing, curled up in your bedroll, instead of perishing in bloody battle. That's why back home I made sure never to sleep alone."
- `638d735f8853594469c72d3a0dab1d17` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/Autostart/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag gives you an understanding nod, but the coaxing undercurrent doesn't disappear from her voice.{/n} "As you wish, {mf|master|mistress}. This is your decision today, but tomorrow... you just might change your mind."
- `2b2ec9d6442d61c4487cb0553647c250` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/Autostart/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag nods playfully.{/n} "It's all right, I can wait. When you start feeling lonely, just let me know."
- `417b7274277dbf246b032378a71e9b49` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/CantUnderstand/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > …ith a frown, Wenduag replies in a dull voice,{/n} "A hunter must be respected. A hunter fights so the tribe can survive. A hunter dies first, so the tribe can live on. Those uplanders aren't my tribe, and if they despise me and laugh at me, why should I fight for their lives? Why should I die? If only I'd known that for my blood I'd get paid in mockery, I would have thought twice before leaving the caves." {n}Now you…
- `dd397547948c2e045a2130b0bc0a2f56` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/CantUnderstand/Cue_0026.jbp`
  > {n}The huntress answers with a furious growl.{/n} "Don't you dare pity me! I am Wenduag, the best hunter in Neathholm! I am a fierce predator and the whole world is my hunting ground! And I don't need pity from anyone! I don't give a damn what they say, let alone what they think! I am strong, and they are just pathetic!"
- `904b25c9eb006f648940964d711f6316` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/CantUnderstand/Cue_0034.jbp`
  > "There's just one question left to answer... What should I do with this dagger now?" {n}An elegant weapon with a blackened blade appears in Wenduag's hands.{/n}
- `109b37f064a548f43ba3b84124a860e2` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/CantUnderstand/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "So? Should I have left it to that one-eyed waste of space instead?"
- `3016670a82ba0d74fb50f8e1cf872fc3` — BlueprintAnswer; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/GoToFinal/Answer_0004_FinalRomWithWendu.jbp`
  > [Stay alone with Wenduag] "Fine."
- `b0e8df9cb0edaad4a9481b8a3d2460c8` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Answer_0019.jbp`
  > "This whole fuss is all because of a couple of slain soldiers? That's why you brought me here?"
- `d7a47e224a7f4664aa217beb3bd06126` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth growls furiously.{/n} "You've killed two men — there are witnesses! Do you even realize that such a crime is punishable by death?"
- `e535b1585cd75ee499d2dac026b15c45` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > {n}Irabeth hisses through gritted teeth.{/n} "Ha! Doesn't affect her? Please! She starts drunken brawls here every night! Every time she gets wasted, she starts picking fights! I had a bad feeling this was going to happen. I knew from the start that someone was going to wind up dead!"
- `c8bc3d12ec7d52a42aca938c8cea174a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > {n}The gathered crowd awaits your verdict in respectful silence. Some of them glance anxiously in Wenduag's direction. The huntress stands alone, her lips curved into an insolent smile.{/n}
- `586a91fc4ad57d3478399e24164bcdde` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/HavocInTheTavern/Cue_0047.jbp`
  > "What? You're going to have me killed over a few lousy corpses? You scum! After all I've done to serve you!"
- `3a23deacd9524254e9bd48ac078fda22` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/KTC_TroublesInTheTavern/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "Commander! We've got trouble — your companion, Wenduag, she's gone berserk. She wreaked havoc in the tavern, there are casualties. You must intervene — or allow me to handle it myself."
- `c13f80b23d314f44598fe6fa61ed8457` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/KTC_TroublesInTheTavern/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > "A drunken brawl that turned into a stabbing spree. By the time I left the tavern, two people had already succumbed to their injuries. I can only pray to Iomedae that the number of dead won't rise while I am away."
- `929e9aa93bfb7934e97a3e48be9919e4` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/PersonalPunishment/Answer_0029.jbp`
  > "You shouldn't have killed them. Try to restrain yourself in the future."
- `1953a3be2dc02a948945b5ab8b39e69d` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/PersonalPunishment/Answer_0039.jbp`
  > "You killed two innocent men on a whim. They can't be brought back to life. Both of them had friends and families, and you hurt them all... for what?"
- `759ab129c088cb2418e442c15ebf23ab` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/PersonalPunishment/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "Of course I do, but I want to feel weak in your hands. Do you know what arouses me the most? Your voice, when you're promising imminent death to our enemies. That is the voice of a real leader, the voice of a ruler. And I want to hear you talk to me like that."
- `380ada90ffb2fc9498271931d73d726a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/PersonalPunishment/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag stays silent for a while, and then says in a cold, dull voice,{/n} "My play makes you nervous? That's too bad. I suppose I should leave you alone, then."
- `a56421543033d454eb2166cf104f9491` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/PersonalPunishment/Cue_0022.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag's fingers carefully brush over the fresh bruises on her wrists.{/n} "I love it when I have marks on my body after sex. The ache reminds me of the moments when you gave them to me. So, do you still want to know why I killed those two in the tavern?"
- `a51c4f1cf80984d42974d5b399451959` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/PersonalPunishment/Cue_0059.jbp`
  > "Fine, I understand. I guess you're right. Forgive me. I will do my best not to anger you and not to cause you any more trouble. Thank you for sparing my pride and not chastising me in front of everyone... {mf|master|mistress}." {n}The last drops of playful mischief disappear from Wenduag's voice. The bow she offers is stilted and speaks of weariness.{/n} "If you don't mind, I'll leave now."
- `bd61f09dbd14b6b4a9559113f24569cb` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SomeThingsAboutMyFather/Answer_0013.jbp`
  > "I won't abandon you like your father did. I promise."
- `47904a3e75dd74743812fe1ad165fc53` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SomeThingsAboutMyFather/Cue_0005.jbp`
  > … in a pool of blood..." {n}She notices your confused expression, and adds,{/n} "My father. He was one of Savamelekh's warriors. I mourned his death so long ago. I barely even remember him. But now that I've seen him again, I can't get him out of my head."…
- `c48a802f0a5b80947ba4d6c6282864fd` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SomeThingsAboutMyFather/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "He was important. He made people listen to him without raising his voice. He knew how to lead. I followed him around everywhere, especially after my mother died. But he kept his distance from everyone... even me. He never let anyone forget that he was a chief. I decided that I wanted to be like him when I grew up. But now... I don't think I ever really understood him."
- `4801ca9d399f22f409defca3cff7ab8b` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SomeThingsAboutMyFather/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "He went hunting and never came back. It happens. I cried for so many gongs. I just couldn't stop. Our healer said that I nearly died from grief — I couldn't breathe... I refused to move. I just lay there like a corpse... I couldn't understand why he had abandoned me. I mean, I knew what death was back then, but I hadn't come to terms with what it meant. He was my whole world, and... I couldn't forgive him for leavin…
- `e903eee323fd39044bc459286bec8cb9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SomeThingsAboutMyFather/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "Of course. Everyone admired him, everyone revered him... but no one looked up to him more than me. It was a great honor to be his daughter. I never dreamed of surpassing him. I just wanted to be worthy of him..." {n}Wenduag's face twists into a grimace.{/n} "But I have surpassed him. Otherwise, I'd be lying on the floor of that manor in the Abyss. I would be dead instead of him."
- `189513e8b9494d74a916af0d7fe10e02` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SullAboutWenduag/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "You know yourshelf what I'm referring to. Shavamelekh, our creator, our origin. How is one to opposhe such a thing? The courage one musht have to shtand up to him, to become the shlayer of their own progenitor... She will need a pillar. And I hope you'll become one for her. Wenduag wandersh in darknessh, she needsh a light to walk toward."
- `56823b667a969d5489f396e4174e4327` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SullAboutWenduag/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "Take care of her. Talk to her. Don't make her go through thish alone."
- `6c6ebc7872db4f849a529e5669ed58b1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/SullAboutWenduag/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > …to her heart. She was preparing hershelf for the day when everyone would leave her, like her father did. She wash preparing to be alone."…
- `79faa1cae6481e8449af72fcad07a36e` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Answer_0004.jbp`
  > "Does the sight of a dead demon turn you on?"
- `190ab8b7274ab6241a6e710052bd8623` — BlueprintAnswer; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Answer_0093.jbp`
  > "So, you almost killed me..."
- `aacd2739d30721b42a08bd669d4aefac` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}The huntress's voice is a soft purr.{/n} "Alone at last."
- `8ffa8da05b0eece4fb3b28a8c6af147a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0018.jbp`
  > "The poison contains Savamelekh's power. This is the poison our ancestors tasted, and they became the first of our kind. The demon gave his poison to everyone who swore to serve him. When you try it for the first time, it feels like two mad scorpions have clawed their way into your brain through your ears and have begun a fight to the death inside your head."
- `19cfb9736ef327942af5593f554f2ca4` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag tries to smile.{/n} "Right. What am I waiting for? After all, I'm sitting right next to you. I might as well stab you now." {n}Another spasm causes your body to convulse violently, and Wenduag's smile vanishes. She looks at you with an expression of sheer terror.{/n}
- `0dbf18c26dc933a4aa73cb09329d1b6a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > "I never tried to comfort you. Comforting words are for weaklings." {n}Wenduag smiles nervously.{/n} "Don't fool yourself. I'm the same wicked, lying, traitorous bitch that I was before. I still don't care about anyone but myself, but I am a little glad that you didn't just drop dead."
- `28f85ba6043930943bdccb09659bce01` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0064.jbp`
  > "Of course. Let's go." {n}Wenduag turns to Savamelekh's corpse, and hisses spitefully,{/n} "Take a look, you winged filth. First, my lover killed you, and now {mf|he|she} has taken your power. You should never have messed with {mf|him|her}!"
- `546368576fc08334a8b94b43bffcba14` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0075.jbp`
  > "Don't you dare! You hear me? Don't you dare leave me here alone! Stay awake, keep talking! You understand me, you damned uplander? Just hold on!" {n}Wenduag shakes you frantically. Her claws pierce your skin as she grasps your shoulders, but you no longer feel any pain.{/n}
- `994224f720c57ae4696d90077d4e3dae` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0082.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag looks you in the eye. Her voice is soft.{/n} "I don't want to think about all the battles you'll have to fight... all the ways you could be killed. I... worry about you. What a strange, ridiculous feeling."
- `434440803b5ea20418dc20a679ddaad2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/ThatIsFinal/Cue_0097.jbp`
  > "Don't you see? We are special. There is no one else like us in the entire world. We're the only ones left — all the others are dead. Look into my eyes. What do you see?" {n}Wenduag brings her face close to yours, and you see yourself reflected in her huge, cat-like eyes.{/n} "When I look into your eyes, I see myself."
- `01ea313a758e6344f8ba610ce7ec6e69` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/TooMuchStress/Cue_0075.jbp`
  > …cause who am I to resist him? And that means my fate is no longer in my own hands. What happens to me next depends on you and you alone, so I have to trust you."…
- `530493fc65e8d4449b3dbe48dc7d0c22` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/TooMuchStress/Cue_0076.jbp`
  > "But I don't know how to trust! I've always been alone, all by myself! The only person I trusted was my father, but he... he..." {n}Angry tears roll down Wenduag's cheeks.{/n} "He didn't even recognize me!" {n}With a sob, Wenduag burrows her head into your chest — no longer the tough huntress, but a lonely little girl who's lost in the dark, dank caves.{/n} "And now I'm all wet... What a pathetic sight. So what shoul…
- `76bb00d0a11d8304f8d03284562cfe76` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/TooMuchStress/Cue_0099.jbp`
  > {n}With soft eyes and a softer smile, Wenduag carefully runs her claws down your shoulders. Gone is the painful passion, but now there's something else in her touch, something new. Trust, acceptance, and warmth.{/n} "I want you, but not like I did before. I don't want to play with power, pain, and helplessness today." {n}Her whisper teases you, and her nimble fingers confidently remove your clothes.{/n} "This time, y…
- `e9bd549b43063ad4d9d7f1adb289c7fd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/Companions/CompanionRomances/Wenduag/YanielsDeath/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag's body stiffens slightly.{/n} "You felt like it? You killed her, a potential ally, just for fun? I appreciate cruelty and violence in a leader, but this is just gore for the sake of it. I'll hope you don't choke me to death in bed one of these days just because you feel like it."
- `1f7d9e2e6fe54554952e083a7fd453a7` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC1_Megaepic/TresholdOutdoor/Demon_SideQuest/Shamira/Cue_0007.jbp`
  > "The penalty for betrayal is death? How fortunate it is that we did not enforce that rule before now. Wouldn't you agree, Wenduag?"
- `0e7776f314ed42fa868ca24c496e8985` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC6_ADanceOfMasks/TavernFinal/WenduagRomance/Wenduag_Labyrith/Cue_0013.jbp`
  > "The past called me here. It spoke to me through the stones, luring me in. So many memories are buried here." {n}The light from the torches paints Wenduag's face in streaks of yellow and black, like some kind of barbaric mask.{/n}
- `969872cb85b3485596a17408b3fd26a6` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC6_ADanceOfMasks/TavernFinal/WenduagRomance/Wenduag_Labyrith/Cue_0055.jbp`
  > "I admit, it would be a shame to die here, buried under the rubble. But what if the assassin had something that would let her slip free from the snare, leaving her prey all alone... Something to make her disappear..." {n}You see a plain gold ring glinting on Wenduag's finger. She has never worn it before.{/n} "Why do you think I invited you here?"
- `81587fced28b44f29b4bc276d7b24d5d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/DLC6_ADanceOfMasks/TavernFinal/WenduagRomance/Wenduag_Labyrith/Cue_0063.jbp`
  > {n}The dark shadow leaves Wenduag's face and her voice returns to normal.{/n} "I am just toying with you. Of course I wouldn't hurt you. And I'd kill anyone who would. You..." {n}Wenduag falters and then says, quietly and seriously,{/n} "There are many things in the world that are worth someone's death. But killing for you isn't just worth it, it's... the right thing to do."
- `86bf0569a9029ae4b8c9d300a41e5739` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0409.jbp`
  > Wenduag took the Commander's death coldly and calmly. Only the keenest observers could tell that from that day on, Wenduag valued her own life even less than before, charging bravely into any battle, as if begging death to send an arrow that would strike her right in the heart. Within a scant few years, her pleas were answered.
- `8f6017d8d3bc5ec46b15773947e80c70` — slide; name; `World/Dialogs/Epilogues/Cue_0410.jbp`
  > After the war, the huntress {d|Wenduag Ignored}left{/d} the Commander's service and, having cobbled together a band of ruthless mercenaries out of veteran crusaders, went to the River Kingdoms to conquer one of them for herself. For a handful of years, she ruled over a dozen villages, until the local lords joined forces to put an end to her reign.
- `9db44fea457ac5249863043df02d42ec` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MeetLann/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag huffs a breath.{/n} "It's a fool's errand. None of us will be able to hold the sword, let alone use it to save anyone. It's not an ordinary weapon — it's made from righteous {g|Heaven}heavenly{/g} flame and will burn anyone who touches it. Do you think you're special, Lann?"
- `815d7fc06d1a9e54c9b5d86b434e2bda` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MeetLann/Cue_0051.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag rewards Lann with an irritated glare.{/n} "Fifty thousand gongs ago, our forebears found a dead {g|angel}angel{/g} here along with the bodies of his comrades. The tribe gave them a dignified burial, and they were laid to rest with their weapons — but the flaming angelic sword was stuck in a rock and no one was able to pull it out. It burned to the touch, like real fire. So the rock was placed over the ange…
- `24489883451abeb4ca0643ae181dbb72` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MeetLann/Cue_0058.jbp`
  > "In {g|Kenabres}Kenabres{/g} they're called the mongrels. People say that they come up to the surface at night and eat anyone foolish enough to wander alone after midnight." {n}Seelah turns to Lann and Wenduag.{/n} "To tell you the truth, I thought you guys were just a tale to tell kids at night."
- `833fef963fa6f5540add186d744af26f` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/CorruptedMongrels/Cue_0016.jbp`
  > "Right." {n}Wenduag isn't looking at you.{/n} "Now do you see why I didn't want Lann and the others to come here? They shouldn't know what happens to the people who venture here. The ones we killed today were mourned long ago. Let's keep it that way."
- `783cef6c744197b478379a5655fd9f96` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "Savamelekh is always in need of new servants. You'd make as good a servant as anyone else. And then..." {n}Wenduag bursts into laughter.{/n} "Once you'd gone missing in the Maze, not even the redoubtable Lann would've dared look for you."
- `0340edac7ee034d40868db4e5ba68c61` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0020.jbp`
  > "You're saying I should have died that day, Lann? But then who would have listened to your feeble jokes? Who would have showed you the best caves for hunting?" {n}A predatory gleam appears in Wenduag's eyes.{/n} "Who would have made you a man?"
- `5d6de4c7962e9fa4daecb35dc63be648` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > "I would have been fine without all that. And without you. Your death will make the world a slightly better place, Wenduag."
- `46f91ff646e06614f906a3da4e76dc53` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag chuckles.{/n} "Go, Lann, the surface is the best place for you! I'll be gaining true power, while you'll wander as an outcast. {name}, you will come to regret this decision, but by then it will be too late! Lann will never give you what I can!"
- `0cfe845c7c3c13d4da3fc1abda8057b9` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0053.jbp`
  > "You will regret this, uplander, but by then it will be too late." {n}Wenduag leaps toward a barely visible crevice in the rockface and vanishes in the shadows.{/n}
- `425ed3b8013528a4f9b1a57475bc09bd` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0066.jbp`
  > "You're saying I should have died that day, Lann? But then who would have listened to your feeble jokes? Who would have showed you the best caves for hunting?" {n}A predatory gleam appears in Wenduag's eyes.{/n} "Who would have made you a man?"
- `df83d8c582058f54384ad8c75b5ff63d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0067.jbp`
  > "I would have been fine without all that. And without you. Your death will make the world a slightly better place, Wenduag."
- `e716523e2be1c4747812fa82087e9bcc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0118.jbp`
  > "Savamelekh is always in need of new servants. You'd make as good a servant as anyone else. And then..." {n}Wenduag bursts into laughter.{/n} "Once you'd gone missing in the Maze, not even the redoubtable Lann would've dared look for you."
- `37490ab6798da804fa06779308de2d26` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/LannVenduChoose/Cue_0128.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag bares her fangs.{/n} "So that's your final word? Then I have nothing else to offer you but death. We will meet again, and you won't enjoy our next encounter."
- `757257afe28a090438c1204ab78a8605` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/SulComesLabyrinth/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}Sull laughs, but it comes out as a rasp.{/n} "Ah, Lann... we don't waste wordsh on people who don't listen to the tribe. You act the fool, you shuffer the consequences." {n}Sull gives a ragged sigh.{/n} "Wenduag... How could she... She deceived them and then she left... What'sh become of ush?"
- `07eb5aa03382f9943ac3e8ab9735bc26` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/NeathholmVillage/LannWakeUp/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "The messenger returned. The tribes are gathering at the entrance to the Maze, our people have already gone there. I've warned Anevia and that old guy who won't even acknowledge me. Take your time, there's no rush — the most important people always show up late." {n}Lann looks around.{/n} "Everything's going to plan, but one thing's bothering me: Wenduag's {d|c0 true mongrels}gone{/d}."
- `0184eae947a47f349b11726c4f72fe13` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/NeathholmVillage/VendougWakeUp/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag frowns.{/n} "What? Oh, them... they're probably dead already, but if we meet anyone on the way, we won't just walk on by. At the end of the day, they are of the tribe."
- `50c1ff8dd50fdcb4aa35d39979e643ba` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/NeathholmVillage/VendougWakeUp/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > …e Maze, but, every time, I've smelled the change in the air. Anyone who can't has no business being in there — they're as good as dead. If you don't believe me, you can wait for another guide to show up. But no one apart from me will get you where you need to go."…
- `c52a7107c2e021d438839b254a4f7911` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/VendougDontTell/Cue_0011.jbp`
  > …o understands how dangerous this is for the tribe. Lann's the only one who benefits from these childish games of heroism. I'll go alone if I have to and find them, or whatever's left of them. Without any heroics, relying only on myself. Risking only my own life." {n}Wenduag slows.{/n} "You and your friends... you can come with me. Perhaps we can make it to the end of the Maze together and find the way out to the surf…
- `18f02f57f87e69e449b4639def922b40` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/EstrodTower/Teldon/Cue_0089.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag turns and in an undertone says:{/n} "We could put him down, {mf|master|mistress}. What if our enemies come here and he tells them all about us? His mind is gone."
- `47f7886f6f1508b4f9a009ec5db43a15` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c1/KenabresBurning/MongrelsOutside/Cue_0036.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag sneers at the mongrels.{/n} "Pathetic. You're not ready to face real battle. We'll see what's left of you afterward."
- `6e353e322b32fbb4b91efeb4a803495c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/AreeluLaboratory/TargonaPrison/Cue_0048.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag's eyes sweep over the elf with contempt.{/n} "You look half dead already. But you've managed to survive this long, so you can't be as pathetic as you seem. Maybe you will be of some use... But if saving you proves more trouble than it's worth, I'll put you down myself."
- `9cb486cd95bdfb84ca1232c9c592e6f2` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Wintersun/Morveg_Dying/Cue_0028.jbp`
  > {n}You have never seen a look of such rage and hatred on Sosiel's face before. He looks like he would love nothing more than to attack Wenduag. However, the cleric reins in his anger. After taking a deep breath, Sosiel murmurs:{/n} "I will forgive you later and forget these words, for you are a self-absorbed, depraved woman who revels in the mire she has in place of a conscience. But for now, shut your mouth and don'…
- `1eb9e190e2566ee40b12e5be3c9c5493` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/Wintersun/Morveg_Dying/Cue_0029.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag's derisive smile cannot hide her astonishment.{/n} "It seems the priests did not succeed in the full castration of your spirit. Fine, say your prayers — but remember, if you dare threaten me a second time, we will fight to the death." {n}The hunter pointedly turns away from the cleric, who ignores her words and is now whispering a prayer with total focus.{/n}
- `13923af616f1b854989970c7e9313181` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/BattleBliss/ArenaHealer_dialogue/Cue_0062.jbp`
  > {n}The demon looks at Wenduag with interest.{/n} "Who created you? How many animals were killed to make you? Your mutations are extremely attractive, and your body radiates strength." {n}He reaches out a hand toward Wenduag.{/n}
- `c8ab1229ca8d9c64aa841aaea33caab1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Portal_ToColyphyr/Cue_0020.jbp`
  > {n}You realize that Wenduag disappeared last night and never returned. You must decide between staying in Alushinyrra and waiting for the huntress or abandoning her to face her fate alone.{/n}
- `eccb6a26372cfcf478d48e78c5556278` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/TavernBadLuck/InnKeeper_mainDialogue/Cue_0157.jbp`
  > "Why remember the dead? We should drink to those who survived!" {n}With a frown, Wenduag takes a sip from her tankard.{/n}
- `b9c117ec0e25618468ee3b9f0bca1609` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Drezen_Under_Siedge/Goddesses_Summit/Cue_0378.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag scoffs sardonically.{/n} "You know what I am about to say, {mf|master|mistress}. This power is ours, no matter where it came from and how we gained it. Only a complete idiot would reject such might. And if you consider the number of enemies you've made, then it would make you an idiot with a death wish."
- `3241cae604aa1694c8b366dae708c334` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Lich/TheDarkestRitual/Cue_0072.jbp`
  > {n}Wenduag's paralyzed body is still and unmoving as you strike the final blow. The mongrel's eyes watch you in horror until death clouds her gaze... forever.{/n}
- `026dcc939631d4d42a1f34b911a84828` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c6/ThresholdExterior/CompanionFarewells/WenduagFarewell/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > {n}Whistling a jaunty tune, Wenduag stares dead ahead, her eyes cold and clear.{/n} "So, this is where it all ends. When I first crossed paths with you in Neathholm, I never thought I'd end up in the very heart of the Worldwound. You certainly know how to enjoy yourself!"

## yaniel

Authored return/rescue predicates: `yaniel.trickster.returned`.

- `fc7637192a51c7a469c3172f58a29852` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/NPC_Common/StoryTeller_MainDialogue/Cue_0163.jbp`
  > …ll out, 'Hey, Deskari's spawn! Who wants the best trophy of the night? You know my name — Yaniel. You know how many of you I have killed with this very hand! You want to curry favor with Darrazand, Minagho or the Echo of your lord? Bring them my head... if you can!'"…

- `f3e957eb2d21f6644a30eee1b9fab454` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0008.jbp`
  > "Yaniel disappeared, but by some miracle Radiance was recovered from the battle. No one else has ever wielded it — it is Yaniel's blade and always will be. That's why it was put in the Tower of Estrod with the other crusade relics. But now it turns out that somebody stole it..."
- `fb9461af4e4bd0b4b81ebb102f001039` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0010.jbp`
  > {n}Seelah frowns.{/n} "Now... nothing, I guess. But this sword was legendary in its day! People say that when Yaniel held it, the blade would glow, striking demons left and right. Soldiers would see Radiance's light from afar and take heart, rushing into the fray and winning. But I don't know what's wrong with it now, or how to restore its power. All I can sense is that they made a mockery of it."
- `281abde723597134d818bcecbb00d5c1` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0023.jbp`
  > "She died as she lived — proudly. She was covering the escape of the refugees fleeing {g|Drezen}Drezen{/g} — that's where she perished. She wasn't even forty, very young for a half-elf. But I guess I don't have to explain that to you! You half-elves live twice as long as we do. If you think about it, the crusades aren't even a hundred years old. That's how little time separates us from the heroes of legend. Yaniel co…
- `fe013caf1bad3e545a9dd8de280a68a8` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c0/CavesUnderKenabres/MongrelLabyrinth/RadianceFound/Cue_0024.jbp`
  > "She died the same way she lived — proudly. She was covering the escape of the refugees fleeing {g|Drezen}Drezen{/g} — that's where she perished. She wasn't even forty, very young for a half-elf. Yaniel could have still been alive today... after all, the crusades are less than a hundred years old. That's how little time separates us from the heroes of legend. But I guess I don't have to explain that to you — it mustn…
- `542322bc430e0644ebf57b84fc3f9995` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/FakeYaniel_First/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > "Yaniel? Oh, I know that name! They used to have marches in the {g|Kenabres}River City{/g} in her honor! But that was a very long time ago... It must be so strange to feel alive when you've been dead to the world for so long."
- `7418d421e3af812439ea312991c37147` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/FakeYaniel_ToAreelu/Cue_0001.jbp`
  > "Forgive me this little masquerade." {n}Yaniel says in a different voice, one that sounds vaguely familiar. Suddenly the illusion vanishes.{/n}
- `b618fff15d921894e84b9b2fe9efaa39` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/FakeYaniel_ToAreelu/Cue_0006.jbp`
  > "The 'Yaniel' you freed from the dungeon was merely a mask. Sometimes I like to wear the guise of my fallen enemies. To study them from the inside. The real Yaniel has been gone for a long time, but unfortunately for her — not permanently. Her lot is far more frightening than the one I showed you. Perhaps you will learn the truth one day. But for now it isn't necessary."
- `21295a72112974f41bbd153a1ad17b1a` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c2_vs/DrezenSiege/Smith/Cue_0038.jbp`
  > "What?" {n}The dwarf seems stunned.{/n} "We thought {g|Yaniel}Yaniel{/g} was dead! My {g|Stonton}brother{/g} mourned her death as much as the fall of Drezen! She couldn't be here — my brother would have known, he would have done something!"
- `bcfd9c001c8a33a41906a9a95ab81e1a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0024.jbp`

- `c798d91c3807c484185fa95a4472d82e` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0034.jbp`
  > Minagho disappears, her malevolent, slightly hysterical laughter echoing after her. But the Commander is no longer concerned with her — {mf|he|she} is busy helping the victims.
- `8d20b87113a10094b87b2ea08d579447` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/Hostages_BE/Cue_0035.jbp`
  > "Do not abandon me, Inheritor, and give me the strength to aid my brothers and sisters in their hour of need," the old woman whispers softly. Her hands once more begin to glow with healing light, and she rushes to help the Commander — to heal the other husks. The Commander now sees her face — it is the face of Yaniel, the hero of the first crusades.
- `ce6287de16950a54cb2f44de26ca844c` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/TrueYaniel/Cue_0002.jbp`
  > "Everyone is dead... " {n}The blue-eyed old woman looks at the bodies of the other husks. A shadow of rage settles on her weary face.{/n} "But I survived and broke away from this endless imprisonment. Thank you, whoever you are. I am Yaniel, a paladin of Iomedae the lightbringer. I will not forget what you did for me."
- `9f7b77ac61de50e4ba607cb8dd5bf737` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/TrueYaniel/Cue_0017.jbp`
  > {n}Radiance flares to life with a soft golden glow that can only be described as... joyous. For a time, Yaniel stands with her eyes half-closed, then opens them and places the sword back in your hand.{/n} "I've been imprisoned for so long. I'm terrified to think what kind of world awaits me up there on the surface. My friends are probably dead, and the Drezen I knew and defended long gone."
- `536ceec8863161f489ef28ddd9c51845` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c3/MidnightFane/TrueYaniel/Cue_0027.jbp`
  > {n}Yaniel clenches her fingers around the hilt, and then returns the sword to you with a sigh.{/n} "I doubt I'll be able to raise this sword again. And should I even want to? My time has passed. The banners I fought under are gone, the blossoming trees I once sat under are gone as well. And my friends — are they alive? Who knows..."
- `bbe64b2a0975d8f4fa2d11011ff2fedc` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0007.jbp`

Everyone was excited about the Commander, but no one cared much about a strange old woman... even if she did share the name of a legendary hero." {n}Yaniel shrugs slightly, as if she's still somewhat puzzled by her status as a legendary hero.{/n}…

- `9d84c1bf8c391744f97d450779773ec8` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > "The Wardstones didn't exist back in my time. The first one was put up in Kenabres after the fall of Drezen... and my imprisonment. So I didn't find out about the Wardstones until after I left the Midnight Fane. I saw one for the first time when I arrived in Drezen with the Queen and her people."
- `751455e8ed099f24c95684e21a22570a` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0015.jbp`
  > "I was born in Mendev, before the Wound was opened. I remember the sadness and confusion caused by Aroden's death. In my town, the priest wouldn't close down the temple, and he refused to turn it over to Iomedae. Delegations were sent to the priest to explain what had happened. They tried to convince him of Aroden's death, but he never believed them. He continued to preside over what was probably Aroden's last surviv…
- `1127bf2974f916143aea4b2d85ebde24` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0019.jbp`
  > "I was in the Worldwound for two years. Everyone thought I was dead. I strayed far, even farther than the cursed city of Iz. I managed to free a lot of prisoners who were being held captive by the demons and cultists... but then I was faced with a new challenge. I had to get them home. And that's when I started to... finally understand my commanding officers. When I was by myself, I could be reckless! I could throw m…
- `9f3e984f55dea164c8ca7978a799c8e5` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0021.jbp`
  > …s that this monster Minagho escaped from the Midnight Fane alive. She ruined Staunton. He made the same stupid mistake I did — he left to be a hero when he should have been thoughtful and cautious. There are many demons in the Abyss, but I still hope to find that eyeless beast, and give her the justice she deserves."…
- `4413cba0ac497bb4798d862491f4354d` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0039.jbp`
  > "I was captured on the day that Drezen fell, but I've changed captors twice. First, the traitor Areelu Vorlesh kept me in her laboratory, where she healed me... after a fashion. Then she gave me to Minagho. It was a subtle mockery, for Minagho was to blame for the death of my city, and for the fall of my friend Staunton."
- `4fb64c9dcf254974f8679bd3bd0214b3` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0040.jbp`
  > "Minagho set up her lair in the Midnight Fane, and that is where she kept me. She told me that she'd make me her 'shell.' That's how lilitus torture mortals, and take their appearances as their own. Minagho had an entire collection. I even saw the elf woman she used to seduce Staunton. Poor girl. Minagho killed her in front of me. 'I'm bored of this look' — that's what she said."
- `2614ac9dc02ed864fbc900be9de52c54` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0041.jbp`
  > "She poisoned me with her dark magic, and left me to languish in the Midnight Fane. She stole my appearance, and wore my face as a disguise. I can only guess what atrocities she committed in my name. People knew who I was back then... How many of them did she deceive? Did they think that 'Yaniel' had 'miraculously' returned?" {n}The warrior's eyes are as cold as steel.{/n}
- `2f68e6b054a99ff4e80d1954265db0a9` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c4/Mythic_Angel/Yaniel/Cue_0043.jbp`
  > "But do you know who came to greet me? Do you know who welcomed me home? The two people I argued with before my departure — my commanding officer and the priest! We nearly fell into each other's arms, and there were so many apologies on all sides! That priest's name was Anthoclitus, and he later became one of the patriarchs of the church of Iomedae. And the name of my commanding officer was Staunton Vhane..."
- `f89fdfdfcef564c489f5a2f46190a825` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0012.jbp`
  > Eliandra, the high priestess of Pulura, steps forward. "I am sorry. But I must protect Pulura's stargazers... and what is left of Sarkoris. This is what my goddess has called me to do. When the power of Heaven flowed through all of us, we had a common purpose — we could have changed the course of this war. But now, we are simply allies... and we must all return to our former lives."
- `c1662db6b8446ee4285b7bac773a5d3d` — BlueprintCue; name; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0014.jbp`
  > Yaniel looks tired and frail. It seems as though her age has finally caught up with her. "I don't have much choice. Your power was the only thing keeping me going... Now that it is gone, I feel the weight of my years like a proverbial ton of bricks. My fighting days are over. It's time for old Yaniel to retreat to the rear."
- `b17c8b226d147af48b7c06a1cbb52573` — BlueprintCue; sibling context; `World/Dialogs/c5/Mythic_Angel/FarewellToMythicGMBE/Cue_0032.jbp`
  > It is Targona who answers. "We felt the power of Heaven leave you! We felt... your death. The goddess told us everything — she told us what you had become. We are currently on a mission, but we had to see for ourselves... It is one thing to hear the truth, and another to see it with your own eyes. We trusted you, {name}! We believed in you. But now you are our enemy. If our paths cross again, we will have no choice —…
